```html
        <div class="hrow"><span class="d" data-i18n="h_day_r1d">Dilluns – divendres</span><span class="t">08:00 – 17:00</span></div>
        <div class="hrow"><span class="d" data-i18n="h_day_r2d">Dissabte – diumenge</span><span class="t">09:00 – 18:00</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="hours-pane" data-hours="night" style="opacity:0;pointer-events:none">
        <div class="htitle" data-i18n="h_night_t">Vespres de vermut</div>
        <div class="hrow"><span class="d" data-i18n="h_night_r1d">Dilluns – dijous</span><span class="t">18:00 – 00:00</span></div>
        <div class="hrow"><span class="d" data-i18n="h_night_r2d">Divendres – dissabte</span><span class="t">18:00 – 02:00</span></div>
        <div class="hrow"><span class="d" data-i18n="h_night_r3d">Diumenge</span><span class="t">18:00 – 23:00</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="reserve" id="reserve">
  <div class="wrap reserve-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow dark" data-i18n="res_eyebrow">Reserves</span>
      <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="res_title">Guarda't <em>taula</em></h2>
      <p class="lead" data-i18n="res_lead">Per a grups de més de 6, truca'ns. Per a la resta, deixa'ns les dades i et confirmem en breu.</p>
    </div>
    <form id="resForm" novalidate>
      <div class="frow">
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_name">Nom</label><input type="text" required></div>
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_phone">Telèfon</label><input type="tel" required></div>
      </div>
      <div class="frow">
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_date">Data</label><input type="date" required></div>
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_time">Hora</label><input type="time" required></div>
      </div>
      <div class="frow">
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_guests">Comensals</label>
          <select required>
            <option value="">—</option><option>1</option><option>2</option><option>3</option><option>4</option><option>5</option><option>6</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field"><label data-i18n="f_when">Moment</label>
          <select required>
            <option value="" data-i18n="f_when_ph">Tria</option>
            <option data-i18n="f_when_day">Cafè / dia</option>
            <option data-i18n="f_when_night">Vermut / nit</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="field full"><label data-i18n="f_notes">Notes</label><textarea></textarea></div>
      <button type="submit" class="btn ink"><span data-i18n="f_submit">Demanar reserva</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
    </form>
  </div>
</section>

<div class="res-ok" id="resOk">
  <div class="card">
    <div class="check">✓</div>
    <h3 data-i18n="ok_title">Rebut!</h3>
    <p data-i18n="ok_msg">Et confirmarem per telèfon en breu. Ens veiem a Can Biel.</p>
    <button class="btn ink" id="okClose"><span data-i18n="ok_close">Tancar</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
  </div>
</div>

<section class="testi">
  <div class="wrap">
    <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="testi_eye">Opinions</span>
    <blockquote data-i18n="testi_q">«El millor vermut del barri, i el cafè que et desperta l'ànima.»</blockquote>
    <cite data-i18n="testi_c">— Time Out Barcelona</cite>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="cta">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="cta-box">
      <h2 data-i18n="cta_title">Vine a <em>Can Biel</em></h2>
      <p data-i18n="cta_p">Taula per a dos o barra per a un: sempre hi ha lloc per a un bon vermut i una bona conversa.</p>
      <a href="#reserve" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="cta_btn">Reservar ara</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

    cf4_n:"Cold Brew",cf4_d:"Infusionat en fred 18 hores. Servit amb gel i pell de taronja.",
    cf5_n:"Cortado",cf5_d:"El tall clàssic de barra, amb un toc de llet calenta.",
    cf6_n:"Cappuccino",cf6_d:"Escuma ferma i cacau ratllat al moment.",
    cf7_n:"V60 de filtre",cf7_d:"Origen rotatiu, preparat a taula davant teu.",
    cf8_n:"Cafè amb gel",cf8_d:"Espresso doble servit a part, per abocar-lo al teu ritme.",
    vm1_n:"Vermut de casa (grifo)",vm1_d:"Recepta pròpia amb herbes del territori. Servit amb oliva i pell de taronja.",
    vm2_n:"Vermut negre reserva",vm2_d:"Criança en bota, notes de regalèssia i fruita confitada.",
    vm3_n:"Vermut blanc amb oliva",vm3_d:"Floral i sec, amb un punt cítric i herbes fines.",
    vm4_n:"Spritz de vermut",vm4_d:"Vermut, soda i un toc d'aperitiu amarg. Lleuger i fresc.",
    vm5_n:"Negroni de la casa",vm5_d:"Vermut reserva, ginebra del barri i bitter. Remogut, no agitat.",
    vm6_n:"Vermut amb sifó",vm6_d:"El servei de sempre, amb el sifó de tota la vida.",
    vm7_n:"Copa de cava",vm7_d:"Cava brut nature ben fred, per obrir o per tancar.",
    vm8_n:"Cervesa artesana",vm8_d:"Rotació de cellers petits catalans, servida a la copa justa.",
    tp1_n:"Patates braves de la casa",tp1_d:"Salsa brava fumada i allioli suau. Les de sempre, fetes al moment.",
    tp2_n:"Croqueta de pernil ibèric",tp2_d:"Beixamel lenta de 24 h, arrebossada fina i cruixent.",
    tp3_n:"Anxoves de l'Escala",tp3_d:"Filets curats en sal, amb pa amb tomàquet i oli verge.",
    tp4_n:"Pa amb tomàquet",tp4_d:"Pa de coca de vidre, tomàquet de penjar i oli arbequina.",
    tp5_n:"Gamba roja a la planxa",tp5_d:"Toc de sal Maldon i llimona. Producte del dia.",
    tp6_n:"Pintxo de botifarra",tp6_d:"Botifarra artesana, mel i un punt de mostassa.",
    tp7_n:"Escalivada amb ventresca",tp7_d:"Pebrot i albergínia al caliu, ventresca de tonyina.",

    pl2_n:"Bacallà a la llauna",pl2_d:"Al forn amb all, pebrot i oli, com a les fondes d'abans.",
    pl3_n:"Carn d'olla",pl3_d:"Guisat lent de la casa, llegums i verdura de temporada.",
    pl4_n:"Taula de formatges",pl4_d:"Selecció de llet crua amb mel, nous i pa de nous.",

    pl6_n:"Amanida de tomàquet",pl6_d:"Tomàquet de temporada, ceba tendra i oli arbequina.",
    pl7_n:"Capipota",pl7_d:"Guisat tradicional de taverna, fet a foc molt lent.",
    pl8_n:"Crema catalana",pl8_d:"Cremosa, amb el sucre cremat al moment davant teu.",

    hero_sub:"Since 1908 we open in the morning with our own roasted coffee and close at night with vermouth on tap and market tapas.",

    m1:"House-roasted coffee",m2:"Vermouth on tap",m3:"Market tapas",m4:"Sunday brunch",m5:"Ice-cold cava",
    about_eyebrow:"The house",about_l1:"Over a century",about_l2:"serving the",about_l3:"neighbourhood.",
    about_p:"Can Biel began as a wine cellar and today it is the corner where the neighbourhood meets: specialty coffee in the morning, house vermouth in the evening and tapas made with produce from the market next door.",
    about_cta:"Explore the menu",badge:"since<br>ever",
    menu_eyebrow:"The menu",menu_title:'What will you <em>have</em>?',menu_note:"Seasonal menu · produce from the market next door",

    cf1_n:"House espresso",cf1_d:"Roasted every week in Poble-sec. Dense body, cocoa finish.",
    cf2_n:"Flat White",cf2_d:"Double ristretto with micro-foamed milk. Balance and silk.",
    cf3_n:"Sheep's milk latte",cf3_d:"Sweet, creamy Pyrenean sheep's milk with a long espresso.",
    cf4_n:"Cold Brew",cf4_d:"Cold-steeped for 18 hours. Served with ice and orange peel.",
    cf5_n:"Cortado",cf5_d:"The classic bar cut, with a touch of warm milk.",
    cf6_n:"Cappuccino",cf6_d:"Firm foam and cocoa grated to order.",
    cf7_n:"V60 filter",cf7_d:"Rotating single origin, brewed at your table.",
    cf8_n:"Iced coffee",cf8_d:"Double espresso served on the side, to pour at your pace.",
    vm1_n:"House vermouth (tap)",vm1_d:"Our own recipe with local herbs. Served with olive and orange peel.",
    vm2_n:"Reserve red vermouth",vm2_d:"Barrel-aged, notes of liquorice and candied fruit.",
    vm3_n:"White vermouth with olive",vm3_d:"Floral and dry, with a citrus edge and fine herbs.",
    vm4_n:"Vermouth spritz",vm4_d:"Vermouth, soda and a touch of bitter aperitif. Light and fresh.",
    vm5_n:"House Negroni",vm5_d:"Reserve vermouth, neighbourhood gin and bitter. Stirred, not shaken.",
    vm6_n:"Vermouth with siphon",vm6_d:"The usual serve, with the siphon of a lifetime.",
    vm7_n:"Glass of cava",vm7_d:"Ice-cold brut nature cava, to start or to finish.",
    vm8_n:"Craft beer",vm8_d:"Rotating small Catalan breweries, poured in the right glass.",
    tp1_n:"House patatas bravas",tp1_d:"Smoked bravas sauce and smooth aioli. The usual, made to order.",
    tp2_n:"Iberian ham croquette",tp2_d:"24-hour slow béchamel, thin and crispy coating.",
    tp3_n:"L'Escala anchovies",tp3_d:"Salt-cured fillets with bread, tomato and virgin oil.",
    tp4_n:"Bread with tomato",tp4_d:"Glass coca bread, hanging tomato and arbequina oil.",
    tp5_n:"Grilled red prawn",tp5_d:"A touch of Maldon salt and lemon. Catch of the day.",
    tp6_n:"Butifarra pintxo",tp6_d:"Artisan butifarra, honey and a hint of mustard.",
    tp7_n:"Escalivada with belly",tp7_d:"Ember-roasted pepper and aubergine, tuna belly.",
    tp8_n:"Cracked olives",tp8_d:"Arbequinas with herbs, the vermouth's companion.",
    pl1_n:"Artichoke rice",pl1_d:"Creamy rice with El Prat artichoke and long sofrito. Minimum 2 people.",
    pl2_n:"Oven cod (llauna)",pl2_d:"Baked with garlic, pepper and oil, like the old inns.",
    pl3_n:"House stew",pl3_d:"Slow house stew, pulses and seasonal vegetables.",
    pl4_n:"Cheese board",pl4_d:"Raw-milk selection with honey, walnuts and walnut bread.",
    pl5_n:"Sunday cannelloni",pl5_d:"Minced roast, béchamel and gratin. Grandma Biel's recipe.",
    pl6_n:"Tomato salad",pl6_d:"Seasonal tomato, spring onion and arbequina oil.",
    pl7_n:"Tripe stew",pl7_d:"Traditional tavern stew, cooked over a very low flame.",
    pl8_n:"Crema catalana",pl8_d:"Creamy, with the sugar burnt to order in front of you.",
    gal_eyebrow:"The space",gal_title:'The room, <em>at the table</em>',
    g1_t:"The bar",g1_d:"Old wood, brass and just enough noise to talk.",
    g2_t:"The coffee",g2_d:"Vintage machine and warm cups from eight o'clock.",
    g3_t:"The kitchen",g3_d:"Market produce cooked every morning, no shortcuts.",
    g4_t:"The vermouth",g4_d:"Collector bottles and the tap always ready.",
    g5_t:"The terrace",g5_d:"Four tables on the street, afternoon sun and street life.",
    hours_eyebrow:"Hours",hours_title:'Two moments, <em>one house</em>',hours_p:"We open in the morning for coffee and the paper, and at night for vermouth and conversation. Pick your moment.",
    htab_day:"Coffee · day",htab_night:"Vermouth · night",

    s1:"Founded",s2:"Coffees a day",s3:"Vermouth refs",s4:"Market tapas",

};

function setLang(lang){
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{
    const k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined) el.innerHTML = I18N[lang][k];
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-ph]').forEach(el=>{
    const k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n-ph');
    if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined) el.placeholder = I18N[lang][k];
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ LAZY IMG + FALLBACK ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(img=>{
  const src = img.getAttribute('data-src');
  img.onerror = ()=> img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');
  img.src = src;
});

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur = document.querySelector('.cursor'), ring = document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px'});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.18;ry+=(my-ry)*.18;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop)})();
document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.card,.panel,.tab,.htab,.menu-item').forEach(el=>{
  el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>ring.classList.add('grow'));
  el.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>ring.classList.remove('grow'));
});

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis = new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const id=a.getAttribute('href'); if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}
}));

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'), pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
const tl=gsap.timeline();
tl.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',
  onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%'}
})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.2')
.add(()=>{ heroIn(); });

/* ============ HERO IN ============ */
function heroIn(){
  if(reduce){
    gsap.set('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0});
    gsap.set('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-foot > *, .scroll-ind',{y:0,opacity:1});
    gsap.set('.hero-bg img',{scale:1});
    return;
  }
  gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.08});
  gsap.from('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-foot > *, .scroll-ind',{y:30,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.3});
  gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{scale:1,duration:1.6,ease:'power2.out'},0);
}
gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{yPercent:18,ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.hero',start:'top top',end:'bottom top',scrub:true}});

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ ABOUT ============ */
gsap.to('#aboutTitle .ln i',{y:0,duration:1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.12,scrollTrigger:{trigger:'#aboutTitle',start:'top 80%'}});
gsap.to('.about .reveal-mask',{scaleX:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.inOut',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 75%'}});
gsap.from('.about .badge',{scale:0,rotation:-30,duration:.8,ease:'back.out(2)',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 60%'}});

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');
mtrack.innerHTML += mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:24,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ MENU TABS ============ */
const tabs=[...document.querySelectorAll('.tab')];
const panes=[...document.querySelectorAll('.menu-pane')];
function showTab(id){
  tabs.forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle('active',t.dataset.tab===id));
  panes.forEach(p=>{
    if(p.dataset.tab===id){
      gsap.set(p,{display:'block'});
      if(reduce){gsap.set(p.children,{y:0,opacity:1});}
      else{gsap.fromTo(p.children,{y:24,opacity:0},{y:0,opacity:1,duration:.55,stagger:.05,ease:'power3.out',overwrite:true});}
    }else{gsap.set(p,{display:'none'});}
  });
}
tabs.forEach(t=>t.addEventListener('click',()=>showTab(t.dataset.tab)));
showTab('cafe');

/* ============ HORIZONTAL SCROLL ============ */
const hscroll=document.getElementById('hscroll');
gsap.to(hscroll,{x:()=>-(hscroll.scrollWidth-window.innerWidth+window.innerWidth*0.06),ease:'none',
  scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.gallery',start:'top top',end:()=>'+='+(hscroll.scrollWidth-window.innerWidth),pin:true,scrub:1,invalidateOnRefresh:true}});

/* ============ HOURS TOGGLE ============ */
const htabs=[...document.querySelectorAll('.htab')];
const hpanes=[...document.querySelectorAll('.hours-pane')];
function showHours(id){
  htabs.forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle('active',t.dataset.hours===id));
  hpanes.forEach(p=>{
    const on = p.dataset.hours===id;
    if(reduce){gsap.set(p,{opacity:on?1:0,pointerEvents:on?'auto':'none'});return;}
    gsap.to(p,{opacity:on?1:0,pointerEvents:on?'auto':'none',duration:.5,ease:'power2.out',
      onStart:()=>{if(on)gsap.fromTo(p.children,{y:18,opacity:0},{y:0,opacity:1,duration:.5,stagger:.06,ease:'power3.out'});}});
  });
}
htabs.forEach(t=>t.addEventListener('click',()=>showHours(t.dataset.hours)));

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.stat .num').forEach(num=>{
  const target=+num.dataset.count;
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:num,start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){num.textContent=target;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:2,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){num.textContent=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v)}});
  }});
});

/* ============ GENERIC FADE-UP ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.tabs,.panel,.hours-wrap > *,.reserve-grid > *,.testi blockquote,.cta-box').forEach(el=>{
  gsap.from(el,{y:50,opacity:0,duration:.9,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}});
});

/* ============ RESERVE FORM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('resForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});return;}
  gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}
  gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});
});

/* ============ MAGNETIC BUTTONS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
    if(reduce)return;
    const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();
    gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.3,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.4,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});
  });
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});

document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open')});

setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

<answer>

## Qué incluye esta web (negocio #2 — bar / cafetería / restaurante)

**Concepto:** *CAN BIEL · 1908*, vermutería + café de especialidad + tapas. Estética **invertida** respecto a la #1 (sitio mayormente oscuro, taberna nocturna, con secciones claras alternas), paleta **carbón + ámbar de vermut + rojo cereza** y tipografía **DM Serif Display + Archivo** para diferenciarse de verdad.

**Stack y efectos premium (mismo sello que la #1)**
- **GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis** (smooth scroll con inercia).
- **Preloader** 0→100 con barra; **reveal por palabras** en hero y por líneas en "La casa"; **parallax** y **máscara de revelado** (`scaleX`).
- **Galería horizontal con pin**, **contadores** animados, **marquee** infinito, **cursor magnético** y **botones magnéticos** (`elastic.out`).

**Tres secciones de front-end nuevas y propias del vertical**
1. **Carta con pestañas interactivas** (Cafés / Vermuts / Tapas / Platos): 4 tabs × 8 ítems con *layout* editorial (nombre · puntos líderes · precio · descripción) y transición GSAP al cambiar de pestaña.
2. **Horarios día/noche con toggle** y *crossfade* animado entre el bloque "Café · día" y "Vermut · noche".
3. **Formulario de reserva** con validación nativa y **overlay de confirmación animado** (check con `back.out`, tarjeta con rebote).

**Trilingüe real (CA / ES / EN)**
- Selector funcional; el objeto `I18N` cubre **todos** los textos: ítems de carta, etiquetas del formulario, días de la semana, mensajes de confirmación y placeholders. Arranca en catalán y cambia también `html lang`.

**Imágenes libres y robustez**
- Imágenes de **Unsplash** vía `data-src` (carga diferida) con `onerror → .img-fallback` (gradiente de respaldo), de modo que el diseño **nunca se rompe**.

**Accesibilidad y responsive**
- `clamp()` + *breakpoints* a 900px / 560px; respeta `prefers-reduced-motion` (en ese caso usa *sets* en lugar de *tweens* en hero, tabs, toggle, stats y overlay).

## Cómo usarla
1. Copia el bloque en `index.html` y ábrelo (necesita internet para fuentes, GSAP, Lenis e imágenes).
2. Edita textos, precios, horarios, teléfono y dirección en el HTML **y** en el objeto `I18N` (las 3 lenguas a la vez).
3. Para producción: sustituye las URLs de Unsplash por tus fotos y conecta el formulario a un backend/servicio (Formspree, email, etc.).

## Siguiente paso
Cuando me digas **`. 3`**, construiré la web completa premium (mismo nivel GSAP/animaciones/trilingüe) para el tipo de cliente **#3 — Constructores, reformistas y albañiles**.

</answer>

otra modalidad 3

## [ASSISTANT] 2026-07-24 10:17 · Qwen3.8-Max-Preview

<thinking>
**1. Desglose formal de la premisa:** El usuario pide "otra modalidad 3", es decir, la web del cliente **#3 — constructores, reformistas y albañiles**, pero aportando una *modalidad* de front-end distinta a las dos anteriores (#1 claro mediterráneo con galería horizontal; #2 taberna oscura con carta por pestañas). Debo mantener el sello premium (GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis, preloader con contador, reveal por palabras, parallax, cursor magnético, marquee, botones magnéticos), el trilingüe CA/ES/EN y las imágenes libres con *fallback*.

**2. Restricciones de variedad y técnica:** No repetir paleta ni tipografía. Descarto claro-serif (#1) y oscuro-serif (#2). Para el vertical de obra elijo **brutalismo industrial**: base hormigón + bloques negros + acento naranja-obra, tipografía condensada **Anton** (titulares) + **Space Grotesk** (cuerpo), números en *outline* con `text-stroke`, sin radios. Y como "modalidad nueva" introduzco **dos interacciones propias del sector que no aparecían antes**: (a) **slider ANTES/DESPUÉS** accesible (controlado por un `<input range>` con `pointer-events:none` para teclado + *pointer handlers* en el contenedor para ratón/touch, moviendo `clip-path`), y (b) **calculadora de presupuesto** interactiva (m² × tipo × calidad → estimación animada con formato de moneda por idioma). Añado además **portfolio con filtro** animado por GSAP.

**3. Evaluación y selección óptima:**
- ¿Re-skin de #1/#2? Descartado, no aporta "otra modalidad".
- ¿Brutalismo + slider comparación + calculadora + filtro? **Elegido**: máxima diferenciación visual y dos módulos de front-end de alto valor real para una empresa de reformas.
- Robustez: mismo patrón `data-src` + `onerror → .img-fallback`; `prefers-reduced-motion` con *sets*; `clamp()` y *breakpoints*; slider operable por teclado vía `:focus-within`.
- i18n: objeto `I18N` con **todas** las claves en 3 lenguas (pasos del proceso, opciones de selects de la calculadora con `value` fijos independientes del idioma, etiquetas antes/después, categorías de filtro, mensaje de confirmación). La calculadora reformatea la moneda al cambiar de idioma (`Intl.NumberFormat` por `ca-ES / es-ES / en-IE`).
- Marca ficticia: **OBRA MESTRA** (logo fijo, no se traduce; el *eyebrow* sí).

</thinking>

Aquí tienes la web premium del cliente **#3 — constructores / reformistas / albañiles** (marca *OBRA MESTRA*), en una **modalidad nueva: brutalismo industrial**. Mismo sello GSAP/Lenis/trilingüe que las anteriores, pero con estética distinta y **dos módulos de front-end inéditos** en la serie: un **slider ANTES/DESPUÉS** arrastrable y accesible por teclado, y una **calculadora de presupuesto** en vivo con formato de moneda por idioma; además de un **portfolio con filtro** animado. Un solo archivo `index.html`. Imágenes de **Unsplash (libres)** con *fallback* de gradiente.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ca">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>OBRA MESTRA — Reformes i obra nova / Reformas y obra nueva / Renovations & new build</title>
<meta name="description" content="OBRA MESTRA — Reformes integrals, cuines, banys i obra nova. Pressupost tancat i terminis clars." />

<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Anton&family=Space+Grotesk:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/ScrollTrigger.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.1.13/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>

<style>
  :root{
    --concrete:#E9E6DF;
    --concrete2:#DAD6CC;
    --ink:#141312;
    --ink2:#222019;
    --accent:#E8531E;
    --accent2:#FF6A33;
    --muted-l:#6A655B;
    --muted-d:rgba(233,230,223,.6);
    --line:rgba(20,19,18,.16);
    --line-d:rgba(233,230,223,.16);
    --disp:'Anton',Impact,sans-serif;
    --sans:'Space Grotesk',system-ui,sans-serif;
    --ease:cubic-bezier(.76,0,.24,1);
  }
  *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
  html,body{background:var(--concrete);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--sans);-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;overflow-x:hidden}
  body{cursor:none}
  a{color:inherit;text-decoration:none}
  img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}
  ::selection{background:var(--accent);color:var(--concrete)}
  .wrap{width:min(92%,1340px);margin-inline:auto}
  em{font-style:normal;color:var(--accent)}
  .eyebrow{font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent);font-weight:700;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem}
  .eyebrow::before{content:"";width:30px;height:2px;background:var(--accent)}
  .eyebrow.inv{color:var(--accent2)}.eyebrow.inv::before{background:var(--accent2)}
  .disp{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:-.01em;line-height:.86}
  .bignum{font-family:var(--disp);-webkit-text-stroke:2px var(--ink);color:transparent;line-height:.8}

  /* CURSOR */
  .cursor{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:14px;height:14px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent2);pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;mix-blend-mode:difference;transform:translate(-50%,-50%)}
  .cursor-ring{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:46px;height:46px;border:2px solid var(--ink);border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none;z-index:9998;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);transition:width .3s var(--ease),height .3s var(--ease),background .3s,border-color .3s}
  .cursor-ring.grow{width:84px;height:84px;background:rgba(232,83,30,.16);border-color:var(--accent)}
  @media(hover:none){body{cursor:auto}.cursor,.cursor-ring{display:none}}

  /* PRELOADER */
  #preloader{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);z-index:10000;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;padding:clamp(1.4rem,4vw,3rem)}
  #preloader .top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase;opacity:.7}
  #preloader .count{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:clamp(5rem,22vw,18rem);line-height:.8;color:var(--accent)}
  #preloader .bar{height:3px;background:rgba(233,230,223,.16);overflow:hidden}
  #preloader .bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:var(--accent)}

  /* NAV */
  header{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;z-index:900;transition:background .4s,backdrop-filter .4s}
  header.scrolled{background:rgba(233,230,223,.85);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);border-bottom:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .nav{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:1.2rem 0}
  .logo{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:1.5rem;letter-spacing:.02em;display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:.5rem;text-transform:uppercase}
  .logo span{font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.28em;color:var(--accent);font-family:var(--sans);font-weight:700}
  .menu{display:flex;gap:2rem;list-style:none;font-size:.8rem;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600}
  .menu a{position:relative;padding:.3rem 0}
  .menu a::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;bottom:-2px;width:0;height:2px;background:var(--accent);transition:width .4s var(--ease)}
  .menu a:hover::after{width:100%}
  .nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1.2rem}
  .lang{display:flex;gap:.2rem;border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:.2rem}
  .lang button{border:0;background:transparent;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.12em;font-weight:700;padding:.35rem .6rem;cursor:none;color:var(--muted-l);transition:.3s}
  .lang button.active{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete)}
  .burger{display:none;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;background:none;border:0;cursor:none}
  .burger i{width:26px;height:2px;background:var(--ink);transition:.3s}

  /* BUTTONS */
  .btn{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem;padding:1rem 1.8rem;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;overflow:hidden;cursor:none;border:2px solid var(--ink);transition:color .35s var(--ease)}
  .btn .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--ink);transform:translateY(101%);transition:transform .45s var(--ease);z-index:0}
  .btn span{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .btn:hover{color:var(--concrete)}
  .btn:hover .fill{transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid{background:var(--accent);color:var(--concrete);border-color:var(--accent)}
  .btn.solid .fill{background:var(--ink);transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid:hover{color:var(--concrete)}
  .btn.solid:hover .fill{transform:translateY(-101%)}
  .btn.inv{border-color:var(--concrete);color:var(--concrete)}
  .btn.inv .fill{background:var(--accent)}

  /* HERO */
  .hero{min-height:100vh;position:relative;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;padding-bottom:clamp(3rem,8vh,6rem);overflow:hidden;background:var(--ink)}
  .hero-bg{position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:0;opacity:.5}
  .hero-bg img{transform:scale(1.2)}
  .hero-bg::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(20,19,18,.4),rgba(20,19,18,.85))}
  .hero .wrap{position:relative;z-index:2;color:var(--concrete)}
  .hero h1{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(3rem,12vw,12rem);line-height:.84;letter-spacing:-.015em}
  .hero h1 .word{display:inline-block;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:top}
  .hero h1 .word i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .hero h1 em{color:var(--accent)}
  .hero-foot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:2rem;margin-top:2.4rem;flex-wrap:wrap;border-top:2px solid var(--line-d);padding-top:1.6rem}
  .hero-foot p{max-width:34ch;color:var(--muted-d);font-size:.95rem;line-height:1.6}
  .hero-cta{display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .scroll-ind{position:absolute;bottom:1.4rem;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:2;font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted-d);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:.6rem}
  .scroll-ind i{width:2px;height:38px;background:var(--muted-d);animation:drop 1.8s var(--ease) infinite}
  @keyframes drop{0%{transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:top}50%{transform:scaleY(1);transform-origin:top}51%{transform-origin:bottom}100%{transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:bottom}}

  /* MARQUEE */
  .marquee{background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink);padding:1.1rem 0;overflow:hidden;border-block:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .marquee .track{display:flex;gap:2.6rem;white-space:nowrap;width:max-content;font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(1.3rem,3.2vw,2.4rem)}
  .marquee .track span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:2.6rem}
  .marquee .dot{width:10px;height:10px;background:var(--ink);display:inline-block;transform:rotate(45deg)}

  /* ABOUT */
  .about{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .about-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:center}
  .about h2{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5.4rem);line-height:.9}
  .about h2 .ln{display:block;overflow:hidden}
  .about h2 .ln i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .about .media{position:relative;aspect-ratio:4/5;overflow:hidden;border:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .about .media .reveal-mask{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--accent);transform-origin:left;z-index:2}
  .about .badge{position:absolute;bottom:-2px;right:-2px;background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);padding:1.2rem 1.4rem;z-index:3;font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;line-height:1.5}
  .about .badge b{display:block;font-family:var(--disp);font-size:2.4rem;letter-spacing:0;color:var(--accent);text-transform:none;line-height:.9}

  /* COMPARE (antes/después) */
  .compare-sec{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .compare{position:relative;aspect-ratio:16/10;max-width:1000px;margin:clamp(2rem,5vh,3.5rem) auto 0;overflow:hidden;border:2px solid var(--concrete);user-select:none;touch-action:none}
  .compare img{position:absolute;inset:0}
  .compare .after{z-index:1}
  .compare .before{z-index:2;clip-path:inset(0 50% 0 0)}
  .compare .tag{position:absolute;top:1rem;z-index:4;background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;padding:.4rem .8rem}
  .compare .tag.b{left:1rem}
  .compare .tag.a{right:1rem;background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink)}
  .compare .handle{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;left:50%;width:2px;background:var(--concrete);z-index:4;transform:translateX(-50%)}
  .compare .handle::after{content:"⇆";position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:48px;height:48px;background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:1.2rem;font-weight:700}
  .compare input[type=range]{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;margin:0;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:6;cursor:ew-resize}
  .compare:focus-within .handle{outline:2px solid var(--accent2);outline-offset:2px}
  .compare-hint{text-align:center;color:var(--muted-d);font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:1.2rem}

  /* SERVICES (brutalist grid) */
  .services{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .sec-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:2rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2.5rem,6vh,4rem);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .sec-head h2{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:.88}
  .sec-head .note{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.8rem;letter-spacing:.08em;max-width:24ch;text-align:right;text-transform:uppercase}
  .srv-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);border-top:2px solid var(--ink);border-left:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .srv{position:relative;padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.6rem);border-right:2px solid var(--ink);border-bottom:2px solid var(--ink);min-height:230px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;transition:background .4s var(--ease),color .4s var(--ease);overflow:hidden}
  .srv .n{font-family:var(--disp);-webkit-text-stroke:2px var(--ink);color:transparent;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,5vw,4rem);line-height:.8;transition:.4s var(--ease)}
  .srv h3{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(1.3rem,2.4vw,2rem);line-height:.95;margin-top:auto}
  .srv p{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.85rem;line-height:1.55;margin-top:.7rem;transition:color .4s}
  .srv:hover{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete)}
  .srv:hover .n{-webkit-text-stroke:2px var(--accent);color:transparent}
  .srv:hover p{color:var(--muted-d)}

  /* PROCESS */
  .process{background:var(--concrete2);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0;border-block:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .proc-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:0;margin-top:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem)}
  .step{padding:1.4rem 1.2rem 1.4rem 0;border-top:2px solid var(--ink);position:relative}
  .step .sn{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:clamp(2rem,4vw,3.4rem);color:var(--accent);line-height:.8}
  .step h4{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:1.1rem;line-height:1;margin-top:1rem}
  .step p{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.82rem;line-height:1.5;margin-top:.6rem}

  /* CALCULATOR */
  .calc{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .calc-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);align-items:stretch}
  .calc-form{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:1.6rem}
  .field label{display:block;font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted-l);margin-bottom:.6rem}
  .field select,.field input[type=number]{width:100%;background:transparent;border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:.9rem 1rem;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.95rem;color:var(--ink);outline:none;cursor:none}
  .field select{appearance:none}
  .range-wrap{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem}
  .range-wrap input[type=range]{flex:1;accent-color:var(--accent);cursor:none;height:2px}
  .range-wrap output{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:1.6rem;min-width:4ch;text-align:right}
  .calc-out{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;border:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .calc-out .lbl{font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.24em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent2);font-weight:700}
  .calc-out .big{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:clamp(2.6rem,7vw,5rem);color:var(--concrete);line-height:.9;margin:1rem 0 .4rem}
  .calc-out .range{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:clamp(1.2rem,3vw,1.8rem);color:var(--accent)}
  .calc-out .legal{color:var(--muted-d);font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.06em;margin-top:1.4rem;line-height:1.5}
  .calc-out .btn{margin-top:1.8rem;align-self:flex-start}

  /* PORTFOLIO */
  .work{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .filters{display:flex;gap:.5rem;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem)}
  .filter{background:none;border:2px solid var(--line-d);color:var(--muted-d);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;padding:.7rem 1.2rem;cursor:none;transition:.3s}
  .filter.active{background:var(--accent);border-color:var(--accent);color:var(--ink)}
  .work-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:2px;background:var(--line-d)}
  .witem{position:relative;aspect-ratio:4/5;overflow:hidden;cursor:none;background:var(--ink)}
  .witem img{transition:transform 1.1s var(--ease),opacity .4s}
  .witem:hover img{transform:scale(1.07);opacity:.55}
  .witem .wi{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;padding:1.4rem;z-index:2}
  .witem .wi .c{font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent2);font-weight:700}
  .witem .wi h3{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:1.4rem;line-height:.95;margin-top:.4rem}

  /* STATS */
  .stats{padding:clamp(4rem,9vh,7rem) 0;border-bottom:2px solid var(--ink)}
  .stats-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:2rem}
  .stat{border-left:2px solid var(--ink);padding-left:1.4rem}
  .stat .num{font-family:var(--disp);font-size:clamp(3rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:.85;color:var(--ink)}
  .stat .num em{color:var(--accent)}
  .stat .lbl{font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:.8rem;color:var(--muted-l);font-weight:600}

  /* TESTI */
  .testi{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0;text-align:center}
  .testi blockquote{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(1.6rem,4.4vw,3.6rem);line-height:1.05;max-width:20ch;margin:2rem auto}
  .testi cite{font-style:normal;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted-l)}

  /* CTA + FORM */
  .contact{background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0}
  .contact-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:start}
  .contact h2{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:.88}
  .contact h2 em{color:var(--ink)}
  .contact .lead{font-size:1rem;line-height:1.6;margin-top:1.6rem;max-width:34ch;font-weight:500}
  .contact .info{margin-top:2rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.6rem;font-weight:600}
  form .frow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.2rem}
  .cfield{margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .cfield.full{grid-column:1/-1}
  .cfield label{display:block;font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:.5rem}
  .cfield input,.cfield select,.cfield textarea{width:100%;background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:2px solid var(--ink);padding:.6rem 0;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.95rem;color:var(--ink);outline:none;cursor:none}
  .cfield textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:70px}
  .contact .btn{border-color:var(--ink);background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete)}
  .contact .btn .fill{background:var(--concrete)}
  .contact .btn:hover{color:var(--ink)}

  .res-ok{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9500;background:rgba(20,19,18,.88);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);display:none;place-items:center;padding:1.5rem}
  .res-ok .card{background:var(--concrete);color:var(--ink);border:2px solid var(--ink);padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);text-align:center;max-width:440px}
  .res-ok .check{width:74px;height:74px;background:var(--accent);color:var(--concrete);display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0 auto 1.4rem;font-family:var(--disp);font-size:2rem}
  .res-ok h3{font-family:var(--disp);text-transform:uppercase;font-size:1.8rem}
  .res-ok p{color:var(--muted-l);margin:1rem 0 1.8rem;line-height:1.6}

  /* FOOTER */
  footer{background:var(--ink);color:var(--concrete);padding:clamp(4rem,8vh,6rem) 0 2rem}
  .foot-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.4fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2.5rem;padding-bottom:3rem;border-bottom:2px solid var(--line-d)}
  .foot-brand .logo{color:var(--concrete);font-size:2rem}
  .foot-brand p{color:var(--muted-d);margin-top:1rem;max-width:30ch;line-height:1.6;font-size:.9rem}
  .foot-col h4{font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent2);margin-bottom:1.2rem;font-weight:700}
  .foot-col a{display:block;font-size:.9rem;padding:.35rem 0;color:rgba(233,230,223,.75);transition:.3s}
  .foot-col a:hover{color:var(--concrete);padding-left:.4rem}
  .foot-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding-top:2rem;font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--muted-d);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem}

  .img-fallback{background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,var(--ink2),var(--ink2) 12px,#2c2922 12px,#2c2922 24px)!important}
  .img-fallback img{display:none}

  @media(max-width:980px){
    .menu{display:none}.burger{display:flex}
    .about-grid,.calc-grid,.contact-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .srv-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .proc-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .work-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .stats-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:2.5rem}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
  }
  @media(max-width:560px){
    .srv-grid,.proc-grid,.work-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    form .frow{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .sec-head .note{text-align:left}
  }
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="cursor"></div>
<div class="cursor-ring"></div>

<div id="preloader">
  <div class="top"><span>OBRA MESTRA</span><span data-i18n="preloader">Aixecant parets</span></div>
  <div class="count" id="pcount">0</div>
  <div><div class="bar"><i id="pbar"></i></div></div>
</div>

<header id="header">
  <div class="wrap nav">
    <a href="#" class="logo">OBRA MESTRA<span data-i18n="logo_sub">Reformes</span></a>
    <ul class="menu">
      <li><a href="#about" data-i18n="nav_about">Equip</a></li>
      <li><a href="#compare" data-i18n="nav_compare">Resultats</a></li>
      <li><a href="#services" data-i18n="nav_services">Serveis</a></li>
      <li><a href="#calc" data-i18n="nav_calc">Pressupost</a></li>
      <li><a href="#work" data-i18n="nav_work">Obres</a></li>
      <li><a href="#contact" data-i18n="nav_contact">Contacte</a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="nav-right">
      <div class="lang">
        <button data-lang="ca" class="active">CA</button>
        <button data-lang="es">ES</button>
        <button data-lang="en">EN</button>
      </div>
      <button class="burger" aria-label="menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<section class="hero">
  <div class="hero-bg"><img class="parallax" data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1541888946425-d81bb19240f5?w=1900&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""></div>
  <div class="wrap">
    <span class="eyebrow inv" data-i18n="hero_eyebrow">Reformes i obra nova · Barcelona</span>
    <h1 id="heroTitle" style="margin-top:1.4rem">
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w1">Obra</i></span>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w2">neta,</i></span><br>
      <span class="word"><i><em data-i18n="hero_w3">resultats</em></i></span>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w4">que</i></span>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w5">duren.</i></span>
    </h1>
    <div class="hero-foot">
      <p data-i18n="hero_sub">De la cuina al bany, de la reforma integral a l'obra nova: pressupost tancat, terminis clars i acabats que es noten.</p>
      <div class="hero-cta">
        <a href="#calc" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="hero_cta1">Calcular pressupost</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
        <a href="#work" class="btn inv"><span data-i18n="hero_cta2">Veure obres</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="scroll-ind"><span data-i18n="scroll">Baixa</span><i></i></div>
</section>

<div class="marquee">
  <div class="track" id="mtrack">
    <span><span data-i18n="m1">Reforma integral</span><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m2">Cuines a mida</span><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m3">Banys</span><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m4">Obra nova</span><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m5">Microciment</span><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m6">Impermeabilització</span><i class="dot"></i></span>
  </div>
</div>

<section class="about" id="about">
  <div class="wrap about-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="about_eyebrow">L'equip</span>
      <h2 id="aboutTitle" style="margin-top:1.4rem">
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l1">Vint anys</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l2">aixecant parets</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l3">i complint terminis.</i></span>
      </h2>
      <p style="color:var(--muted-l);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.8rem;max-width:48ch" data-i18n="about_p">Som un equip d'oficials de primera: paletes, electricistes, fontaners i pintors. Un sol interlocutor, un sol pressupost tancat i zero excuses. Si una cosa no es pot fer, t'ho diem abans de començar.</p>
      <a href="#services" class="btn" style="margin-top:2rem"><span data-i18n="about_cta">Què fem</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
    </div>
    <div class="media">
      <div class="reveal-mask"></div>
      <img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1504307651254-35680f356dfd?w=1100&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="Oficial d'obra">
      <div class="badge"><span><b>20</b><span data-i18n="badge">anys<br>d'ofici</span></span></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="compare-sec" id="compare">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:0">
      <div>
        <span class="eyebrow inv" data-i18n="cmp_eyebrow">Resultats reals</span>
        <h2 class="disp" style="margin-top:1rem;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,7vw,6rem)" data-i18n="cmp_title">Abans i <em>després</em></h2>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="compare" id="cmp1">
      <img class="after" data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1600585154340-be6161a56a0c?w=1200&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="Després">
      <img class="before" data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1581094794329-c8112a89af12?w=1200&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="Abans">
      <span class="tag b" data-i18n="cmp_before">Abans</span>
      <span class="tag a" data-i18n="cmp_after">Després</span>
      <div class="handle"></div>
      <input type="range" min="0" max="100" value="50" aria-label="Comparador abans i després">
    </div>
    <p class="compare-hint" data-i18n="cmp_hint">Arrossega el control · o fes servir el teclat</p>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="services" id="services">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="srv_title">Què <em>fem</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="srv_note">Un sol equip per a tota l'obra · sense subcontractar a cegues</p>
    </div>
    <div class="srv-grid">
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">01</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s1_t">Reforma integral</h3><p data-i18n="s1_d">Pis buit a casa acabada: enderrocs, instal·lacions, acabats i neteja final.</p></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">02</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s2_t">Cuines a mida</h3><p data-i18n="s2_d">Disseny, fontaneria, electricitat i muntatge del mobiliari, tot coordinat.</p></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">03</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s3_t">Banys</h3><p data-i18n="s3_d">Impermeabilització, plats a ras de terra i rajolat sense juntes vistes.</p></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">04</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s4_t">Obra nova</h3><p data-i18n="s4_d">Ampliacions, baixos i habitatge des de zero, amb direcció d'obra pròpia.</p></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">05</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s5_t">Pintura i microciment</h3><p data-i18n="s5_d">Acabats continus, parets de calç i microciment en terres i banys.</p></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><span class="n">06</span><div><h3 data-i18n="s6_t">Cobertes i humitats</h3><p data-i18n="s6_d">Impermeabilització de terrasses, baixants i tractament de capil·laritat.</p></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="process">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:0"><h2 data-i18n="proc_title">Com <em>treballem</em></h2></div>
    <div class="proc-grid">
      <div class="step"><div class="sn">01</div><h4 data-i18n="p1_t">Visita i mesures</h4><p data-i18n="p1_d">Anem al lloc, mesurem i escoltem què necessites de veritat.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="sn">02</div><h4 data-i18n="p2_t">Pressupost tancat</h4><p data-i18n="p2_d">Partides clares i preu tancat. El que signem és el que pagues.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="sn">03</div><h4 data-i18n="p3_t">Planificació</h4><p data-i18n="p3_d">Calendari d'oficis i terminis per escrit abans de tocar res.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="sn">04</div><h4 data-i18n="p4_t">Execució</h4><p data-i18n="p4_d">Equip fix a l'obra, neteja diària i un responsable sempre localitzable.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="sn">05</div><h4 data-i18n="p5_t">Entrega i garantia</h4><p data-i18n="p5_d">Revisió final plegats i garantia per escrit de l'execució.</p></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="calc" id="calc">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem)"><h2 data-i18n="calc_title">Calcula el teu <em>pressupost</em></h2></div>
    <div class="calc-grid">
      <div class="calc-form">
        <div class="field">
          <label data-i18n="calc_type">Tipus de reforma</label>
          <select id="calcType">
            <option value="integral" data-i18n="ct_integral">Reforma integral</option>
            <option value="cuina" data-i18n="ct_cuina">Cuina</option>
            <option value="bany" data-i18n="ct_bany">Bany</option>
            <option value="pintura" data-i18n="ct_pintura">Pintura i acabats</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label data-i18n="calc_m2">Metres quadrats</label>
          <div class="range-wrap">
            <input type="range" id="calcM2" min="10" max="200" value="70">
            <output id="calcM2out">70</output>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label data-i18n="calc_q">Qualitat dels acabats</label>
          <select id="calcQ">
            <option value="std" data-i18n="cq_std">Estàndard</option>
            <option value="prem" data-i18n="cq_prem">Premium</option>
            <option value="lux" data-i18n="cq_lux">Alta gamma</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="calc-out">
        <span class="lbl" data-i18n="calc_out_lbl">Estimació orientativa</span>
        <div class="big" id="calcOut">0 €</div>
        <div class="range" id="calcRange">0 € – 0 €</div>
        <p class="legal" data-i18n="calc_legal">±15% · IVA no inclòs · orientatiu. El pressupost real, sense compromís, després de la visita.</p>
        <a href="#contact" class="btn inv"><span data-i18n="calc_btn">Pressupost exacte</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="work" id="work">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:clamp(1.5rem,4vh,2.5rem)">
      <h2 data-i18n="work_title">Obres <em>recents</em></h2>
    </div>
    <div class="filters">
      <button class="filter active" data-filter="all" data-i18n="f_all">Totes</button>
      <button class="filter" data-filter="integral" data-i18n="f_integral">Integrals</button>
      <button class="filter" data-filter="cuina" data-i18n="f_cuina">Cuines</button>
      <button class="filter" data-filter="bany" data-i18n="f_bany">Banys</button>
    </div>
    <div class="work-grid" id="workGrid">
      <div class="witem" data-cat="integral"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1600210492486-724fe5c67fb0?w=900&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="wi"><span class="c" data-i18n="f_integral">Integrals</span><h3 data-i18n="w1">Pis Eixample · 95 m²</h3></div></div>

let curLang='ca';
function setLang(lang){
  curLang=lang;
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{
    const k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined) el.innerHTML = I18N[lang][k];
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
  calc();
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ LAZY IMG + FALLBACK ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(img=>{
  const src = img.getAttribute('data-src');
  img.onerror = ()=> img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');
  img.src = src;
});

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur = document.querySelector('.cursor'), ring = document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px'});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.18;ry+=(my-ry)*.18;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop)})();
document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.srv,.witem,.step').forEach(el=>{
  el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>ring.classList.add('grow'));
  el.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>ring.classList.remove('grow'));
});

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis = new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const id=a.getAttribute('href'); if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}
}));

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'), pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
const tl=gsap.timeline();
tl.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',
  onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%'}
})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.2')
.add(()=>{ heroIn(); });

/* ============ HERO IN ============ */
function heroIn(){
  if(reduce){gsap.set('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0});gsap.set('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-foot > *, .scroll-ind',{y:0,opacity:1});gsap.set('.hero-bg img',{scale:1});return;}
  gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.08});
  gsap.from('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-foot > *, .scroll-ind',{y:30,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.3});
  gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{scale:1,duration:1.6,ease:'power2.out'},0);
}
gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{yPercent:18,ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.hero',start:'top top',end:'bottom top',scrub:true}});

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ ABOUT ============ */
gsap.to('#aboutTitle .ln i',{y:0,duration:1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.12,scrollTrigger:{trigger:'#aboutTitle',start:'top 80%'}});
gsap.to('.about .reveal-mask',{scaleX:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.inOut',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 75%'}});
gsap.from('.about .badge',{x:40,opacity:0,duration:.8,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 60%'}});

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');
mtrack.innerHTML += mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:22,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ BEFORE/AFTER COMPARE ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.compare').forEach(cmp=>{
  const before=cmp.querySelector('.before'), handle=cmp.querySelector('.handle'), range=cmp.querySelector('input[type=range]');
  function update(v){before.style.clipPath=`inset(0 ${100-v}% 0 0)`;handle.style.left=v+'%';}
  range.addEventListener('input',()=>update(+range.value));
  let drag=false;
  function fromEvent(e){const r=cmp.getBoundingClientRect();const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width)*100));range.value=v;update(v);}
  cmp.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{drag=true;cmp.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);fromEvent(e);});
  cmp.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(drag)fromEvent(e);});
  cmp.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>drag=false);
  cmp.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>drag=false);
  update(+range.value);
});

/* ============ CALCULATOR ============ */
const FACT={integral:650,cuina:480,bany:420,pintura:35};
const QMUL={std:1,prem:1.4,lux:1.9};
const calcType=document.getElementById('calcType'),calcM2=document.getElementById('calcM2'),calcM2out=document.getElementById('calcM2out'),calcQ=document.getElementById('calcQ'),calcOut=document.getElementById('calcOut'),calcRange=document.getElementById('calcRange');
function fmt(n){const loc=curLang==='en'?'en-IE':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES';return new Intl.NumberFormat(loc,{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(n);}
function calc(){
  const m2=+calcM2.value, base=m2*FACT[calcType.value]*QMUL[calcQ.value];
  calcM2out.textContent=m2;
  calcRange.textContent=fmt(base*0.85)+' – '+fmt(base*1.15);
  if(reduce){calcOut.textContent=fmt(base);return;}
  gsap.to({v:0},{v:base,duration:1,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:true,onUpdate:function(){calcOut.textContent=fmt(Math.round(this.targets()[0].v));}});
}
[calcType,calcQ].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('change',calc));
calcM2.addEventListener('input',calc);
calc();

/* ============ PORTFOLIO FILTER ============ */
const filters=[...document.querySelectorAll('.filter')], witems=[...document.querySelectorAll('.witem')];
filters.forEach(f=>f.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  filters.forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===f));
  const id=f.dataset.filter;
  witems.forEach(it=>{
    const match=id==='all'||it.dataset.cat===id;
    if(match){
      gsap.set(it,{display:'block'});
      if(!reduce)gsap.fromTo(it,{opacity:0,scale:.96},{opacity:1,scale:1,duration:.5,ease:'power3.out',overwrite:true});
    }else{
      if(reduce){gsap.set(it,{display:'none'});}
      else gsap.to(it,{opacity:0,scale:.96,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(it,{display:'none'})});
    }
  });
}));

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.stat .num').forEach(num=>{
  const target=+num.dataset.count;
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:num,start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){num.textContent=target;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:2,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){num.textContent=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);}});
  }});
});

/* ============ GENERIC FADE-UP ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.srv,.step,.compare,.calc-grid > *,.testi blockquote,.contact-grid > *').forEach(el=>{
  gsap.from(el,{y:50,opacity:0,duration:.9,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}});
});

/* ============ CONTACT FORM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('ctForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});return;}
  gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}
  gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});
});

/* ============ MAGNETIC BUTTONS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(reduce)return;const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.3,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.4,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});});
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});

document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open')});

setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

<answer>

## Qué incluye esta web (negocio #3 — constructores / reformistas / albañiles)

**Modalidad nueva: brutalismo industrial.** Tercera identidad visual distinta a la #1 (claro mediterráneo) y la #2 (taberna oscura): base **hormigón** + bloques **negros** + acento **naranja-obra**, tipografía condensada **Anton** (titulares en mayúsculas, números en *outline* con `text-stroke`) + **Space Grotesk**, bordes gruesos de 2 px y sin radios. Marca ficticia *OBRA MESTRA*.

**Sello premium mantenido**
- GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis; preloader 0→100; reveal por palabras/líneas; parallax; cursor magnético; marquee; botones magnéticos; contadores; fade-up genérico.

**Dos módulos de front-end inéditos en la serie (la "otra modalidad")**
1. **Slider ANTES/DESPUÉS** accesible: un `<input type=range>` con `pointer-events:none` gestiona el **teclado** (Tab + flechas, con `:focus-within` para el foco visible), mientras los *pointer handlers* del contenedor gestionan **ratón y touch** (con `setPointerCapture`). Ambos mueven `clip-path` de la capa "antes" y la línea divisoria.
2. **Calculadora de presupuesto** en vivo: `m² × factor(tipo) × multiplicador(calidad)` → cifra animada con GSAP y **formato de moneda por idioma** (`Intl.NumberFormat` para `ca-ES / es-ES / en-IE`), más rango ±15%. Se recalcula al cambiar idioma.

**Más secciones propias del vertical**
- **Servicios** en grid brutalista con hover que invierte a negro y rellena el número en naranja.
- **Proceso** 01–05 (visita → presupuesto cerrado → planificación → ejecución → garantía).
- **Portfolio con filtro** animado por GSAP (Todas / Integrales / Cocinas / Baños).
- Stats, testimonio, **formulario de contacto** con overlay de confirmación animado, footer.

**Trilingüe real (CA / ES / EN)**
- Objeto `I18N` con **todas** las claves (pasos del proceso, opciones de los selects de la calculadora con `value` fijos independientes del idioma, etiquetas antes/después, categorías de filtro, mensaje de confirmación). Arranca en catalán y cambia `html lang`.

**Robustez y accesibilidad**
- Imágenes de **Unsplash** vía `data-src` con `onerror → .img-fallback` (gradiente tipo "cinta de obra"); `clamp()` + *breakpoints* a 980px / 560px; respeta `prefers-reduced-motion` con *sets*.

## Cómo usarla
1. Copia el bloque en `index.html` y ábrelo (necesita internet para fuentes, GSAP, Lenis e imágenes).
2. Edita textos, factores de la calculadora (`FACT`/`QMUL`), teléfono y dirección en el HTML **y** en `I18N` (las 3 lenguas).
3. Para producción: sustituye las URLs por tus fotos reales de antes/después y conecta el formulario a un backend/servicio.

## Siguiente paso
Cuando me digas **`. 4`**, construiré la web completa premium para el tipo de cliente **#4 — Asesorías, gestorías y despachos de abogados** (con su propia modalidad de front-end e identidad visual).

</answer>
