## [ASSISTANT] 2026-07-24 10:27 · Qwen3.8-Max-Preview

Aquí tienes la web premium del cliente **#4 — asesorías / gestorías / despachos de abogados** (marca *MONTCLAR & ASSOCIATS*), en una **modalidad nueva: editorial corporativo sobrio** (marfil + azul tinta + latón, serif de autoridad *Cormorant Garamond* + *Manrope*, aire de documento oficial). Mismo sello GSAP/Lenis/trilingüe, con **tres módulos de front-end inéditos** en la serie: un **acordeón de FAQ animado por altura**, un **wizard de 2 pasos que recomienda un plan**, y un **calendario fiscal en timeline con progreso de scroll**. Un solo archivo `index.html`. Imágenes de **Unsplash (libres)** con *fallback*.

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ca">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>MONTCLAR & ASSOCIATS — Assessoria fiscal, laboral i jurídica / Asesoría / Advisory</title>
<meta name="description" content="MONTCLAR & ASSOCIATS — Assessoria fiscal, laboral, mercantil i jurídica per a autònoms i societats. Honoraris clars." />

<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cormorant+Garamond:ital,wght@0,400;0,500;0,600;1,400;1,500&family=Manrope:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/ScrollTrigger.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.1.13/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>

<style>
  :root{
    --paper:#F6F3EC;
    --paper2:#EDE8DC;
    --ink:#10243B;
    --ink2:#16314F;
    --brass:#A98545;
    --brass2:#C2A05C;
    --muted-l:#5E6B78;
    --muted-d:rgba(246,243,236,.62);
    --line:rgba(16,36,59,.14);
    --line-d:rgba(246,243,236,.16);
    --serif:'Cormorant Garamond',Georgia,serif;
    --sans:'Manrope',system-ui,sans-serif;
    --ease:cubic-bezier(.76,0,.24,1);
  }
  *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
  html,body{background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--sans);-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;overflow-x:hidden}
  body{cursor:none}
  a{color:inherit;text-decoration:none}
  img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}
  ::selection{background:var(--brass);color:var(--paper)}
  .wrap{width:min(92%,1280px);margin-inline:auto}
  em{font-style:italic;color:var(--brass)}
  .serif{font-family:var(--serif)}
  .eyebrow{font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.34em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brass);font-weight:600;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.8rem}
  .eyebrow::before{content:"";width:30px;height:1px;background:var(--brass)}
  .eyebrow.inv{color:var(--brass2)}.eyebrow.inv::before{background:var(--brass2)}

  /* CURSOR */
  .cursor{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--brass2);pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;mix-blend-mode:difference;transform:translate(-50%,-50%)}
  .cursor-ring{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:44px;height:44px;border:1px solid var(--ink);border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none;z-index:9998;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);transition:width .3s var(--ease),height .3s var(--ease),background .3s,border-color .3s}
  .cursor-ring.grow{width:84px;height:84px;background:rgba(169,133,69,.12);border-color:var(--brass)}
  @media(hover:none){body{cursor:auto}.cursor,.cursor-ring{display:none}}

  /* PRELOADER */
  #preloader{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);z-index:10000;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;padding:clamp(1.4rem,4vw,3rem)}
  #preloader .top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.32em;text-transform:uppercase;opacity:.7}
  #preloader .count{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(5rem,22vw,18rem);line-height:.8;color:var(--brass2);font-weight:500}
  #preloader .bar{height:1px;background:rgba(246,243,236,.18);overflow:hidden}
  #preloader .bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:var(--brass2)}

  /* NAV */
  header{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;z-index:900;transition:background .4s,backdrop-filter .4s}
  header.scrolled{background:rgba(246,243,236,.86);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .nav{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:1.3rem 0}
  .logo{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.45rem;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.06em;display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1}
  .logo span{font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.34em;color:var(--brass);font-family:var(--sans);font-weight:600;margin-top:.35rem;text-transform:uppercase}
  .menu{display:flex;gap:2.1rem;list-style:none;font-size:.8rem;letter-spacing:.06em;font-weight:500}
  .menu a{position:relative;padding:.3rem 0}
  .menu a::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;bottom:0;width:0;height:1px;background:var(--brass);transition:width .4s var(--ease)}
  .menu a:hover::after{width:100%}
  .nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1.3rem}
  .lang{display:flex;gap:.2rem;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;padding:.2rem}
  .lang button{border:0;background:transparent;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.14em;font-weight:600;padding:.35rem .6rem;cursor:none;color:var(--muted-l);transition:.3s}
  .lang button.active{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper)}
  .burger{display:none;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;background:none;border:0;cursor:none}
  .burger i{width:26px;height:1px;background:var(--ink);transition:.3s}

  /* BUTTONS */
  .btn{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem;padding:.95rem 1.8rem;font-size:.76rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600;overflow:hidden;cursor:none;border:1px solid var(--ink);transition:color .4s var(--ease)}
  .btn .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--ink);transform:translateY(101%);transition:transform .5s var(--ease);z-index:0}
  .btn span{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .btn:hover{color:var(--paper)}
  .btn:hover .fill{transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);border-color:var(--ink)}
  .btn.solid .fill{background:var(--brass);transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid:hover{color:var(--ink)}
  .btn.solid:hover .fill{transform:translateY(-101%)}
  .btn.inv{border-color:var(--paper);color:var(--paper)}
  .btn.inv .fill{background:var(--brass)}
  .btn.inv:hover{color:var(--ink)}

  /* HERO */
  .hero{min-height:100vh;position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;padding:clamp(7rem,14vh,10rem) 0 clamp(3rem,8vh,6rem);overflow:hidden}
  .hero-bg{position:absolute;inset:0;z-index:0}
  .hero-bg img{transform:scale(1.15);opacity:.16;filter:grayscale(.3)}
  .hero-bg::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--paper) 35%,rgba(246,243,236,.4))}
  .hero .wrap{position:relative;z-index:2;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr .7fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,5rem);align-items:end}
  .hero h1{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:500;font-size:clamp(2.8rem,8vw,7.5rem);line-height:.96;letter-spacing:-.01em}
  .hero h1 .word{display:inline-block;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:top}
  .hero h1 .word i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .hero h1 em{font-style:italic;color:var(--brass)}
  .hero-side{border-left:1px solid var(--line);padding-left:clamp(1.2rem,3vw,2.4rem)}
  .hero-side p{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.98rem;line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:1.8rem}
  .hero-cta{display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .hero-meta{display:flex;gap:2.5rem;margin-top:clamp(2.5rem,6vh,4rem);border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:1.6rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .hero-meta div{font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--muted-l);text-transform:uppercase}
  .hero-meta b{display:block;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.7rem;color:var(--ink);letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none;margin-bottom:.2rem;font-weight:600}
  .scroll-ind{position:absolute;bottom:1.4rem;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);z-index:2;font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted-l);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:.6rem}
  .scroll-ind i{width:1px;height:38px;background:var(--muted-l);animation:drop 1.8s var(--ease) infinite}
  @keyframes drop{0%{transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:top}50%{transform:scaleY(1);transform-origin:top}51%{transform-origin:bottom}100%{transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:bottom}}

  /* MARQUEE */
  .marquee{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);padding:1.3rem 0;overflow:hidden;border-block:1px solid rgba(246,243,236,.1)}
  .marquee .track{display:flex;gap:3rem;white-space:nowrap;width:max-content;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.3rem,3vw,2.2rem);font-style:italic}
  .marquee .track span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3rem}
  .marquee .track em{color:var(--brass2);font-style:normal;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase}
  .marquee .dot{width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--brass);display:inline-block}

  /* ABOUT */
  .about{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .about-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:center}
  .about h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:500;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4.4rem);line-height:1.04}
  .about h2 .ln{display:block;overflow:hidden}
  .about h2 .ln i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .about .media{position:relative;aspect-ratio:4/5;overflow:hidden}
  .about .media .reveal-mask{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--ink);transform-origin:left;z-index:2}
  .about .creds{margin-top:2rem;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.4rem 2rem}
  .about .creds div{border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:1rem}
  .about .creds b{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:600;display:block}
  .about .creds span{font-size:.78rem;color:var(--muted-l);line-height:1.5;display:block;margin-top:.3rem}

  /* AREAS (document style) */
  .areas{background:var(--paper2);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0;border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
  .sec-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:2rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2.5rem,6vh,4rem);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .sec-head h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:500;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);line-height:1}
  .sec-head .note{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.82rem;letter-spacing:.06em;max-width:26ch;text-align:right;line-height:1.6}
  .area-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
  .area{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;gap:1.6rem;align-items:start;padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem) clamp(1rem,2vw,2rem);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);transition:background .4s var(--ease);cursor:none}
  .area:nth-child(odd){border-right:1px solid var(--line)}
  .area .rom{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.4rem;color:var(--brass);font-style:italic}
  .area h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.4rem,2.6vw,2rem);font-weight:600;line-height:1.05}
  .area p{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.86rem;line-height:1.6;margin-top:.6rem;max-width:42ch}
  .area .ar{font-size:1.2rem;color:var(--ink);transition:transform .4s var(--ease),color .4s}
  .area:hover{background:var(--paper)}
  .area:hover .ar{transform:translateX(6px);color:var(--brass)}

  /* WIZARD */
  .wizard-sec{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .wizard{max-width:880px;margin:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem) auto 0;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--paper);position:relative;min-height:420px}
  .wbar{display:flex;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .wbar .ws{flex:1;padding:1.1rem 1.4rem;font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted-l);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem}
  .wbar .ws .n{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.1rem;color:var(--brass);font-style:italic}
  .wbar .ws.active{color:var(--ink);background:var(--paper2)}
  .wbar .ws.done{color:var(--ink)}
  .wbody{padding:clamp(1.8rem,4vw,3rem)}
  .wstep{display:none}
  .wstep h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3vw,2.2rem);font-weight:600;margin-bottom:.4rem}
  .wstep .sub{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.9rem;margin-bottom:1.8rem}
  .choices{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:1rem}
  .choice{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:1.4rem 1.2rem;cursor:none;text-align:center;transition:.3s var(--ease);background:var(--paper)}
  .choice:hover{border-color:var(--brass)}
  .choice.sel{border-color:var(--ink);background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper)}
  .choice .ic{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.8rem;color:var(--brass);display:block;margin-bottom:.6rem}
  .choice.sel .ic{color:var(--brass2)}
  .choice b{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.2rem;font-weight:600;display:block}
  .choice span{font-size:.76rem;color:var(--muted-l);display:block;margin-top:.4rem;line-height:1.4}
  .choice.sel span{color:var(--muted-d)}
  .chips{display:flex;gap:.7rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .chip{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.7rem 1.2rem;font-size:.8rem;letter-spacing:.04em;cursor:none;transition:.3s;font-weight:500}
  .chip.sel{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);border-color:var(--ink)}
  .wnav{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:2.2rem;gap:1rem}
  .wresult{display:none;text-align:center;padding:1rem 0}
  .wresult .tag{font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.24em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brass);font-weight:600}
  .wresult h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.4rem);font-weight:600;margin:.6rem 0}
  .wresult .price{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.6rem,4vw,2.6rem);color:var(--brass)}
  .wresult .price small{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--muted-l);font-family:var(--sans)}
  .wresult ul{list-style:none;max-width:34ch;margin:1.6rem auto;text-align:left}
  .wresult li{padding:.6rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:.9rem;color:var(--muted-l);display:flex;gap:.7rem}
  .wresult li::before{content:"—";color:var(--brass)}

  /* TIMELINE / CALENDARI FISCAL */
  .timeline-sec{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .tl{position:relative;max-width:760px;margin:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem) auto 0;padding-left:2.4rem}
  .tl-line{position:absolute;left:7px;top:6px;bottom:6px;width:1px;background:var(--line-d)}
  .tl-fill{position:absolute;left:7px;top:6px;width:1px;background:var(--brass2);height:100%;transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:top}
  .tl-item{position:relative;padding:0 0 clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem) 1.6rem}
  .tl-item:last-child{padding-bottom:0}
  .tl-item .dot{position:absolute;left:-2.4rem;top:.3rem;width:15px;height:15px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid var(--brass2);background:var(--ink);transition:.5s var(--ease)}
  .tl-item.on .dot{background:var(--brass2);box-shadow:0 0 0 5px rgba(194,160,92,.18)}
  .tl-item .when{font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brass2);font-weight:600}
  .tl-item h4{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.3rem,2.6vw,1.9rem);font-weight:600;margin:.4rem 0 .5rem;opacity:.5;transition:.5s}
  .tl-item.on h4{opacity:1}
  .tl-item p{color:var(--muted-d);font-size:.86rem;line-height:1.6;max-width:48ch;opacity:.5;transition:.5s}
  .tl-item.on p{opacity:1}

  /* FAQ ACCORDION */
  .faq{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .faq-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:.8fr 1.2fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:start}
  .acc-item{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .acc-q{width:100%;background:none;border:0;cursor:none;text-align:left;padding:1.5rem 0;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:1rem;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.2rem,2.4vw,1.7rem);font-weight:600;color:var(--ink)}
  .acc-q .pm{font-family:var(--sans);font-size:1.4rem;color:var(--brass);transition:transform .4s var(--ease);flex:none}
  .acc-item.open .acc-q .pm{transform:rotate(45deg)}
  .acc-a{height:0;overflow:hidden}
  .acc-a p{color:var(--muted-l);font-size:.92rem;line-height:1.7;padding:0 0 1.6rem;max-width:54ch}

  /* TEAM */
  .team{background:var(--paper2);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0;border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
  .team-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:clamp(1.2rem,2.5vw,2rem)}
  .member{cursor:none}
  .member .ph{aspect-ratio:3/4;overflow:hidden;filter:grayscale(1);transition:filter .6s var(--ease)}
  .member:hover .ph{filter:grayscale(0)}
  .member h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:600;margin-top:1rem}
  .member .role{font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brass);margin-top:.3rem}

  /* STATS */
  .stats{padding:clamp(4rem,9vh,7rem) 0}
  .stats-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:2rem;text-align:center}
  .stat .num{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(3rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:1;color:var(--ink);font-weight:500}
  .stat .num em{color:var(--brass)}
  .stat .lbl{font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:.8rem;color:var(--muted-l)}

  /* TESTI */
  .testi{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0;text-align:center}
  .testi blockquote{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(1.6rem,4vw,3.2rem);line-height:1.25;max-width:20ch;margin:2rem auto}
  .testi cite{font-style:normal;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--muted-d)}

  /* CTA + FORM */
  .contact{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0}
  .contact-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:start}
  .contact h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:500;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);line-height:1}
  .contact .lead{color:var(--muted-l);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.6rem;max-width:34ch}
  .contact .info{margin-top:2rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.7rem;font-weight:500}
  .contact .info a{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding-bottom:.5rem;transition:.3s}
  .contact .info a:hover{border-color:var(--brass);color:var(--brass)}
  form .frow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.2rem}
  .cfield{margin-bottom:1.3rem}
  .cfield.full{grid-column:1/-1}
  .cfield label{display:block;font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted-l);margin-bottom:.5rem}
  .cfield input,.cfield select,.cfield textarea{width:100%;background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:.6rem 0;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.95rem;color:var(--ink);outline:none;cursor:none;transition:border-color .3s}
  .cfield textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:70px}
  .cfield input:focus,.cfield select:focus,.cfield textarea:focus{border-color:var(--brass)}
  .cfield select{appearance:none}

  .res-ok{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9500;background:rgba(16,36,59,.9);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);display:none;place-items:center;padding:1.5rem}
  .res-ok .card{background:var(--paper);color:var(--ink);padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);text-align:center;max-width:440px}
  .res-ok .check{width:74px;height:74px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ink);color:var(--brass2);display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0 auto 1.4rem;font-size:2rem}
  .res-ok h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.9rem;font-weight:600}
  .res-ok p{color:var(--muted-l);margin:1rem 0 1.8rem;line-height:1.6}

  /* FOOTER */
  footer{background:var(--ink);color:var(--paper);padding:clamp(4rem,8vh,6rem) 0 2rem}
  .foot-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.5fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2.5rem;padding-bottom:3rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .foot-brand .logo{color:var(--paper)}
  .foot-brand p{color:var(--muted-d);margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:32ch;line-height:1.6;font-size:.9rem}
  .foot-col h4{font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--brass2);margin-bottom:1.2rem;font-weight:600}
  .foot-col a{display:block;font-size:.9rem;padding:.35rem 0;color:rgba(246,243,236,.75);transition:.3s}
  .foot-col a:hover{color:var(--paper);padding-left:.4rem}
  .foot-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding-top:2rem;font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--muted-d);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem}

  .img-fallback{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--ink2),var(--brass))!important}
  .img-fallback img{display:none}

  @media(max-width:980px){
    .menu{display:none}.burger{display:flex}
    .hero .wrap{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .hero-side{border-left:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-left:0;padding-top:1.6rem}
    .about-grid,.faq-grid,.contact-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .area-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .area:nth-child(odd){border-right:0}
    .team-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .stats-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:2.5rem}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .choices{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  }
  @media(max-width:560px){
    form .frow{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .sec-head .note{text-align:left}
    .about .creds{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  }
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="cursor"></div>
<div class="cursor-ring"></div>

<div id="preloader">
  <div class="top"><span>MONTCLAR & ASSOCIATS</span><span data-i18n="preloader">Preparant l'expedient</span></div>
  <div class="count" id="pcount">0</div>
  <div><div class="bar"><i id="pbar"></i></div></div>
</div>

<header id="header">
  <div class="wrap nav">
    <a href="#" class="logo">MONTCLAR<span data-i18n="logo_sub">& Associats · 1998</span></a>
    <ul class="menu">
      <li><a href="#about" data-i18n="nav_about">Despatx</a></li>
      <li><a href="#areas" data-i18n="nav_areas">Àrees</a></li>
      <li><a href="#plan" data-i18n="nav_plan">El teu pla</a></li>
      <li><a href="#calendar" data-i18n="nav_calendar">Calendari</a></li>
      <li><a href="#faq" data-i18n="nav_faq">Dubtes</a></li>
      <li><a href="#contact" data-i18n="nav_contact">Consulta</a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="nav-right">
      <div class="lang">
        <button data-lang="ca" class="active">CA</button>
        <button data-lang="es">ES</button>
        <button data-lang="en">EN</button>
      </div>
      <button class="burger" aria-label="menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<section class="hero">
  <div class="hero-bg"><img class="parallax" data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1589829545856-d10d557cf95f?w=1900&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""></div>
  <div class="wrap">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="hero_eyebrow">Assessoria fiscal · laboral · jurídica</span>
      <h1 id="heroTitle" style="margin-top:1.4rem">
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w1">Tranquil·litat</i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w2">fiscal,</i></span><br>
        <span class="word"><i><em data-i18n="hero_w3">decisions</em></i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w4">amb</i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w5">criteri.</i></span>
      </h1>
    </div>
    <div class="hero-side">
      <p data-i18n="hero_sub">Des del 1998 portem els números i els papers d'autònoms, pimes i famílies. Tu decideixes; nosaltres ho deixem tot en ordre i a temps.</p>
      <div class="hero-cta">
        <a href="#contact" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="hero_cta1">Primera consulta</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
        <a href="#plan" class="btn"><span data-i18n="hero_cta2">Quin pla em cal?</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="wrap hero-meta" style="position:relative;z-index:2">
    <div><b>1998</b><span data-i18n="hm1">Any de fundació</span></div>
    <div><b>+1.200</b><span data-i18n="hm2">Clients actius</span></div>
    <div><b>48h</b><span data-i18n="hm3">Resposta garantida</span></div>
    <div><b>4</b><span data-i18n="hm4">Àrees especialitzades</span></div>
  </div>
  <div class="scroll-ind"><span data-i18n="scroll">Descobreix</span><i></i></div>
</section>

<div class="marquee">
  <div class="track" id="mtrack">
    <span><span data-i18n="m1">Fiscal</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m2">Autònoms</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m3">Laboral</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m4">Societats</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m5">Mercantil</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m6">Herències</em><i class="dot"></i></span>
  </div>
</div>

<section class="about" id="about">
  <div class="wrap about-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="about_eyebrow">El despatx</span>
      <h2 id="aboutTitle" style="margin-top:1.4rem">
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l1">Un despatx</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l2">que t'explica</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l3">les coses <em>clar</em>.</i></span>
      </h2>
      <p style="color:var(--muted-l);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.8rem;max-width:48ch" data-i18n="about_p">No parlem en clau fiscal per impressionar. Traduïm la normativa a decisions entenedores, t'avisem dels terminis abans que arribin i defensem els teus interessos davant de l'administració si cal.</p>
      <div class="creds">
        <div><b data-i18n="cr1_b">Col·legiats</b><span data-i18n="cr1_d">Economistes i advocats col·legiats i assegurats.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr2_b">Confidencial</b><span data-i18n="cr2_d">Secret professional i dades protegides per contracte.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr3_b">Honoraris clars</b><span data-i18n="cr3_d">Quota tancada, sense sorpreses a final de mes.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr4_b">Proximitat</b><span data-i18n="cr4_d">Un gestor assignat que coneix el teu cas.</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="media">
      <div class="reveal-mask"></div>
      <img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1521791136064-7986c2920216?w=1100&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="Reunió de despatx">
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="areas" id="areas">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="areas_title">Àrees de <em>pràctica</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="areas_note">Quatre especialitats, un sol equip que coordina tot el teu expedient.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="area-grid">
      <div class="area"><span class="rom">I</span><div><h3 data-i18n="a1_t">Fiscal i comptable</h3><p data-i18n="a1_d">IVA, IRPF, Societats, comptes anuals i planificació fiscal per pagar el just.</p></div><span class="ar">→</span></div>
      <div class="area"><span class="rom">II</span><div><h3 data-i18n="a2_t">Laboral i nòmines</h3><p data-i18n="a2_d">Altes, contractes, nòmines, seguretat social i acomiadaments ben fets.</p></div><span class="ar">→</span></div>
      <div class="area"><span class="rom">III</span><div><h3 data-i18n="a3_t">Mercantil i societats</h3><p data-i18n="a3_d">Constitució, estatuts, juntes, compravenda de participacions i fusions.</p></div><span class="ar">→</span></div>
      <div class="area"><span class="rom">IV</span><div><h3 data-i18n="a4_t">Jurídic i herències</h3><p data-i18n="a4_d">Contractes, reclamacions, successions, donacions i plusvàlua municipal.</p></div><span class="ar">→</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="wizard-sec" id="plan">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:0">
      <div>
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="wiz_eyebrow">El teu pla</span>
        <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="wiz_title">Què et <em>cal</em>?</h2>
      </div>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="wiz_note">Dues preguntes i et recomanem el pla d'assessoria més ajustat al teu cas.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="wizard" id="wizard">
      <div class="wbar">
        <div class="ws active" data-step="1"><span class="n">01</span><span data-i18n="ws1">El teu perfil</span></div>
        <div class="ws" data-step="2"><span class="n">02</span><span data-i18n="ws2">Què necessites</span></div>
        <div class="ws" data-step="3"><span class="n">03</span><span data-i18n="ws3">El teu pla</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="wbody">
        <!-- STEP 1 -->
        <div class="wstep" data-step="1" style="display:block">
          <h3 data-i18n="w1_h">Com et presentes a Hisenda?</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="w1_sub">Tria l'opció que més s'assembli a la teva situació actual.</p>
          <div class="choices" id="wProfile">
            <div class="choice" data-val="auto"><span class="ic">§</span><b data-i18n="wp_auto">Autònom</b><span data-i18n="wp_auto_d">Activitat per compte propi</span></div>
            <div class="choice" data-val="soc"><span class="ic">⌘</span><b data-i18n="wp_soc">Societat</b><span data-i18n="wp_soc_d">SL, SC o comunitat de béns</span></div>
            <div class="choice" data-val="fam"><span class="ic">⚖</span><b data-i18n="wp_fam">Particular</b><span data-i18n="wp_fam_d">Renda, herència o patrimoni</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="wnav"><span></span><button class="btn solid" id="wNext1"><span data-i18n="w_next">Següent</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
        </div>
        <!-- STEP 2 -->
        <div class="wstep" data-step="2">
          <h3 data-i18n="w2_h">Què vols que portem?</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="w2_sub">Marca tot el que necessitis; pots triar-ne més d'un.</p>
          <div class="chips" id="wNeeds">
            <div class="chip" data-val="fisc" data-i18n="wn_fisc">Fiscal i comptable</div>
            <div class="chip" data-val="lab" data-i18n="wn_lab">Nòmines i laboral</div>
            <div class="chip" data-val="merc" data-i18n="wn_merc">Mercantil</div>
            <div class="chip" data-val="jur" data-i18n="wn_jur">Jurídic puntual</div>
          </div>
          <div class="wnav"><button class="btn" id="wBack2"><span data-i18n="w_back">Enrere</span><span class="fill"></span></button><button class="btn solid" id="wNext2"><span data-i18n="w_see">Veure el pla</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
        </div>
        <!-- STEP 3 -->
        <div class="wstep" data-step="3">
          <div class="wresult" id="wResult" style="display:block">
            <span class="tag" data-i18n="wr_tag">Et recomanem</span>
            <h3 id="wrName">—</h3>
            <div class="price"><span id="wrPrice">—</span> <small data-i18n="wr_per">/ mes + IVA</small></div>
            <ul id="wrList"></ul>
            <a href="#contact" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="wr_cta">Contractar aquest pla</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="timeline-sec" id="calendar">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:0">
      <div>
        <span class="eyebrow inv" data-i18n="cal_eyebrow">Calendari fiscal</span>
        <h2 class="serif" style="margin-top:1rem;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);font-weight:500" data-i18n="cal_title">Els terminis que <em>no pots</em> perdre</h2>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="tl" id="tl">
      <div class="tl-line"></div>
      <div class="tl-fill" id="tlFill"></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t1_w">Gener · 1–30</div><h4 data-i18n="t1_t">Resum anual i IVA Q4</h4><p data-i18n="t1_d">Models 303, 390 i 190. Tanquem l'exercici i preparem el resum de l'any.</p></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t2_w">Març · 1–31</div><h4 data-i18n="t2_t">Impost de Societats (exercici tancat)</h4><p data-i18n="t2_d">Presentació del model 200 per a societats amb exercici natural.</p></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t3_w">Abril · 1–25</div><h4 data-i18n="t3_t">IVA Q1 i pagaments a compte</h4><p data-i18n="t3_d">Models 303, 130 i 131. Primer trimestre fiscal de l'any.</p></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t4_w">Abril–Juny</div><h4 data-i18n="t4_t">Campanya de la Renda</h4><p data-i18n="t4_d">Declaració de l'IRPF. Revisem deduccions i evitem errors costosos.</p></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t5_w">Juliol · 1–25</div><h4 data-i18n="t5_t">IVA Q2 i Societats</h4><p data-i18n="t5_d">Segon trimestre i termini general de l'impost de societats.</p></div>
      <div class="tl-item"><span class="dot"></span><div class="when" data-i18n="t6_w">Octubre · 1–25</div><h4 data-i18n="t6_t">IVA Q3 i pagaments a compte</h4><p data-i18n="t6_d">Tercer trimestre. Revisem previsió de tancament per evitar sobresalts.</p></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="faq" id="faq">
  <div class="wrap faq-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="faq_eyebrow">Dubtes freqüents</span>
      <h2 style="margin-top:1rem;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);font-weight:500;line-height:1" data-i18n="faq_title">El que tothom <em>pregunta</em></h2>
      <p style="color:var(--muted-l);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.6rem;max-width:30ch" data-i18n="faq_lead">Si no trobes la teva resposta, escriu-nos: la primera consulta és gratuïta.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="acc" id="acc">
      <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q1">Quant costa portar la meva comptabilitat?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a1">Depèn del volum de factures i del règim fiscal. Un autònom en mòduls parteix d'una quota molt ajustada; una societat amb treballadors puja de nivell. T'ho detallem per escrit abans de començar, sense compromís.</p></div></div>
      <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q2">Puc canviar de gestor a mig any?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a2">Sí, en qualsevol moment. Nosaltres gestionem el traspàs d'expedient amb el gestor anterior i t'acompanyem perquè no es perdi cap termini durant el canvi.</p></div></div>
      <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q3">Ateneu també en català i telemàticament?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a3">I tant. Treballem en català, castellà i anglès, i tot l'expedient és accessible des d'un entorn segur en línia, a més de les visites presencials al despatx.</p></div></div>
      <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q4">Què passa si rebo una notificació d'Hisenda?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a4">Ens la reenvies i nosaltres la gestionem: comprovem el contingut, preparem la resposta o l'al·legació i et representem davant de l'administració si cal.</p></div></div>
      <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q5">Porteu també temes d'herències i plusvàlua?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a5">Sí, dins l'àrea jurídica i fiscal: successions, donacions, plusvàlua municipal i planificació patrimonial per minimitzar la càrrega fiscal legalment.</p></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="contact" id="contact">
  <div class="wrap contact-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="ct_eyebrow">Consulta</span>
      <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="ct_title">Parlem del teu <em>cas</em></h2>
      <p class="lead" data-i18n="ct_lead">La primera consulta és gratuïta i sense compromís. Explica'ns la situació i et diem, amb números, com t'ho podem resoldre.</p>
      <div class="info">
        <a href="tel:+34933000000">+34 933 00 00 00</a>
        <a href="mailto:hola@montclar.cat">hola@montclar.cat</a>
        <span data-i18n="ct_addr">Passeig de Gràcia, 88 · 08008 Barcelona</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <form id="ctForm" novalidate>
      <div class="frow">
        <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_name">Nom</label><input type="text" required></div>
        <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_phone">Telèfon</label><input type="tel" required></div>
      </div>
      <div class="frow">
        <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_email">Correu</label><input type="email" required></div>
        <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_area">Àrea</label>
          <select required>
            <option value="" data-i18n="f_area_ph">Tria</option>
            <option data-i18n="a1_t">Fiscal i comptable</option>
            <option data-i18n="a2_t">Laboral i nòmines</option>
            <option data-i18n="a3_t">Mercantil i societats</option>
            <option data-i18n="a4_t">Jurídic i herències</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="cfield full"><label data-i18n="f_msg">Explica el teu cas</label><textarea></textarea></div>
      <button type="submit" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="f_submit">Demanar consulta</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
    </form>
  </div>
</section>

<div class="res-ok" id="resOk">

};
let curLang='ca';
function setLang(lang){
  curLang=lang;
  document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{
    const k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined) el.innerHTML = I18N[lang][k];
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
  renderResult();
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ LAZY IMG + FALLBACK ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(img=>{
  const src = img.getAttribute('data-src');
  img.onerror = ()=> img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');
  img.src = src;
});

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur = document.querySelector('.cursor'), ring = document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px'});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.18;ry+=(my-ry)*.18;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop)})();
document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.area,.choice,.chip,.member').forEach(el=>{
  el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>ring.classList.add('grow'));
  el.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>ring.classList.remove('grow'));
});

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis = new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const id=a.getAttribute('href'); if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}
}));

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'), pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
const tl=gsap.timeline();
tl.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',
  onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%'}
})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.2')
.add(()=>{ heroIn(); });

/* ============ HERO IN ============ */
function heroIn(){
  if(reduce){gsap.set('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0});gsap.set('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-side, .hero-meta > *, .scroll-ind',{y:0,opacity:1});gsap.set('.hero-bg img',{scale:1});return;}
  gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.08});
  gsap.from('.hero .eyebrow, .hero-side, .hero-meta > *, .scroll-ind',{y:30,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.3});
  gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{scale:1.15,duration:1.6,ease:'power2.out'},0);
}
gsap.to('.hero-bg img',{yPercent:14,ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.hero',start:'top top',end:'bottom top',scrub:true}});

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ ABOUT ============ */
gsap.to('#aboutTitle .ln i',{y:0,duration:1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.12,scrollTrigger:{trigger:'#aboutTitle',start:'top 80%'}});
gsap.to('.about .reveal-mask',{scaleX:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.inOut',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 75%'}});
gsap.from('.about .creds div',{y:24,opacity:0,duration:.7,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .creds',start:'top 80%'}});

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');
mtrack.innerHTML += mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:26,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ AREAS FADE ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.area').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{y:40,opacity:0,duration:.8,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 90%'}}));

/* ============ WIZARD ============ */
const wsteps=[...document.querySelectorAll('.wstep')], wbars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.wbar .ws')];
let wProfile=null; const wNeeds=new Set();
function goStep(n){
  wsteps.forEach(s=>s.style.display = (+s.dataset.step===n)?'block':'none');
  wbars.forEach(b=>{const sn=+b.dataset.step;b.classList.toggle('active',sn===n);b.classList.toggle('done',sn<n);});
  if(!reduce){const cur=wsteps.find(s=>+s.dataset.step===n);gsap.fromTo(cur,{opacity:0,x:24},{opacity:1,x:0,duration:.5,ease:'power3.out'});}
}
document.querySelectorAll('#wProfile .choice').forEach(c=>c.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  document.querySelectorAll('#wProfile .choice').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));
  c.classList.add('sel'); wProfile=c.dataset.val;
}));
document.querySelectorAll('#wNeeds .chip').forEach(c=>c.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  c.classList.toggle('sel');
  if(wNeeds.has(c.dataset.val))wNeeds.delete(c.dataset.val);else wNeeds.add(c.dataset.val);
}));
document.getElementById('wNext1').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(wProfile)goStep(2);else flash('#wProfile');});
document.getElementById('wBack2').addEventListener('click',()=>goStep(1));
document.getElementById('wNext2').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(wNeeds.size){computePlan();goStep(3);}else flash('#wNeeds');});
function flash(sel){if(reduce)return;gsap.fromTo(sel,{x:-6},{x:6,duration:.06,repeat:3,yoyo:true});}

const PLANS={
  basic:{name:'pl_basic',price:59,desc:'pl_basic_d',feat:['wr_f1','wr_f6']},
  pro:{name:'pl_pro',price:149,desc:'pl_pro_d',feat:['wr_f1','wr_f2','wr_f3','wr_f6']},
  full:{name:'pl_full',price:289,desc:'pl_full_d',feat:['wr_f1','wr_f2','wr_f3','wr_f4','wr_f5','wr_f6']}
};
function computePlan(){
  let key='basic';
  if(wNeeds.has('lab')||wNeeds.has('merc'))key='pro';
  if(wNeeds.has('merc')&&wNeeds.has('jur'))key='full';
  if(wProfile==='soc'&&wNeeds.size>=2)key='full';
  if(wProfile==='fam')key = wNeeds.has('jur')?'pro':'basic';
  window.__plan=key;
  renderResult();
}
function fmt(n){const loc=curLang==='en'?'en-IE':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES';return new Intl.NumberFormat(loc,{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(n);}
function renderResult(){
  const key=window.__plan||'pro'; const p=PLANS[key]; const L=I18N[curLang];
  document.getElementById('wrName').textContent=L[p.name];
  document.getElementById('wrPrice').textContent=fmt(p.price);
  const ul=document.getElementById('wrList'); ul.innerHTML='';
  p.feat.forEach(f=>{const li=document.createElement('li');li.textContent=L[f];ul.appendChild(li);});
}
renderResult();

/* ============ TIMELINE ============ */
gsap.to('#tlFill',{scaleY:1,ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'#tl',start:'top 60%',end:'bottom 75%',scrub:true}});
document.querySelectorAll('.tl-item').forEach(it=>{
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:it,start:'top 72%',onEnter:()=>it.classList.add('on'),onLeaveBack:()=>it.classList.remove('on')});
});

/* ============ FAQ ACCORDION ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('#acc .acc-item').forEach(item=>{
  const q=item.querySelector('.acc-q'), a=item.querySelector('.acc-a');
  q.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const open=item.classList.contains('open');
    document.querySelectorAll('#acc .acc-item').forEach(o=>{
      if(o!==item&&o.classList.contains('open')){o.classList.remove('open');o.querySelector('.acc-q').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(reduce){o.querySelector('.acc-a').style.height='0';}else gsap.to(o.querySelector('.acc-a'),{height:0,duration:.4,ease:'power2.inOut'});}
    });
    if(open){item.classList.remove('open');q.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(reduce){a.style.height='0';}else gsap.to(a,{height:0,duration:.4,ease:'power2.inOut'});}
    else{
      item.classList.add('open');q.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
      if(reduce){a.style.height='auto';}
      else{gsap.set(a,{height:'auto'});const h=a.offsetHeight;gsap.fromTo(a,{height:0},{height:h,duration:.5,ease:'power2.inOut',onComplete:()=>a.style.height='auto'});}
    }
  });
});

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.stat .num').forEach(num=>{
  const target=+num.dataset.count; const em=num.querySelector('em'); const suf=em?em.outerHTML:'';
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:num,start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){num.innerHTML=target+suf;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:2,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){num.innerHTML=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v)+suf;}});
  }});
});

/* ============ GENERIC FADE-UP ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.team-grid .member,.testi blockquote,.contact-grid > *,.faq-grid > *').forEach(el=>{
  gsap.from(el,{y:50,opacity:0,duration:.9,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}});
});

/* ============ CONTACT FORM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('ctForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});return;}
  gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}
  gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});
});

/* ============ MAGNETIC BUTTONS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(reduce)return;const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.3,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.4,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});});
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});

document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open')});

setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

<answer>

## Qué incluye esta web (negocio #4 — asesorías / gestorías / despachos)

**Modalidad nueva: editorial corporativo sobrio.** Cuarta identidad distinta a las anteriores (ni cálido mediterráneo #1, ni taberna #2, ni brutalista #3): base **marfil/papel** + **azul tinta** + **latón**, tipografía de autoridad *Cormorant Garamond* (serif, cursivas para acento) + *Manrope*, líneas finas tipo documento oficial y numeración romana en las áreas. Marca ficticia *MONTCLAR & ASSOCIATS*.

**Sello premium mantenido**
- GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis; preloader 0→100; reveal por palabras/líneas; parallax; cursor magnético; marquee; botones magnéticos; contadores (con sufijo `+`/`%` preservado); fade-up genérico.

**Tres módulos de front-end inéditos en la serie**
1. **Acordeón de FAQ animado por altura real**: mide `scrollHeight`, anima `height` con GSAP y devuelve a `auto` al abrir (responsive); un solo panel abierto a la vez, con `aria-expanded` y rotación del `+`→`×`.
2. **Wizard de 2 pasos → plan recomendado**: paso 1 elige perfil (autónomo / sociedad / particular), paso 2 elige necesidades con *chips* multi-selección; al pulsar "Ver el plan" calcula **Básico / Profesional / Integral** con lógica determinista y muestra precio con **formato de moneda por idioma** (`Intl.NumberFormat`) + lista de prestaciones traducida. Barra de progreso 01–02–03 con estados *active/done*.
3. **Calendario fiscal en timeline con progreso de scroll**: una línea vertical cuyo relleno (`scaleY` con `scrub`) avanza según el scroll, y cada hito (modelos 303/390, Renta, Sociedades…) se **ilumina** al cruzar su umbral. Muy propio del vertical y visualmente nuevo.

**Más secciones propias del vertical**
- Hero con **barra de métricas** (año, clientes, 48 h, áreas).
- **Áreas de práctica** en grid tipo documento con numeración romana y hover.
- **Credenciales** (colegiados, confidencial, honorarios claros, proximidad).
- **Equipo** con fotos en escala de grises que pasan a color en hover.
- Stats, testimonio, **formulario de consulta** con overlay de confirmación animado, footer.

**Trilingüe real (CA / ES / EN)**
- Objeto `I18N` con **todas** las claves: preguntas/respuestas del FAQ, plazos del calendario, opciones del wizard (con `value` fijos independientes del idioma), nombres de planes y sus prestaciones, mensaje de confirmación. Arranca en catalán y cambia `html lang`; al cambiar de idioma se **re-renderiza** el resultado del wizard con la moneda y textos correctos.

**Robustez y accesibilidad**
- Imágenes de **Unsplash** vía `data-src` con `onerror → .img-fallback`; `clamp()` + *breakpoints* a 980px / 560px; respeta `prefers-reduced-motion` con *sets* en hero, acordeón, wizard, timeline y overlay; acordeón operable con `<button>` reales.

## Cómo usarla
1. Copia el bloque en `index.html` y ábrelo (necesita internet para fuentes, GSAP, Lenis e imágenes).
2. Edita textos, **precios de los planes** (`PLANS`), plazos del calendario, teléfono y dirección en el HTML **y** en `I18N` (las 3 lenguas).
3. Para producción: sustituye las URLs por fotos reales del despacho/equipo y conecta el formulario a un backend/servicio.

## Siguiente paso
Cuando me digas **`. 5`**, construiré la web completa premium para el tipo de cliente **#5 — Peluquerías, barberías y centros de estética** (con su propia modalidad de front-end e identidad visual).

</answer>
