```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ca">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>CLARA — Assessoria fiscal 100% online / Asesoría fiscal online / Online tax advisory</title>
<meta name="description" content="CLARA — La teva assessoria fiscal, laboral i comptable al núvol. Dades en temps real, quota tancada." />

<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sora:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/ScrollTrigger.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.1.13/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>

<style>
  @property --ang{syntax:'<angle>';inherits:false;initial-value:0deg}
  :root{
    --bg:#080B10;
    --bg2:#0C1118;
    --surf:rgba(255,255,255,.035);
    --surf2:rgba(255,255,255,.06);
    --line:rgba(255,255,255,.09);
    --line2:rgba(255,255,255,.16);
    --txt:#E9F0F4;
    --mut:rgba(233,240,244,.56);
    --mut2:rgba(233,240,244,.38);
    --mint:#43E0A0;
    --mint2:#7CF0C0;
    --cyan:#3FD8FF;
    --serif:'Sora',system-ui,sans-serif;
    --mono:'JetBrains Mono',ui-monospace,monospace;
    --ease:cubic-bezier(.76,0,.24,1);
  }
  *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
  html,body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--txt);font-family:var(--serif);-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;overflow-x:hidden}
  body{cursor:none}
  body::before{content:"";position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:0;pointer-events:none;background:
    radial-gradient(60% 50% at 80% -5%,rgba(67,224,160,.12),transparent 60%),
    radial-gradient(50% 40% at 0% 30%,rgba(63,216,255,.08),transparent 60%)}
  a{color:inherit;text-decoration:none}
  img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}
  ::selection{background:var(--mint);color:var(--bg)}
  .wrap{width:min(92%,1280px);margin-inline:auto;position:relative;z-index:1}
  em{font-style:normal;color:var(--mint)}
  .mono{font-family:var(--mono)}
  .eyebrow{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mint);display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem}
  .eyebrow::before{content:"";width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--mint);box-shadow:0 0 12px var(--mint)}
  .glass{background:var(--surf);border:1px solid var(--line);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(14px)}

  /* CURSOR */
  .cursor{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--mint);pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;mix-blend-mode:difference;transform:translate(-50%,-50%)}
  .cursor-ring{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:42px;height:42px;border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none;z-index:9998;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);transition:width .3s var(--ease),height .3s var(--ease),background .3s,border-color .3s}
  .cursor-ring.grow{width:80px;height:80px;background:rgba(67,224,160,.12);border-color:var(--mint)}
  @media(hover:none){body{cursor:auto}.cursor,.cursor-ring{display:none}}

  /* PRELOADER */
  #preloader{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--txt);z-index:10000;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;padding:clamp(1.4rem,4vw,3rem)}
  #preloader .top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;opacity:.7}
  #preloader .count{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(5rem,22vw,18rem);line-height:.8;background:linear-gradient(120deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
  #preloader .bar{height:2px;background:var(--line);overflow:hidden;border-radius:2px}
  #preloader .bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan))}

  /* NAV */
  header{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;z-index:900;transition:background .4s,backdrop-filter .4s}
  header.scrolled{background:rgba(8,11,16,.72);backdrop-filter:blur(16px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .nav{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:1.2rem 0}
  .logo{font-weight:800;font-size:1.4rem;letter-spacing:.04em;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.55rem}
  .logo .dot{width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:3px;background:var(--mint);box-shadow:0 0 14px var(--mint)}
  .logo span{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.2em;color:var(--mut);font-weight:500;text-transform:uppercase}
  .menu{display:flex;gap:2rem;list-style:none;font-size:.82rem;font-weight:500}
  .menu a{position:relative;padding:.3rem 0;color:var(--mut);transition:color .3s}
  .menu a:hover{color:var(--txt)}
  .menu a::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;bottom:-2px;width:0;height:2px;background:var(--mint);transition:width .4s var(--ease)}
  .menu a:hover::after{width:100%}
  .nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1.1rem}
  .lang{display:flex;gap:.2rem;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:.2rem}
  .lang button{border:0;background:transparent;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.1em;font-weight:500;padding:.35rem .55rem;border-radius:6px;cursor:none;color:var(--mut);transition:.3s}
  .lang button.active{background:var(--mint);color:var(--bg)}
  .burger{display:none;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;background:none;border:0;cursor:none}
  .burger i{width:24px;height:2px;background:var(--txt);transition:.3s}

  /* BUTTONS */
  .btn{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.6rem;padding:.9rem 1.6rem;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.04em;font-weight:600;overflow:hidden;cursor:none;border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:10px;color:var(--txt);transition:color .35s var(--ease),border-color .35s}
  .btn .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--surf2);transform:translateY(101%);transition:transform .45s var(--ease);z-index:0}
  .btn span{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .btn:hover{border-color:var(--mint)}
  .btn:hover .fill{transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid{background:var(--mint);color:var(--bg);border-color:var(--mint)}
  .btn.solid .fill{background:var(--txt);transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid:hover{color:var(--bg);border-color:var(--txt)}
  .btn.solid:hover .fill{transform:translateY(-101%)}
  .btn .ar{transition:transform .35s var(--ease)}
  .btn:hover .ar{transform:translateX(4px)}

  /* HERO */
  .hero{min-height:100vh;position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;padding:clamp(7rem,14vh,9rem) 0 clamp(3rem,7vh,5rem);overflow:hidden}
  .hero-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.15fr .85fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);align-items:center}
  .chip{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.6rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mint);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:.45rem .9rem;background:var(--surf)}
  .chip .live{width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--mint);box-shadow:0 0 10px var(--mint);animation:pulse 1.6s infinite}
  @keyframes pulse{0%,100%{opacity:1}50%{opacity:.3}}
  .hero h1{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.6rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:.98;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin-top:1.4rem}
  .hero h1 .word{display:inline-block;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:top}
  .hero h1 .word i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .hero h1 em{background:linear-gradient(120deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
  .hero p{color:var(--mut);font-size:1.02rem;line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.6rem;max-width:42ch}
  .hero-cta{display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:2.2rem}
  .hero-trust{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem;margin-top:2.4rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mut2);letter-spacing:.06em}
  .avatars{display:flex}
  .avatars i{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:50%;border:2px solid var(--bg);margin-left:-8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan));display:block}
  .avatars i:first-child{margin-left:0}

  /* dashboard mock */
  .dash{position:relative;border-radius:18px;padding:1.3rem;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,.05),rgba(255,255,255,.02));border:1px solid var(--line);box-shadow:0 40px 80px -30px rgba(0,0,0,.8)}
  .dash::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:-1px;border-radius:18px;background:conic-gradient(from 120deg,transparent,rgba(67,224,160,.4),transparent 40%);z-index:-1;filter:blur(8px);opacity:.6}
  .dash-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .dash-top .t{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut)}
  .dash-top .dots{display:flex;gap:5px}
  .dash-top .dots i{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--line2)}
  .dash-top .dots i:first-child{background:var(--mint)}
  .dash-kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:.8rem;margin-bottom:1rem}
  .kpi{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:1rem;background:rgba(255,255,255,.02)}
  .kpi .l{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut2)}
  .kpi .v{font-weight:700;font-size:1.5rem;margin-top:.4rem}
  .kpi .v small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;color:var(--mint);margin-left:.4rem}
  .dash-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.3fr .7fr;gap:.8rem}
  .panel{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:1rem;background:rgba(255,255,255,.02)}
  .panel .ph{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut2);margin-bottom:.9rem}
  .bars{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:7px;height:96px}
  .bars .b{flex:1;border-radius:4px 4px 0 0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--mint),rgba(67,224,160,.25));transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:bottom}
  .donut-wrap{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.9rem}
  .donut{width:78px;height:78px;flex:none;transform:rotate(-90deg)}
  .donut circle{fill:none;stroke-width:9}
  .donut .bg{stroke:var(--line)}
  .donut .fg{stroke:url(#dg);stroke-linecap:round}
  .donut-meta .pc{font-weight:700;font-size:1.4rem}
  .donut-meta .lb{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut2);margin-top:.2rem}
  .notif{position:absolute;right:-14px;background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:12px;padding:.8rem 1rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem;box-shadow:0 20px 40px -16px rgba(0,0,0,.8);font-size:.78rem;max-width:230px}
  .notif.n1{top:18%}
  .notif.n2{bottom:14%;right:auto;left:-18px}
  .notif .ic{width:30px;height:30px;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(67,224,160,.16);color:var(--mint);display:grid;place-items:center;flex:none;font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700}
  .notif b{font-weight:600;display:block;font-size:.78rem}
  .notif small{color:var(--mut2);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem}

  /* MARQUEE */
  .marquee{border-block:1px solid var(--line);padding:1.4rem 0;overflow:hidden;background:var(--bg2)}
  .marquee .track{display:flex;gap:3rem;white-space:nowrap;width:max-content;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut)}
  .marquee .track span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3rem}
  .marquee .track em{color:var(--mint);font-style:normal}
  .marquee .dot{width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--line2);display:inline-block}

  /* SECTION HEAD */
  .sec{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0}
  .sec-head{max-width:640px;margin-bottom:clamp(2.5rem,6vh,4rem)}
  .sec-head h2{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2rem,5.5vw,4.2rem);line-height:1;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin-top:1.1rem}
  .sec-head p{color:var(--mut);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.2rem;font-size:1rem}

  /* BENTO */
  .bento{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);grid-auto-rows:minmax(180px,auto);gap:1rem}
  .b{border-radius:18px;padding:clamp(1.4rem,2.5vw,2rem);position:relative;overflow:hidden;transition:transform .5s var(--ease),border-color .5s}
  .b:hover{transform:translateY(-4px);border-color:var(--line2)}
  .b .ic{width:46px;height:46px;border-radius:12px;background:rgba(67,224,160,.12);color:var(--mint);display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;font-size:1.1rem;margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .b h3{font-weight:700;font-size:clamp(1.2rem,2vw,1.6rem);line-height:1.1}
  .b p{color:var(--mut);font-size:.88rem;line-height:1.6;margin-top:.7rem}
  .b.big{grid-column:span 2;grid-row:span 2;background:linear-gradient(160deg,rgba(67,224,160,.1),rgba(63,216,255,.04))}
  .b.big h3{font-size:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)}
  .b.wide{grid-column:span 2}
  .b .glow{position:absolute;width:200px;height:200px;border-radius:50%;background:radial-gradient(circle,rgba(67,224,160,.25),transparent 70%);right:-60px;bottom:-60px;opacity:0;transition:opacity .5s}
  .b:hover .glow{opacity:1}
  .b .mini-bars{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:6px;height:70px;margin-top:1.4rem}
  .b .mini-bars i{flex:1;border-radius:3px 3px 0 0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--mint),rgba(67,224,160,.2));transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:bottom}

  /* CALC */
  .calc-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.4rem;align-items:stretch}
  .calc-card{border-radius:18px;padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.6rem)}
  .calc-card h3{font-weight:700;font-size:1.4rem;margin-bottom:.3rem}
  .calc-card .sub{color:var(--mut);font-size:.86rem;margin-bottom:1.8rem}
  .field{margin-bottom:1.6rem}
  .field label{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut);margin-bottom:.8rem}
  .field label b{color:var(--mint);font-size:.8rem}
  .field input[type=range]{width:100%;accent-color:var(--mint);cursor:none;height:3px}
  .field select{width:100%;background:var(--surf);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:.8rem 1rem;color:var(--txt);font-family:var(--serif);font-size:.9rem;cursor:none;appearance:none}
  .calc-out{background:linear-gradient(160deg,rgba(67,224,160,.1),rgba(255,255,255,.02));display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center}
  .calc-out .lbl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mint)}
  .calc-out .save{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,6vw,4rem);line-height:.95;margin:.6rem 0 .3rem;background:linear-gradient(120deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
  .calc-out .per{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mut);letter-spacing:.08em}
  .cmp{margin-top:1.8rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:1rem}
  .cmp .row .top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.78rem;margin-bottom:.4rem}
  .cmp .row .top span:first-child{color:var(--mut)}
  .cmp .row .top b{font-family:var(--mono)}
  .cmp .bar{height:8px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--line);overflow:hidden}
  .cmp .bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;border-radius:8px}
  .cmp .bar.bad i{background:rgba(255,120,120,.7)}
  .cmp .bar.good i{background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan))}
  .calc-out .legal{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;color:var(--mut2);margin-top:1.6rem;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:.04em}

  /* PRICING */
  .toggle{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:999px;padding:.3rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.08em}
  .toggle button{border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--mut);padding:.6rem 1.2rem;border-radius:999px;cursor:none;transition:.3s;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem}
  .toggle button.active{background:var(--mint);color:var(--bg)}
  .toggle .save-tag{font-size:.58rem;background:rgba(67,224,160,.2);color:var(--mint);padding:.15rem .45rem;border-radius:999px}
  .toggle button.active .save-tag{background:rgba(8,11,16,.2);color:var(--bg)}
  .price-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:1.2rem;align-items:stretch}
  .plan{border-radius:18px;padding:2px;position:relative}
  .plan .inner{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:clamp(1.6rem,2.5vw,2.2rem);height:100%;display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .plan.feat{background:conic-gradient(from var(--ang),var(--mint),var(--cyan),var(--mint));animation:spin 7s linear infinite}
  @keyframes spin{to{--ang:360deg}}
  .plan.feat .inner{border-color:transparent}
  .plan .tag{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mint);margin-bottom:.8rem}
  .plan h3{font-weight:700;font-size:1.5rem}
  .plan .desc{color:var(--mut);font-size:.84rem;line-height:1.5;margin-top:.5rem;min-height:2.6em}
  .plan .price{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,4vw,3rem);margin:1.4rem 0 .2rem}
  .plan .price small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;font-weight:500;color:var(--mut)}
  .plan ul{list-style:none;margin:1.4rem 0 1.8rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.7rem;flex:1}
  .plan li{font-size:.86rem;color:var(--mut);display:flex;gap:.7rem;align-items:flex-start}
  .plan li::before{content:"✓";color:var(--mint);font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:700;flex:none}
  .plan .btn{width:100%;justify-content:center}

  /* FAQ + SEARCH */
  .faq-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:.8fr 1.2fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,5rem);align-items:start}
  .faq-search{position:relative;margin-bottom:1.6rem}
  .faq-search input{width:100%;background:var(--surf);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:1rem 1rem 1rem 2.8rem;color:var(--txt);font-family:var(--serif);font-size:.92rem;outline:none;cursor:none;transition:border-color .3s}
  .faq-search input:focus{border-color:var(--mint)}
  .faq-search .si{position:absolute;left:1rem;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);color:var(--mut);font-family:var(--mono)}
  .acc-item{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:.7rem;overflow:hidden;background:var(--surf);transition:border-color .3s}
  .acc-item.open{border-color:var(--line2)}
  .acc-item.hide{display:none}
  .acc-q{width:100%;background:none;border:0;cursor:none;text-align:left;padding:1.2rem 1.3rem;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:1rem;font-weight:600;font-size:1rem;color:var(--txt)}
  .acc-q .pm{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:1.2rem;color:var(--mint);transition:transform .4s var(--ease);flex:none}
  .acc-item.open .acc-q .pm{transform:rotate(45deg)}
  .acc-a{height:0;overflow:hidden}
  .acc-a p{color:var(--mut);font-size:.9rem;line-height:1.7;padding:0 1.3rem 1.3rem}
  .faq-empty{color:var(--mut2);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.8rem;padding:1rem;display:none}

  /* TESTI CAROUSEL */
  .testi-sec{background:var(--bg2);border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
  .carousel{position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .ctrack{display:flex}
  .tslide{min-width:100%;padding:0 clamp(.5rem,2vw,2rem)}
  .tcard{border-radius:18px;padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);text-align:center;max-width:760px;margin:0 auto}
  .tcard .stars{color:var(--mint);font-family:var(--mono);letter-spacing:.2em;margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .tcard blockquote{font-weight:600;font-size:clamp(1.3rem,3vw,2.2rem);line-height:1.3}
  .tcard cite{font-style:normal;display:block;margin-top:1.6rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut)}
  .cdots{display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:.6rem;margin-top:2rem}
  .cdots button{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;border:0;background:var(--line2);cursor:none;transition:.3s;padding:0}
  .cdots button.active{background:var(--mint);width:24px;border-radius:8px}

  /* STATS */
  .stats-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:1rem}
  .stat{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:clamp(1.4rem,2.5vw,2rem);background:var(--surf);text-align:center}
  .stat .num{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,5vw,4rem);line-height:1;background:linear-gradient(120deg,var(--mint),var(--cyan));-webkit-background-clip:text;background-clip:text;color:transparent}
  .stat .lbl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut);margin-top:.7rem}

  /* CTA + FORM */
  .cta-box{border-radius:22px;padding:clamp(2.5rem,6vw,5rem);position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:linear-gradient(160deg,rgba(67,224,160,.12),rgba(63,216,255,.05));border:1px solid var(--line)}
  .cta-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);align-items:center}
  .cta-box h2{font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);line-height:1;letter-spacing:-.02em}
  .cta-box .lead{color:var(--mut);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:34ch}
  form .frow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1rem}
  .cfield{margin-bottom:1rem}
  .cfield.full{grid-column:1/-1}
  .cfield label{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut);margin-bottom:.5rem}
  .cfield input,.cfield select,.cfield textarea{width:100%;background:var(--surf);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:.8rem 1rem;color:var(--txt);font-family:var(--serif);font-size:.92rem;outline:none;cursor:none;transition:border-color .3s}
  .cfield textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:80px}
  .cfield input:focus,.cfield select:focus,.cfield textarea:focus{border-color:var(--mint)}
  .cfield select{appearance:none}

  .res-ok{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9500;background:rgba(8,11,16,.9);backdrop-filter:blur(10px);display:none;place-items:center;padding:1.5rem}
  .res-ok .card{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--line2);border-radius:18px;padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3rem);text-align:center;max-width:440px}
  .res-ok .check{width:70px;height:70px;border-radius:50%;background:rgba(67,224,160,.16);color:var(--mint);display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0 auto 1.2rem;font-size:1.8rem;font-family:var(--mono)}
  .res-ok h3{font-weight:700;font-size:1.7rem}
  .res-ok p{color:var(--mut);margin:1rem 0 1.6rem;line-height:1.6}

  /* FOOTER */
  footer{border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding:clamp(4rem,8vh,6rem) 0 2rem;background:var(--bg)}
  .foot-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.5fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2.5rem;padding-bottom:3rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .foot-brand p{color:var(--mut);margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:32ch;line-height:1.6;font-size:.9rem}
  .foot-col h4{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mint);margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .foot-col a{display:block;font-size:.9rem;padding:.35rem 0;color:var(--mut);transition:.3s}
  .foot-col a:hover{color:var(--txt);padding-left:.4rem}
  .foot-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding-top:2rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mut2);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem}

  .img-fallback{background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(67,224,160,.3),rgba(63,216,255,.2))!important}
  .img-fallback img{display:none}

  @media(max-width:980px){
    .menu{display:none}.burger{display:flex}
    .hero-grid,.calc-grid,.cta-grid,.faq-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .bento{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .b.big,.b.wide{grid-column:span 2}
    .price-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .stats-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .notif{display:none}
  }
  @media(max-width:560px){
    .bento{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .b.big,.b.wide{grid-column:span 1}
    form .frow{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .stats-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .dash-row{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  }
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute"><defs><linearGradient id="dg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#43E0A0"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#3FD8FF"/></linearGradient></defs></svg>

<div class="cursor"></div>
<div class="cursor-ring"></div>

<div id="preloader">
  <div class="top"><span>CLARA · fiscal online</span><span data-i18n="preloader">Sincronitzant dades</span></div>
  <div class="count" id="pcount">0</div>
  <div><div class="bar"><i id="pbar"></i></div></div>
</div>

<header id="header">
  <div class="wrap nav">
    <a href="#" class="logo"><span class="dot"></span>CLARA<span data-i18n="logo_sub">fiscal · online</span></a>
    <ul class="menu">
      <li><a href="#features" data-i18n="nav_feat">Funcions</a></li>
      <li><a href="#calc" data-i18n="nav_calc">Estalvi</a></li>
      <li><a href="#pricing" data-i18n="nav_price">Preus</a></li>
      <li><a href="#faq" data-i18n="nav_faq">Dubtes</a></li>
      <li><a href="#contact" data-i18n="nav_contact">Comença</a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="nav-right">
      <div class="lang">
        <button data-lang="ca" class="active">CA</button>
        <button data-lang="es">ES</button>
        <button data-lang="en">EN</button>
      </div>
      <button class="burger" aria-label="menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<section class="hero">
  <div class="wrap hero-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="chip"><span class="live"></span><span data-i18n="hero_chip">Assessoria 100% al núvol</span></span>
      <h1 id="heroTitle">
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w1">La teva</i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w2">gestoria,</i></span><br>
        <span class="word"><i><em data-i18n="hero_w3">en temps</em></i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w4">real.</i></span>
      </h1>
      <p data-i18n="hero_sub">Fiscal, comptable i laboral en una sola app. Sense papers, sense desplaçaments i amb un gestor humà darrere la pantalla quan el necessites.</p>
      <div class="hero-cta">
        <a href="#pricing" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="hero_cta1">Prova-ho 30 dies</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
        <a href="#calc" class="btn"><span data-i18n="hero_cta2">Calcula el teu estalvi</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div>
      <div class="hero-trust">
        <div class="avatars"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
        <span data-i18n="hero_trust">+3.400 autònoms i pimes ja hi confien</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="dash" id="dash">
      <div class="dash-top"><span class="t" data-i18n="dash_t">Panell · Q2 2026</span><span class="dots"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span></div>
      <div class="dash-kpis">
        <div class="kpi"><div class="l" data-i18n="k1">Facturació</div><div class="v">48.2k<small>+12%</small></div></div>
        <div class="kpi"><div class="l" data-i18n="k2">Impostos Q2</div><div class="v">6.1k<small data-i18n="k2b">previst</small></div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="dash-row">
        <div class="panel"><div class="ph" data-i18n="dash_bars">Ingressos / mes</div><div class="bars" id="dashBars"><div class="b" style="height:40%"></div><div class="b" style="height:62%"></div><div class="b" style="height:48%"></div><div class="b" style="height:78%"></div><div class="b" style="height:66%"></div><div class="b" style="height:92%"></div></div></div>
        <div class="panel"><div class="ph" data-i18n="dash_donut">Càrrega fiscal</div><div class="donut-wrap"><svg class="donut" viewBox="0 0 120 120"><circle class="bg" cx="60" cy="60" r="52"/><circle class="fg" id="donutFg" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" stroke-dasharray="326.7" stroke-dashoffset="326.7"/></svg><div class="donut-meta"><div class="pc" id="donutPc">0%</div><div class="lb" data-i18n="dash_opt">optimitzat</div></div></div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="notif n1" id="notif1"><span class="ic">✓</span><div><b data-i18n="n1_t">IVA presentat</b><small data-i18n="n1_d">Model 303 · Q2</small></div></div>
      <div class="notif n2" id="notif2"><span class="ic">!</span><div><b data-i18n="n2_t">Avís de termini</b><small data-i18n="n2_d">Renda · falten 9 dies</small></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<div class="marquee">
  <div class="track" id="mtrack">
    <span><em data-i18n="m1">Sense paper</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m2">Quota tancada</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m3">Gestor humà</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m4">Dades en viu</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m5">Signatura digital</em><i class="dot"></i></span>
  </div>
</div>

<section class="sec" id="features">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="feat_eye">Tot en un lloc</span>
      <h2 data-i18n="feat_title">Una app que fa de <em>gestoria</em></h2>
      <p data-i18n="feat_p">Connecta el teu banc, emet factures i deixa que CLARA calculi els impostos mentre tu treballes. El teu gestor revisa tot abans de presentar-ho.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="bento">
      <div class="b big glass"><span class="glow"></span><div class="ic">∑</div><h3 data-i18n="b1_t">Comptabilitat automàtica</h3><p data-i18n="b1_d">Llegim els teus moviments bancaris i els classifiquem. Tu només confirmes el que sigui dubtós. Comptes sempre al dia, sense picar dades.</p><div class="mini-bars" id="bentoBars"><i style="height:50%"></i><i style="height:70%"></i><i style="height:40%"></i><i style="height:85%"></i><i style="height:60%"></i><i style="height:95%"></i><i style="height:72%"></i></div></div>
      <div class="b glass"><span class="glow"></span><div class="ic">§</div><h3 data-i18n="b2_t">Impostos al dia</h3><p data-i18n="b2_d">IVA, IRPF i Societats calculats i presentats telemàticament, amb avís previ de cada termini.</p></div>
      <div class="b glass"><span class="glow"></span><div class="ic">€</div><h3 data-i18n="b3_t">Factures en 10 s</h3><p data-i18n="b3_d">Emet i rep factures conformes a Verifactu, amb cobrament i recordatoris automàtics.</p></div>
      <div class="b wide glass"><span class="glow"></span><div class="ic">⌘</div><h3 data-i18n="b4_t">Nòmines i contractes</h3><p data-i18n="b4_d">Altes, baixes, nòmines i comunicats a la Seguretat Social des del mateix panell, amb plantilla laboral assignada.</p></div>
      <div class="b glass"><span class="glow"></span><div class="ic">◷</div><h3 data-i18n="b5_t">Gestor humà</h3><p data-i18n="b5_d">Xat i videotrucada amb el teu gestor assignat. Resposta en menys de 4 hores laborables.</p></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="sec" id="calc" style="padding-top:0">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="calc_eye">Calculadora</span>
      <h2 data-i18n="calc_title">Quant pots <em>estalviar</em>?</h2>
      <p data-i18n="calc_p">Mou el control i compara el que pagaries sense optimitzar amb el que pagues amb CLARA.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="calc-grid">
      <div class="calc-card glass">
        <h3 data-i18n="calc_h">El teu cas</h3>
        <p class="sub" data-i18n="calc_sub">Dades orientatives per fer el càlcul a l'instant.</p>
        <div class="field">
          <label><span data-i18n="calc_rev">Facturació anual</span><b id="revOut">40.000 €</b></label>
          <input type="range" id="rev" min="12000" max="200000" step="1000" value="40000">
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label><span data-i18n="calc_reg">Règim fiscal</span></label>
          <select id="reg">
            <option value="estim" data-i18n="reg_estim">Estimació directa</option>
            <option value="modul" data-i18n="reg_modul">Mòduls</option>
            <option value="soc" data-i18n="reg_soc">Societat (SL)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label><span data-i18n="calc_ded">Despeses deduïbles</span><b id="dedOut">30%</b></label>
          <input type="range" id="ded" min="0" max="60" step="5" value="30">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="calc-card calc-out glass">
        <span class="lbl" data-i18n="calc_save_lbl">Estalvi estimat / any</span>
        <div class="save" id="saveOut">0 €</div>
        <div class="per" data-i18n="calc_per">respecte a portar-ho sense optimitzar</div>
        <div class="cmp">
          <div class="row"><div class="top"><span data-i18n="cmp_bad">Sense optimitzar</span><b id="badOut">0 €</b></div><div class="bar bad"><i id="badBar"></i></div></div>
          <div class="row"><div class="top"><span data-i18n="cmp_good">Amb CLARA</span><b id="goodOut">0 €</b></div><div class="bar good"><i id="goodBar"></i></div></div>
        </div>
        <p class="legal" data-i18n="calc_legal">Càlcul orientatiu · no constitueix assessorament formal. El número exacte, amb el teu gestor, en 24 h.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="sec" id="pricing" style="padding-top:0">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="price_eye">Preus</span>
      <h2 data-i18n="price_title">Una quota, <em>tot inclòs</em></h2>
      <p data-i18n="price_p">Sense matrícules ni extres amagats. Canvia o cancel·la quan vulguis.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="toggle" id="billToggle">
      <button class="active" data-bill="m" data-i18n="bill_m">Mensual</button>
      <button data-bill="y"><span data-i18n="bill_y">Anual</span><span class="save-tag" data-i18n="bill_save">-20%</span></button>
    </div>
    <div class="price-grid">
      <div class="plan glass"><div class="inner">
        <span class="tag" data-i18n="p1_tag">Autònom</span>
        <h3 data-i18n="p1_n">Start</h3>
        <p class="desc" data-i18n="p1_d">Per a qui comença i factura sense treballadors.</p>
        <div class="price"><span class="pval" data-m="39" data-y="31">39</span>€<small data-i18n="p_per">/mes</small></div>
        <ul><li data-i18n="p1_f1">Fiscal i comptable</li><li data-i18n="p1_f2">Facturació il·limitada</li><li data-i18n="p1_f3">App i panell web</li><li data-i18n="p1_f4">Xat amb gestor</li></ul>
        <a href="#contact" class="btn"><span data-i18n="p_cta">Comença</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div></div>
      <div class="plan feat"><div class="inner">
        <span class="tag" data-i18n="p2_tag">Més popular</span>
        <h3 data-i18n="p2_n">Pro</h3>
        <p class="desc" data-i18n="p2_d">Fiscal, comptable i laboral per a qui ja té equip.</p>
        <div class="price"><span class="pval" data-m="89" data-y="71">89</span>€<small data-i18n="p_per">/mes</small></div>
        <ul><li data-i18n="p2_f1">Tot el de Start</li><li data-i18n="p2_f2">Nòmines (fins a 3)</li><li data-i18n="p2_f3">Gestor assignat</li><li data-i18n="p2_f4">Videotrucada mensual</li><li data-i18n="p2_f5">Alertes fiscals en viu</li></ul>
        <a href="#contact" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="p_cta">Comença</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div></div>
      <div class="plan glass"><div class="inner">
        <span class="tag" data-i18n="p3_tag">Societat</span>
        <h3 data-i18n="p3_n">Business</h3>
        <p class="desc" data-i18n="p3_d">Per a SL amb volum i necessitats mercantils.</p>
        <div class="price"><span class="pval" data-m="189" data-y="151">189</span>€<small data-i18n="p_per">/mes</small></div>
        <ul><li data-i18n="p3_f1">Tot el de Pro</li><li data-i18n="p3_f2">Comptabilitat mercantil</li><li data-i18n="p3_f3">Comptes anuals i juntes</li><li data-i18n="p3_f4">Suport jurídic base</li></ul>
        <a href="#contact" class="btn"><span data-i18n="p_cta">Comença</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="sec" id="faq" style="padding-top:0">
  <div class="wrap faq-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="faq_eye">Dubtes</span>
      <h2 style="font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.6rem);line-height:1;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin-top:1.1rem" data-i18n="faq_title">Preguntes <em>freqüents</em></h2>
      <p style="color:var(--mut);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.2rem" data-i18n="faq_lead">Busca pel teu dubte o escriu-nos: la primera consulta és gratis.</p>
    </div>
    <div>
      <div class="faq-search"><span class="si">⌕</span><input type="text" id="faqSearch" data-i18n-ph="faq_ph" placeholder="Cerca una pregunta…"></div>
      <div id="acc">
        <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q1">És realment 100% online?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a1">Sí. Alta, signatura, documents i comunicació amb el teu gestor, tot des de l'app o el web. Si vols, també pots reservar una videotrucada.</p></div></div>
        <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q2">Les meves dades estan segures?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a2">Xifratge de punta a punta, servidors a la UE i compliment del RGPD. Les teves dades fiscals mai es comparteixen amb tercers.</p></div></div>
        <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q3">Puc venir d'una altra gestoria?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a3">I tant. Nosaltres gestionem el traspàs d'expedient i t'acompanyem perquè no es perdi cap termini durant el canvi, sense cost addicional.</p></div></div>
        <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q4">Què passa si tinc una inspecció?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a4">El teu gestor ho assumeix: prepara la documentació, presenta al·legacions i et representa davant de l'administració dins del teu pla.</p></div></div>
        <div class="acc-item"><button class="acc-q" aria-expanded="false"><span data-i18n="q5">Hi ha permanència?</span><span class="pm">+</span></button><div class="acc-a"><p data-i18n="a5">Cap. Pagues per mes o per any amb descompte, i pots cancel·lar quan vulguis des del panell, sense lletres petites.</p></div></div>
      </div>
      <p class="faq-empty" id="faqEmpty" data-i18n="faq_empty">Cap resultat. Prova amb una altra paraula.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="sec" id="contact" style="padding-top:0">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="cta-box">
      <div class="cta-grid">
        <div>
          <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="ct_eye">Comença avui</span>
          <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="ct_title">30 dies <em>gratis</em>, sense targeta</h2>
          <p class="lead" data-i18n="ct_lead">Obre el teu compte en 2 minuts i connecta el teu banc. El teu gestor et truca el primer dia per posar-ho tot en ordre.</p>
        </div>
        <form id="ctForm" novalidate>
          <div class="frow">
            <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_name">Nom</label><input type="text" required></div>
            <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_email">Correu</label><input type="email" required></div>
          </div>
          <div class="frow">
            <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_phone">Telèfon</label><input type="tel" required></div>
            <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_kind">Perfil</label>
              <select required>
                <option value="" data-i18n="f_kind_ph">Tria</option>
                <option data-i18n="fk_auto">Autònom</option>
                <option data-i18n="fk_soc">Societat</option>
                <option data-i18n="fk_new">Encara no he començat</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button type="submit" class="btn solid" style="width:100%;justify-content:center"><span data-i18n="f_submit">Crea el meu compte</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

};
let curLang='ca', curBill='m';
function setLang(lang){
  curLang=lang;
  document.documentElement.lang=lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.getAttribute('data-i18n');if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined)el.innerHTML=I18N[lang][k];});
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n-ph]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.getAttribute('data-i18n-ph');if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined)el.placeholder=I18N[lang][k];});
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
  calc(); applyBill();
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ LAZY IMG + FALLBACK ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(img=>{const s=img.getAttribute('data-src');img.onerror=()=>img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');img.src=s;});

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur=document.querySelector('.cursor'),ring=document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px';});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.18;ry+=(my-ry)*.18;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop);})();
document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.b,.plan,.acc-q,.tslide').forEach(el=>{el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>ring.classList.add('grow'));el.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>ring.classList.remove('grow'));});

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis=new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{const id=a.getAttribute('href');if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}}));

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'),pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
const tl=gsap.timeline();
tl.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%';}})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.2')
.add(()=>{heroIn();dashIn();});

/* ============ HERO IN ============ */
function heroIn(){
  if(reduce){gsap.set('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0});gsap.set('.hero .chip,.hero p,.hero-cta,.hero-trust',{y:0,opacity:1});return;}
  gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.09});
  gsap.from('.hero .chip,.hero p,.hero-cta,.hero-trust',{y:26,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.3});
}
function dashIn(){
  if(reduce){gsap.set('#dashBars .b',{scaleY:1});gsap.set('#bentoBars i',{scaleY:1});document.getElementById('donutFg').style.strokeDashoffset=326.7*(1-0.72);document.getElementById('donutPc').textContent='72%';gsap.set('.notif',{opacity:1,x:0});return;}
  gsap.from('#dash',{y:40,opacity:0,duration:1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.2});
  gsap.to('#dashBars .b',{scaleY:1,duration:.9,stagger:.08,ease:'power3.out',delay:.6});
  const fg=document.getElementById('donutFg'),pc=document.getElementById('donutPc'),C=326.7,target=0.72;
  gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:1.4,ease:'power2.out',delay:.8,onUpdate:function(){const p=this.targets()[0].v;fg.style.strokeDashoffset=C*(1-p);pc.textContent=Math.round(p*100)+'%';}});
  gsap.from('#notif1',{x:40,opacity:0,duration:.7,ease:'back.out(1.6)',delay:1.3});
  gsap.from('#notif2',{x:-40,opacity:0,duration:.7,ease:'back.out(1.6)',delay:1.6});
}

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');mtrack.innerHTML+=mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:24,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ BENTO ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.b').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{y:46,opacity:0,duration:.8,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}}));
ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:'#bentoBars',start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{if(reduce){gsap.set('#bentoBars i',{scaleY:1});return;}gsap.to('#bentoBars i',{scaleY:1,duration:.8,stagger:.07,ease:'power3.out'});}});

/* ============ CALCULATOR ============ */
const rev=document.getElementById('rev'),reg=document.getElementById('reg'),ded=document.getElementById('ded'),revOut=document.getElementById('revOut'),dedOut=document.getElementById('dedOut'),saveOut=document.getElementById('saveOut'),badOut=document.getElementById('badOut'),goodOut=document.getElementById('goodOut'),badBar=document.getElementById('badBar'),goodBar=document.getElementById('goodBar');
const RATE={estim:0.21,modul:0.07,soc:0.23};
function fmt(n){const loc=curLang==='en'?'en-IE':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES';return new Intl.NumberFormat(loc,{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(n);}
function calc(){
  const r=+rev.value, d=+ded.value/100, rate=RATE[reg.value];
  revOut.textContent=fmt(r); dedOut.textContent=(d*100)+'%';
  const base=r*(1-d);
  const bad=Math.round(base*rate*1.18);      // sin optimizar: tipo efectivo mayor
  const good=Math.round(base*rate*0.82);     // con CLARA: optimización
  const save=Math.max(0,bad-good);
  badOut.textContent=fmt(bad); goodOut.textContent=fmt(good);
  if(reduce){saveOut.textContent=fmt(save);badBar.style.width='100%';goodBar.style.width=Math.round(good/bad*100)+'%';return;}
  gsap.to({v:0},{v:save,duration:.9,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto',onUpdate:function(){saveOut.textContent=fmt(Math.round(this.targets()[0].v));}});
  gsap.to(badBar,{width:'100%',duration:.9,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto'});
  gsap.to(goodBar,{width:Math.max(6,good/bad*100)+'%',duration:.9,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto'});
}
[rev,ded].forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('input',calc));
reg.addEventListener('change',calc);
calc();

/* ============ PRICING TOGGLE ============ */
const billBtns=[...document.querySelectorAll('#billToggle button')];
function applyBill(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.pval').forEach(p=>{
    const val=+p.dataset[curBill];
    if(reduce){p.textContent=val;return;}
    gsap.to({v:+p.textContent.replace(/\D/g,'')||val},{v:val,duration:.6,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto',onUpdate:function(){p.textContent=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);}});
  });
}
billBtns.forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{billBtns.forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));curBill=b.dataset.bill;applyBill();}));

/* ============ FAQ SEARCH + ACCORDION ============ */
const faqItems=[...document.querySelectorAll('#acc .acc-item')],faqEmpty=document.getElementById('faqEmpty');
document.getElementById('faqSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const q=e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();let shown=0;
  faqItems.forEach(it=>{const match=it.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);it.classList.toggle('hide',!match);if(match)shown++;});
  faqEmpty.style.display=shown?'none':'block';
});
faqItems.forEach(item=>{
  const q=item.querySelector('.acc-q'),a=item.querySelector('.acc-a');
  q.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const open=item.classList.contains('open');
    faqItems.forEach(o=>{if(o!==item&&o.classList.contains('open')){o.classList.remove('open');o.querySelector('.acc-q').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(reduce)o.querySelector('.acc-a').style.height='0';else gsap.to(o.querySelector('.acc-a'),{height:0,duration:.4,ease:'power2.inOut'});}});
    if(open){item.classList.remove('open');q.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');if(reduce)a.style.height='0';else gsap.to(a,{height:0,duration:.4,ease:'power2.inOut'});}
    else{item.classList.add('open');q.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');if(reduce){a.style.height='auto';}else{gsap.set(a,{height:'auto'});const h=a.offsetHeight;gsap.fromTo(a,{height:0},{height:h,duration:.5,ease:'power2.inOut',onComplete:()=>a.style.height='auto';});}}
  });
});

/* ============ TESTIMONIAL CAROUSEL ============ */
const ctrack=document.getElementById('ctrack'),slides=[...ctrack.children],cdots=document.getElementById('cdots');
let ci=0,ctween;
slides.forEach((_,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');if(i===0)b.classList.add('active');b.addEventListener('click',()=>goC(i,true));cdots.appendChild(b);});
function goC(i,manual){
  ci=i;
  if(reduce){ctrack.style.transform=`translateX(-${i*100}%)`;}
  else{gsap.to(ctrack,{xPercent:-i*100,duration:.7,ease:'power3.inOut'});}
  [...cdots.children].forEach((d,j)=>d.classList.toggle('active',j===i));
  if(manual)restartC();
}
function nextC(){goC((ci+1)%slides.length);}
function restartC(){gsap.killTweensOf({});clearInterval(window.__cint);window.__cint=setInterval(nextC,5000);}
restartC();
const car=document.getElementById('carousel');
car.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>clearInterval(window.__cint));
car.addEventListener('mouseleave',restartC);

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.stat .num').forEach(num=>{
  const target=+num.dataset.count;const suf=num.querySelector('em,span');const sHtml=suf?suf.outerHTML:'';
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:num,start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){num.innerHTML=target+sHtml;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:2,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){num.innerHTML=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v)+sHtml;}});
  }});
});

/* ============ GENERIC FADE-UP ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.stat,.cta-box').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{y:50,opacity:0,duration:.9,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}}));

/* ============ CONTACT FORM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('ctForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});return;}
  gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});
  gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});});

/* ============ MAGNETIC BUTTONS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(reduce)return;const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.25,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.35,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});});
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});

document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open');});

setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

<answer>

## Qué es esta entrega

Una **segunda modalidad del mismo vertical #4** (asesoría / gestoría / despacho), ahora en clave **fintech / asesoría 100% online** con la marca *CLARA*. Es el A/B opuesto a la versión sobria anterior: mismo negocio, ejecución radicalmente distinta para que el cliente elija estilo.

**Identidad visual (toda nueva)**
- **Modo oscuro** con *glows* radiales, **glassmorphism** (`backdrop-filter` + bordes sutiles), acento **verde-menta + cian** en degradado.
- Tipografía geométrica **Sora** (titulares 800) + **JetBrains Mono** para cifras, etiquetas y el aire "data/producto".
- Borde cónico rotando en el plan destacado vía `@property --ang` (con degradación elegante si el navegador no lo soporta).

**Sello premium mantenido**
- GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis; preloader 0→100 con número en degradado; reveal por palabras; cursor magnético; marquee; botones magnéticos; contadores con sufijo preservado.

**Seis módulos de front-end nuevos (ninguno de la versión sobria)**
1. **Dashboard mock animado** en el hero: KPIs, **bar-chart** que crece (`scaleY`), **donut SVG** con `stroke-dashoffset` animado vía `onUpdate`, y dos **notificaciones** que entran con `back.out`.
2. **Bento grid** de funciones con *glass cards*, glow en hover y un mini bar-chart animado en la tarjeta grande.
3. **Calculadora de ahorro** con 2 sliders + select: recalcula al instante y anima **dos barras comparativas** ("sin optimizar" vs "con CLARA") por ancho, más el ahorro con formato de moneda por idioma.
4. **Pricing con toggle mensual/anual**: el precio **cuenta** al cambiar (`data-m`/`data-y`) y muestra tag de -20%.
5. **FAQ con buscador en vivo** que filtra preguntas por texto + acordeón por altura real y mensaje "sin resultados".
6. **Carrusel de testimonios** con autoplay (5 s), pausa en hover y dots clicables.

**Trilingüe real (CA / ES / EN)**
- `I18N` con **todas** las claves: funciones del bento, planes y prestaciones, preguntas/respuestas, testimonios, labels del dashboard y la calculadora, placeholders. Al cambiar de idioma se **recalculan** la calculadora y los precios con la moneda correcta (`Intl.NumberFormat`).

**Robustez y accesibilidad**
- `data-src` + `onerror → .img-fallback`; `clamp()` + breakpoints a 980px / 560px; `prefers-reduced-motion` con *sets* en hero, dashboard, bento, calculadora, acordeón, carrusel, stats y overlay; acordeón y carrusel con `<button>` reales y `aria-expanded`.

## Cómo usarla
1. Copia el bloque en `index.html` y ábrelo (necesita internet para fuentes, GSAP, Lenis).
2. Edita textos, **precios** (`data-m`/`data-y`), **tasas de la calculadora** (`RATE` y los multiplicadores de `calc()`), teléfono y correo en el HTML **y** en `I18N` (las 3 lenguas).
3. Para producción: conecta el formulario a un backend/servicio y añade tu logo real.

## Nota sobre tu petición y siguiente paso
He interpretado «otra modalidad» (sin número) como **rehacer el #4 con un estilo distinto**, que es lo que aporta más valor como variante A/B del mismo cliente. Por eso ahora tienes **dos** webs premium del vertical de asesorías: la *editorial sobria* (MONTCLAR) y esta *fintech online* (CLARA).

- Si lo que querías era **avanzar al siguiente cliente** de la lista, dime **`. 5`** y construyo el **#5 — Peluquerías, barberías y centros de estética** con su propia modalidad.
- Si prefieres **otra modalidad más** de cualquier cliente ya hecho, dímelo con el número (p. ej. «otra modalidad 2»).

</answer>
