```html
<html lang="ca">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
<title>MAISON COLETTE — Saló i barberia / Salón y barbería / Salon & barbershop</title>
<meta name="description" content="MAISON COLETTE — Saló de perruqueria, barberia i estètica. Reserva online amb el teu estilista." />

<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Playfair+Display:ital,wght@0,400;0,500;0,600;1,400;1,500;1,600&family=Jost:wght@300;400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/gsap.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/gsap/3.12.5/ScrollTrigger.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://unpkg.com/lenis@1.1.13/dist/lenis.min.js"></script>

<style>
  :root{
    --nude:#F2EBE4;
    --nude2:#E8DDD2;
    --ink:#1A1715;
    --ink2:#2A2420;
    --wine:#7E2B3A;
    --wine2:#9C3A4B;
    --gold:#B98A4E;
    --mut-l:#6E6258;
    --mut-d:rgba(242,235,228,.6);
    --line:rgba(26,23,21,.14);
    --line-d:rgba(242,235,228,.16);
    --serif:'Playfair Display',Georgia,serif;
    --sans:'Jost',system-ui,sans-serif;
    --ease:cubic-bezier(.76,0,.24,1);
  }
  *{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
  html,body{background:var(--nude);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--sans);font-weight:300;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;overflow-x:hidden}
  body{cursor:none}
  a{color:inherit;text-decoration:none}
  img{display:block;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}
  ::selection{background:var(--wine);color:var(--nude)}
  .wrap{width:min(92%,1320px);margin-inline:auto}
  em{font-style:italic;color:var(--wine)}
  .serif{font-family:var(--serif)}
  .eyebrow{font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.34em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wine);font-weight:500;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.8rem}
  .eyebrow::before{content:"";width:30px;height:1px;background:var(--wine)}
  .eyebrow.inv{color:var(--gold)}.eyebrow.inv::before{background:var(--gold)}

  /* CURSOR */
  .cursor{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--wine2);pointer-events:none;z-index:9999;mix-blend-mode:difference;transform:translate(-50%,-50%)}
  .cursor-ring{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:44px;height:44px;border:1px solid var(--ink);border-radius:50%;pointer-events:none;z-index:9998;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);transition:width .3s var(--ease),height .3s var(--ease),background .3s,border-color .3s}
  .cursor-ring.grow{width:84px;height:84px;background:rgba(126,43,58,.1);border-color:var(--wine)}
  @media(hover:none){body{cursor:auto}.cursor,.cursor-ring{display:none}}

  /* PRELOADER */
  #preloader{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);z-index:10000;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;padding:clamp(1.4rem,4vw,3rem)}
  #preloader .top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-size:.68rem;letter-spacing:.32em;text-transform:uppercase;opacity:.7}
  #preloader .count{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:clamp(5rem,22vw,18rem);line-height:.8;color:var(--gold)}
  #preloader .bar{height:1px;background:rgba(242,235,228,.18);overflow:hidden}
  #preloader .bar i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:var(--gold)}

  /* NAV */
  header{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100%;z-index:900;transition:background .4s,backdrop-filter .4s}
  header.scrolled{background:rgba(242,235,228,.86);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .nav{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:1.3rem 0}
  .logo{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:.04em;display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1}
  .logo span{font-size:.54rem;letter-spacing:.34em;color:var(--wine);font-family:var(--sans);font-weight:500;margin-top:.35rem;text-transform:uppercase}
  .menu{display:flex;gap:2.1rem;list-style:none;font-size:.78rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:400}
  .menu a{position:relative;padding:.3rem 0}
  .menu a::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;bottom:0;width:0;height:1px;background:var(--wine);transition:width .4s var(--ease)}
  .menu a:hover::after{width:100%}
  .nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1.2rem}
  .open-now{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500}
  .open-now .led{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%}
  .open-now.on .led{background:#3fae6b;box-shadow:0 0 10px #3fae6b;animation:pulse 1.6s infinite}
  .open-now.off .led{background:var(--wine)}
  @keyframes pulse{0%,100%{opacity:1}50%{opacity:.35}}
  .lang{display:flex;gap:.2rem;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:2px;padding:.2rem}
  .lang button{border:0;background:transparent;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.64rem;letter-spacing:.14em;font-weight:500;padding:.35rem .6rem;cursor:none;color:var(--mut-l);transition:.3s}
  .lang button.active{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude)}
  .burger{display:none;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;background:none;border:0;cursor:none}
  .burger i{width:26px;height:1px;background:var(--ink);transition:.3s}

  /* BUTTONS */
  .btn{position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem;padding:.95rem 1.8rem;font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;overflow:hidden;cursor:none;border:1px solid var(--ink);transition:color .4s var(--ease)}
  .btn .fill{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--ink);transform:translateY(101%);transition:transform .5s var(--ease);z-index:0}
  .btn span{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .btn:hover{color:var(--nude)}
  .btn:hover .fill{transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid{background:var(--wine);color:var(--nude);border-color:var(--wine)}
  .btn.solid .fill{background:var(--ink);transform:translateY(0)}
  .btn.solid:hover{color:var(--nude)}
  .btn.solid:hover .fill{transform:translateY(-101%)}
  .btn.inv{border-color:var(--nude);color:var(--nude)}
  .btn.inv .fill{background:var(--gold)}
  .btn.inv:hover{color:var(--ink)}

  /* HERO */
  .hero{min-height:100vh;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .hero-txt{display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;padding:clamp(7rem,12vh,9rem) clamp(1.5rem,5vw,5rem) clamp(3rem,6vh,5rem) clamp(1.5rem,5vw,5rem)}
  .hero-txt .eyebrow{margin-bottom:1.6rem}
  .hero h1{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(2.8rem,7vw,7rem);line-height:.96;letter-spacing:-.01em}
  .hero h1 .word{display:inline-block;overflow:hidden;vertical-align:top}
  .hero h1 .word i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .hero h1 em{font-style:italic;color:var(--wine)}
  .hero-txt p{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:1rem;line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.8rem;max-width:34ch}
  .hero-cta{display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:2.2rem}
  .hero-media{position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .hero-media img{transform:scale(1.12)}
  .hero-media::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--nude) 0%,transparent 22%)}
  .hero-badge{position:absolute;bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,4rem);right:clamp(1.5rem,4vw,3rem);z-index:2;background:var(--nude);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:50%;width:clamp(120px,12vw,160px);aspect-ratio:1;display:grid;place-items:center;text-align:center;font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;line-height:1.5}
  .hero-badge b{display:block;font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:1.7rem;letter-spacing:0;color:var(--wine);text-transform:none}
  .scroll-ind{position:absolute;bottom:1.4rem;left:clamp(1.5rem,5vw,5rem);z-index:3;font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.3em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-l);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem}
  .scroll-ind i{width:38px;height:1px;background:var(--mut-l);animation:slide 1.8s var(--ease) infinite}
  @keyframes slide{0%{transform:scaleX(0);transform-origin:left}50%{transform:scaleX(1);transform-origin:left}51%{transform-origin:right}100%{transform:scaleX(0);transform-origin:right}}

  /* MARQUEE */
  .marquee{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);padding:1.3rem 0;overflow:hidden}
  .marquee .track{display:flex;gap:3rem;white-space:nowrap;width:max-content;font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:clamp(1.3rem,3vw,2.2rem)}
  .marquee .track span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3rem}
  .marquee .track em{color:var(--gold);font-style:normal;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.24em;text-transform:uppercase}
  .marquee .dot{width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--wine);display:inline-block}

  /* ABOUT */
  .about{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .about-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:.9fr 1.1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:center}
  .about .media{position:relative;aspect-ratio:4/5;overflow:hidden}
  .about .media .reveal-mask{position:absolute;inset:0;background:var(--wine);transform-origin:left;z-index:2}
  .about h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4.4rem);line-height:1.04}
  .about h2 .ln{display:block;overflow:hidden}
  .about h2 .ln i{display:inline-block;font-style:normal;transform:translateY(110%)}
  .about .creds{margin-top:2.2rem;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.4rem 2rem}
  .about .creds div{border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:1rem}
  .about .creds b{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.25rem;font-weight:500;display:block}
  .about .creds span{font-size:.8rem;color:var(--mut-l);line-height:1.5;display:block;margin-top:.3rem}

  /* SERVICES LOOKBOOK */
  .services{background:var(--nude2);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0;border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
  .sec-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:2rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2.5rem,6vh,4rem);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .sec-head h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);line-height:1}
  .sec-head .note{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.82rem;letter-spacing:.06em;max-width:26ch;text-align:right;line-height:1.6}
  .srv-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:clamp(1.2rem,2.5vw,2rem)}
  .srv{position:relative;aspect-ratio:3/4;overflow:hidden;cursor:none}
  .srv img{transition:transform 1.2s var(--ease)}
  .srv:hover img{transform:scale(1.07)}
  .srv::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,transparent 35%,rgba(26,23,21,.85));transition:opacity .5s}
  .srv .info{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;padding:1.6rem;z-index:2;color:var(--nude)}
  .srv .info .cat{font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--gold)}
  .srv .info h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:500;margin-top:.4rem}
  .srv .meta{display:flex;gap:1.4rem;margin-top:.8rem;font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.06em;opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px);transition:.5s var(--ease)}
  .srv:hover .meta{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}
  .srv .meta .dur{color:var(--mut-d)}
  .srv .meta .pr{color:var(--gold);font-weight:500}

  /* BOOKING */
  .booking{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .book{max-width:980px;margin:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem) auto 0;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--nude);position:relative}
  .bbar{display:flex;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .bbar .bs{flex:1;padding:1.1rem 1.2rem;font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;color:var(--mut-l);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.7rem;border-right:1px solid var(--line)}
  .bbar .bs:last-child{border-right:0}
  .bbar .bs .n{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:1.1rem;color:var(--gold)}
  .bbar .bs.active{color:var(--ink);background:var(--nude2)}
  .bbar .bs.done{color:var(--ink)}
  .bbody{padding:clamp(1.8rem,4vw,3rem);min-height:340px}
  .bstep{display:none}
  .bstep h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3vw,2.2rem);font-weight:500;margin-bottom:.3rem}
  .bstep .sub{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.88rem;margin-bottom:1.8rem}
  .opt-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:.9rem}
  .opt{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:1.2rem 1.3rem;cursor:none;transition:.3s var(--ease);background:var(--nude);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:1rem}
  .opt:hover{border-color:var(--wine)}
  .opt.sel{border-color:var(--ink);background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude)}
  .opt .nm{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.15rem;font-weight:500}
  .opt .d{font-size:.72rem;color:var(--mut-l);margin-top:.2rem}
  .opt.sel .d{color:var(--mut-d)}
  .opt .pr{font-size:.82rem;letter-spacing:.06em;color:var(--wine);white-space:nowrap}
  .opt.sel .pr{color:var(--gold)}
  /* stylist cards */
  .sty-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:1rem}
  .sty{border:1px solid var(--line);cursor:none;transition:.3s var(--ease);text-align:center;background:var(--nude);position:relative}
  .sty:hover{border-color:var(--wine)}
  .sty.sel{border-color:var(--ink);box-shadow:0 0 0 1px var(--ink)}
  .sty .ph{aspect-ratio:1;overflow:hidden;filter:grayscale(1);transition:filter .5s}
  .sty:hover .ph,.sty.sel .ph{filter:grayscale(0)}
  .sty .nm{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:500;padding:.8rem .5rem 0}
  .sty .rl{font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--wine);padding:0 .5rem .9rem}
  .sty .avail{position:absolute;top:.6rem;right:.6rem;font-size:.54rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;padding:.25rem .5rem;border-radius:999px}
  .sty .avail.yes{background:rgba(63,174,107,.16);color:#2c8a52}
  .sty .avail.no{background:rgba(126,43,58,.12);color:var(--wine)}
  /* date + slots */
  .dates{display:flex;gap:.6rem;overflow-x:auto;padding-bottom:.4rem;margin-bottom:1.6rem}
  .date{flex:none;min-width:74px;border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.8rem .5rem;text-align:center;cursor:none;transition:.3s;background:var(--nude)}
  .date:hover{border-color:var(--wine)}
  .date.sel{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);border-color:var(--ink)}
  .date.closed{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed}
  .date .wd{font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase}
  .date .dn{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:500;line-height:1;margin:.2rem 0}
  .date .mo{font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-l)}
  .date.sel .mo{color:var(--mut-d)}
  .slots{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(6,1fr);gap:.5rem}
  .slot{border:1px solid var(--line);padding:.7rem 0;text-align:center;font-size:.82rem;letter-spacing:.04em;cursor:none;transition:.25s;background:var(--nude)}
  .slot:hover:not(.taken){border-color:var(--wine)}
  .slot.sel{background:var(--wine);color:var(--nude);border-color:var(--wine)}
  .slot.taken{opacity:.35;cursor:not-allowed;text-decoration:line-through}
  .slots-empty{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.86rem;padding:1rem 0}
  /* form step */
  form .frow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.1rem}
  .cfield{margin-bottom:1.2rem}
  .cfield.full{grid-column:1/-1}
  .cfield label{display:block;font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;color:var(--mut-l);margin-bottom:.5rem}
  .cfield input,.cfield textarea{width:100%;background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:.6rem 0;font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.92rem;color:var(--ink);outline:none;cursor:none;transition:border-color .3s}
  .cfield textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:60px}
  .cfield input:focus,.cfield textarea:focus{border-color:var(--wine)}
  .summary{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--nude2);padding:1.4rem 1.6rem;margin-bottom:1.6rem}
  .summary .sr{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:.5rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:.86rem}
  .summary .sr:last-child{border-bottom:0}
  .summary .sr span{color:var(--mut-l)}
  .summary .sr b{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:500}
  .bnav{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:2rem;gap:1rem}

  .res-ok{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9500;background:rgba(26,23,21,.9);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);display:none;place-items:center;padding:1.5rem}
  .res-ok .card{background:var(--nude);color:var(--ink);padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);text-align:center;max-width:460px}
  .res-ok .check{width:74px;height:74px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--wine);color:var(--nude);display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0 auto 1.4rem;font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:2rem}
  .res-ok h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.9rem;font-weight:500}
  .res-ok p{color:var(--mut-l);margin:1rem 0 1.6rem;line-height:1.6}
  .res-ok .mini{text-align:left;border:1px solid var(--line);padding:1rem 1.2rem;margin-bottom:1.6rem;font-size:.84rem}
  .res-ok .mini div{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:.3rem 0}
  .res-ok .mini div span{color:var(--mut-l)}

  /* TEAM */
  .team{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .team .sec-head h2{color:var(--nude)}
  .team .sec-head .note{color:var(--mut-d)}
  .team-bar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .team-bar .lbl{font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .avail-toggle{display:inline-flex;border:1px solid var(--line-d);border-radius:999px;padding:.25rem}
  .avail-toggle button{border:0;background:transparent;color:var(--mut-d);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;padding:.5rem 1rem;border-radius:999px;cursor:none;transition:.3s}
  .avail-toggle button.active{background:var(--gold);color:var(--ink)}
  .team-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:clamp(1.2rem,2.5vw,2rem)}
  .member{cursor:none;transition:opacity .4s}
  .member.dim{opacity:.28}
  .member .ph{aspect-ratio:3/4;overflow:hidden;position:relative}
  .member .ph img{filter:grayscale(1);transition:filter .6s var(--ease),transform 1s var(--ease)}
  .member:hover .ph img{filter:grayscale(0);transform:scale(1.05)}
  .member .badge{position:absolute;top:.8rem;left:.8rem;font-size:.54rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;padding:.25rem .55rem;border-radius:999px}
  .member .badge.yes{background:rgba(63,174,107,.9);color:#06281a}
  .member .badge.no{background:rgba(26,23,21,.7);color:var(--mut-d)}
  .member h3{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:500;margin-top:1rem}
  .member .role{font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--gold);margin-top:.3rem}
  .member .book-me{margin-top:.9rem;font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--nude);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d);display:inline-block;padding-bottom:.2rem;opacity:0;transition:.4s}
  .member:hover .book-me{opacity:1}

  /* LOOKBOOK MASONRY */
  .lookbook{padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0}
  .masonry{columns:3;column-gap:clamp(1rem,2vw,1.6rem)}
  .mitem{break-inside:avoid;margin-bottom:clamp(1rem,2vw,1.6rem);position:relative;overflow:hidden;cursor:none}
  .mitem img{transition:transform 1.1s var(--ease)}
  .mitem:hover img{transform:scale(1.06)}
  .mitem .cap{position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,transparent 55%,rgba(26,23,21,.8));opacity:0;transition:.4s;display:flex;align-items:flex-end;padding:1.3rem;color:var(--nude)}
  .mitem:hover .cap{opacity:1}
  .mitem .cap .c{font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--gold)}
  .mitem .cap h4{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.2rem;font-weight:500;margin-top:.3rem}

  /* LIGHTBOX */
  .lightbox{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9600;background:rgba(26,23,21,.95);display:none;place-items:center;padding:2rem}
  .lightbox .lb-img{max-width:min(90vw,900px);max-height:80vh;object-fit:contain;border:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .lightbox .lb-cap{position:absolute;bottom:2rem;left:0;right:0;text-align:center;color:var(--nude)}
  .lightbox .lb-cap .c{font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--gold)}
  .lightbox .lb-cap h4{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:500;margin-top:.3rem}
  .lb-btn{position:absolute;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);width:52px;height:52px;border-radius:50%;border:1px solid var(--line-d);background:transparent;color:var(--nude);font-size:1.3rem;cursor:none;transition:.3s}
  .lb-btn:hover{background:var(--wine);border-color:var(--wine)}
  .lb-prev{left:2rem}.lb-next{right:2rem}
  .lb-close{position:absolute;top:1.6rem;right:1.8rem;background:none;border:0;color:var(--nude);font-size:1.6rem;cursor:none}

  /* HOURS */
  .hours{background:var(--nude2);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,10rem) 0;border-block:1px solid var(--line)}
  .hours-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,6vw,6rem);align-items:center}
  .hours h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);line-height:1}
  .hours .nowbox{margin-top:1.8rem;border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--nude);padding:1.4rem 1.6rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem}
  .hours .nowbox .led{width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:50%;flex:none}
  .hours .nowbox.on .led{background:#3fae6b;box-shadow:0 0 12px #3fae6b}
  .hours .nowbox.off .led{background:var(--wine)}
  .hours .nowbox .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:500}
  .hours .nowbox .s{font-size:.74rem;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mut-l);text-transform:uppercase}
  .hlist{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .hrow{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:baseline;padding:1.1rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .hrow:last-child{border-bottom:0}
  .hrow .d{font-size:.92rem}
  .hrow.today .d{color:var(--wine);font-weight:500}
  .hrow .t{font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.15rem}
  .hrow .t.closed{color:var(--mut-l);font-style:italic}

  /* STATS */
  .stats{padding:clamp(4rem,9vh,7rem) 0}
  .stats-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:2rem;text-align:center}
  .stat .num{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:clamp(3rem,7vw,6rem);line-height:1;color:var(--ink)}
  .stat .num em{color:var(--wine);font-style:normal}
  .stat .lbl{font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:.8rem;color:var(--mut-l)}

  /* TESTI */
  .testi{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);padding:clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem) 0;text-align:center}
  .testi blockquote{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(1.6rem,4vw,3.2rem);line-height:1.25;max-width:20ch;margin:2rem auto}
  .testi cite{font-style:normal;font-size:.76rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}

  /* CTA */
  .cta{padding:0 0 clamp(5rem,12vh,9rem)}
  .cta-box{background:var(--wine);color:var(--nude);padding:clamp(3rem,8vw,7rem);text-align:center;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .cta-box h2{font-family:var(--serif);font-weight:400;font-size:clamp(2.4rem,8vw,7rem);line-height:.95}
  .cta-box h2 em{font-style:italic;color:var(--gold)}
  .cta-box p{color:var(--mut-d);max-width:42ch;margin:1.6rem auto 2.4rem;line-height:1.6}

  /* FOOTER */
  footer{background:var(--ink);color:var(--nude);padding:clamp(4rem,8vh,6rem) 0 2rem}
  .foot-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.5fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2.5rem;padding-bottom:3rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .foot-brand .logo{color:var(--nude)}
  .foot-brand p{color:var(--mut-d);margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:32ch;line-height:1.6;font-size:.9rem}
  .foot-col h4{font-size:.66rem;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--gold);margin-bottom:1.2rem;font-weight:500}
  .foot-col a{display:block;font-size:.9rem;padding:.35rem 0;color:rgba(242,235,228,.75);transition:.3s}
  .foot-col a:hover{color:var(--nude);padding-left:.4rem}
  .foot-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding-top:2rem;font-size:.72rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mut-d);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem}

  .img-fallback{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--ink2),var(--wine))!important}
  .img-fallback img{display:none}

  @media(max-width:980px){
    .menu{display:none}.burger{display:flex}
    .hero{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .hero-media{min-height:50vh;order:-1}
    .hero-media::after{background:linear-gradient(180deg,transparent 60%,var(--nude))}
    .about-grid,.hours-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .srv-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .sty-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .team-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .masonry{columns:2}
    .stats-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:2.5rem}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .opt-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .slots{grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr)}
    .open-now{display:none}
  }
  @media(max-width:560px){
    .srv-grid,.sty-grid,.team-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .masonry{columns:1}
    form .frow{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .sec-head .note{text-align:left}
    .bbar .bs span:not(.n){display:none}
    .slots{grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr)}
    .about .creds{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  }
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="cursor"></div>
<div class="cursor-ring"></div>

<div id="preloader">
  <div class="top"><span>MAISON COLETTE</span><span data-i18n="preloader">Preparant el saló</span></div>
  <div class="count" id="pcount">0</div>
  <div><div class="bar"><i id="pbar"></i></div></div>
</div>

<header id="header">
  <div class="wrap nav">
    <a href="#" class="logo">MAISON COLETTE<span data-i18n="logo_sub">Saló · Barberia · Estètica</span></a>
    <ul class="menu">
      <li><a href="#about" data-i18n="nav_about">Maison</a></li>
      <li><a href="#services" data-i18n="nav_services">Serveis</a></li>
      <li><a href="#booking" data-i18n="nav_booking">Reserva</a></li>
      <li><a href="#team" data-i18n="nav_team">Equip</a></li>
      <li><a href="#lookbook" data-i18n="nav_look">Lookbook</a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="nav-right">
      <span class="open-now" id="openNow"><span class="led"></span><span class="ot">—</span></span>
      <div class="lang">
        <button data-lang="ca" class="active">CA</button>
        <button data-lang="es">ES</button>
        <button data-lang="en">EN</button>
      </div>
      <button class="burger" aria-label="menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<section class="hero">
  <div class="hero-txt">
    <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="hero_eyebrow">Saló de perruqueria i barberia · Barcelona</span>
    <h1 id="heroTitle">
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w1">L'art</i></span>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w2">de</i></span>
      <span class="word"><i><em data-i18n="hero_w3">cuidar-te</em></i></span><br>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w4">el</i></span>
      <span class="word"><i data-i18n="hero_w5">cabell.</i></span>
    </h1>
    <p data-i18n="hero_sub">Tall, color, barberia i estètica amb producte professional i estilistes que t'escolten. Reserva online amb qui tu vulguis.</p>
    <div class="hero-cta">
      <a href="#booking" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="hero_cta1">Reservar cita</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      <a href="#services" class="btn"><span data-i18n="hero_cta2">Veure serveis</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="hero-media">
    <img class="parallax" data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1560066984-138dadb4c035?w=1300&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="">
    <div class="hero-badge"><span><b>15</b><span data-i18n="badge">anys fent<br>estil</span></span></div>
  </div>
  <div class="scroll-ind"><i></i><span data-i18n="scroll">Descobreix</span></div>
</section>

<div class="marquee">
  <div class="track" id="mtrack">
    <span><span data-i18n="m1">Tall i pentinat</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m2">Color</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m3">Barberia</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m4">Estètica</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m5">Tractaments</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m6">Núvies</em><i class="dot"></i></span>
  </div>
</div>

<section class="about" id="about">
  <div class="wrap about-grid">
    <div class="media">
      <div class="reveal-mask"></div>
      <img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1521590832167-7bcbfaa6381f?w=1100&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="Interior del saló">
    </div>
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="about_eyebrow">La maison</span>
      <h2 id="aboutTitle" style="margin-top:1.4rem">
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l1">Un saló on</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l2">et quedes</i></span>
        <span class="ln"><i data-i18n="about_l3"><em>una hora més.</em></i></span>
      </h2>
      <p style="color:var(--mut-l);line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.8rem;max-width:46ch" data-i18n="about_p">Vam obrir per fer-ho a poc a poc i bé: diagnòstic del cabell, producte professional i temps de debò per a cada client. Aquí no vas amb pressa, i el resultat es nota.</p>
      <div class="creds">
        <div><b data-i18n="cr1_b">Producte professional</b><span data-i18n="cr1_d">Marques de saló, sense sulfats agressius ni experiments.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr2_b">Diagnòstic gratuït</b><span data-i18n="cr2_d">Parlem del teu cabell abans de tocar-lo.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr3_b">Reserva online</b><span data-i18n="cr3_d">Tria servei, estilista i hora en menys d'un minut.</span></div>
        <div><b data-i18n="cr4_b">Sense esperes</b><span data-i18n="cr4_d">Cita tancada: arribes i comencem.</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="services" id="services">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="srv_title">La <em>carta</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="srv_note">Preus orientatius segons llargada i densitat del cabell. T'ho confirmem al diagnòstic.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="srv-grid">
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1562322140-8baeececf3df?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_cut">Tall</span><h3 data-i18n="s1_t">Tall i rentat</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s1_d">45 min</span><span class="pr">28 €</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1522337360788-8b13dee7a37e?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_color">Color</span><h3 data-i18n="s2_t">Color i matís</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s2_d">90 min</span><span class="pr">65 €</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1605497788044-5a32c7078486?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_mech">Mechas</span><h3 data-i18n="s3_t">Balayage</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s3_d">150 min</span><span class="pr">120 €</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1503951914875-452162b0f3f1?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_barber">Barberia</span><h3 data-i18n="s4_t">Tall i barba</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s4_d">40 min</span><span class="pr">24 €</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1487412947147-5cebf100ffc2?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_treat">Tractament</span><h3 data-i18n="s5_t">Botox capil·lar</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s5_d">75 min</span><span class="pr">55 €</span></div></div></div>
      <div class="srv"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1457972729786-0411a3b2b626?w=800&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="info"><span class="cat" data-i18n="cat_beauty">Estètica</span><h3 data-i18n="s6_t">Pestanyes i celles</h3><div class="meta"><span class="dur" data-i18n="s6_d">50 min</span><span class="pr">38 €</span></div></div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="booking" id="booking">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head" style="margin-bottom:0">
      <div>
        <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="book_eye">Reserva online</span>
        <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="book_title">La teva <em>cita</em></h2>
      </div>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="book_note">Quatre passos i tens la cita tancada amb el teu estilista.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="book" id="book">
      <div class="bbar">
        <div class="bs active" data-step="1"><span class="n">01</span><span data-i18n="bs1">Servei</span></div>
        <div class="bs" data-step="2"><span class="n">02</span><span data-i18n="bs2">Estilista</span></div>
        <div class="bs" data-step="3"><span class="n">03</span><span data-i18n="bs3">Dia i hora</span></div>
        <div class="bs" data-step="4"><span class="n">04</span><span data-i18n="bs4">Dades</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="bbody">
        <!-- STEP 1 -->
        <div class="bstep" data-step="1" style="display:block">
          <h3 data-i18n="b1_h">Què et ve de gust?</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="b1_sub">Tria el servei principal de la teva visita.</p>
          <div class="opt-grid" id="svcOpts">
            <div class="opt" data-svc="cut" data-dur="45" data-price="28"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s1_t">Tall i rentat</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s1_d">45 min</div></div><span class="pr">28 €</span></div>
            <div class="opt" data-svc="color" data-dur="90" data-price="65"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s2_t">Color i matís</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s2_d">90 min</div></div><span class="pr">65 €</span></div>
            <div class="opt" data-svc="balayage" data-dur="150" data-price="120"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s3_t">Balayage</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s3_d">150 min</div></div><span class="pr">120 €</span></div>
            <div class="opt" data-svc="barber" data-dur="40" data-price="24"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s4_t">Tall i barba</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s4_d">40 min</div></div><span class="pr">24 €</span></div>
            <div class="opt" data-svc="botox" data-dur="75" data-price="55"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s5_t">Botox capil·lar</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s5_d">75 min</div></div><span class="pr">55 €</span></div>
            <div class="opt" data-svc="lashes" data-dur="50" data-price="38"><div><div class="nm" data-i18n="s6_t">Pestanyes i celles</div><div class="d" data-i18n="s6_d">50 min</div></div><span class="pr">38 €</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="bnav"><span></span><button class="btn solid" id="bNext1"><span data-i18n="b_next">Següent</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
        </div>
        <!-- STEP 2 -->
        <div class="bstep" data-step="2">
          <h3 data-i18n="b2_h">Amb qui?</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="b2_sub">Tria el teu estilista o deixa'ns triar el primer lliure.</p>
          <div class="sty-grid" id="styOpts"></div>
          <div class="bnav"><button class="btn" id="bBack2"><span data-i18n="b_back">Enrere</span><span class="fill"></span></button><button class="btn solid" id="bNext2"><span data-i18n="b_next">Següent</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
        </div>
        <!-- STEP 3 -->
        <div class="bstep" data-step="3">
          <h3 data-i18n="b3_h">Quan?</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="b3_sub">Tria dia i hora. En gris, les hores ja ocupades.</p>
          <div class="dates" id="dateRow"></div>
          <div class="slots" id="slotRow"></div>
          <div class="bnav"><button class="btn" id="bBack3"><span data-i18n="b_back">Enrere</span><span class="fill"></span></button><button class="btn solid" id="bNext3"><span data-i18n="b_next">Següent</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
        </div>
        <!-- STEP 4 -->
        <div class="bstep" data-step="4">
          <h3 data-i18n="b4_h">Les teves dades</h3>
          <p class="sub" data-i18n="b4_sub">Et confirmarem la cita per WhatsApp o correu.</p>
          <div class="summary" id="summary"></div>
          <form id="bookForm" novalidate>
            <div class="frow">
              <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_name">Nom</label><input type="text" required></div>
              <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_phone">Telèfon</label><input type="tel" required></div>
            </div>
            <div class="cfield full"><label data-i18n="f_email">Correu</label><input type="email" required></div>
            <div class="cfield full"><label data-i18n="f_notes">Notes (al·lèrgies, referències…)</label><textarea></textarea></div>
            <div class="bnav" style="margin-top:1rem"><button type="button" class="btn" id="bBack4"><span data-i18n="b_back">Enrere</span><span class="fill"></span></button><button type="submit" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="b_confirm">Confirmar cita</span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="team" id="team">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="team_title">L'<em>equip</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="team_note">Clica un estilista per reservar-hi directament.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="team-bar">
      <span class="lbl" data-i18n="team_filter">Mostra</span>
      <div class="avail-toggle" id="availToggle">
        <button class="active" data-f="all" data-i18n="tf_all">Tothom</button>
        <button data-f="today" data-i18n="tf_today">Lliures avui</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="team-grid" id="teamGrid"></div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="lookbook" id="lookbook">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="look_title"><em>Lookbook</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="look_note">Treballs reals del saló. Clica per ampliar.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="masonry" id="masonry">
      <div class="mitem" data-cap-c="cat_color" data-cap-t="lk1"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1522337360788-8b13dee7a37e?w=700&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="cap"><div><span class="c" data-i18n="cat_color">Color</span><h4 data-i18n="lk1">Ros cendrat</h4></div></div></div>
      <div class="mitem" data-cap-c="cat_cut" data-cap-t="lk2"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1634480495809-803930315d44?w=700&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="cap"><div><span class="c" data-i18n="cat_cut">Tall</span><h4 data-i18n="lk2">Bob desfilat</h4></div></div></div>
      <div class="mitem" data-cap-c="cat_mech" data-cap-t="lk3"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1519699047748-de8e457a634e?w=700&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><div class="cap"><div><span class="c" data-i18n="cat_mech">Mechas</span><h4 data-i18n="lk3">Balayage mel</h4></div></div></div>

let curLang='ca';
function setLang(lang){
  curLang=lang;
  document.documentElement.lang=lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.getAttribute('data-i18n');if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined)el.innerHTML=I18N[lang][k];});
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
  renderStylists(); renderDates(); renderTeam(); renderHours(); updateOpenNow(); renderSummary();
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ LAZY IMG + FALLBACK ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(img=>{const s=img.getAttribute('data-src');img.onerror=()=>img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');img.src=s;});

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur=document.querySelector('.cursor'),ring=document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px';});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.18;ry+=(my-ry)*.18;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop);})();
document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.srv,.opt,.sty,.member,.mitem,.slot,.date').forEach(el=>{el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>ring.classList.add('grow'));el.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>ring.classList.remove('grow'));});

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis=new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{const id=a.getAttribute('href');if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}}));

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'),pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
const tl=gsap.timeline();
tl.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%';}})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.2')
.add(()=>{heroIn();});

/* ============ HERO IN ============ */
function heroIn(){
  if(reduce){gsap.set('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0});gsap.set('.hero .eyebrow,.hero-txt p,.hero-cta,.hero-badge,.scroll-ind',{y:0,opacity:1});gsap.set('.hero-media img',{scale:1});return;}
  gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.08});
  gsap.from('.hero .eyebrow,.hero-txt p,.hero-cta,.scroll-ind',{y:26,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',delay:.3});
  gsap.from('.hero-badge',{scale:0,rotation:-20,duration:.8,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.6});
  gsap.to('.hero-media img',{scale:1,duration:1.6,ease:'power2.out'},0);
}
gsap.to('.hero-media img',{yPercent:14,ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.hero',start:'top top',end:'bottom top',scrub:true}});

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ ABOUT ============ */
gsap.to('#aboutTitle .ln i',{y:0,duration:1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.12,scrollTrigger:{trigger:'#aboutTitle',start:'top 80%'}});
gsap.to('.about .reveal-mask',{scaleX:0,duration:1.1,ease:'expo.inOut',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .media',start:'top 75%'}});
gsap.from('.about .creds div',{y:24,opacity:0,duration:.7,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'.about .creds',start:'top 80%'}});

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');mtrack.innerHTML+=mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:26,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ GENERIC FADE-UP ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.srv,.mitem,.stat,.cta-box,.testi blockquote,.hours-grid > *').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{y:46,opacity:0,duration:.85,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}}));

/* ============ HOURS DATA ============ */
// index 0=Sun..6=Sat ; [openHour, closeHour] ; null = closed
const HOURS=[null,[9,19],[9,19],[9,19],[9,20],[9,20],[10,18]];
function pad(n){return String(n).padStart(2,'0');}
function todayIdx(){return new Date().getDay();}
function isOpenNow(){
  const now=new Date(),d=now.getDay(),h=now.getHours()+now.getMinutes()/60;
  const r=HOURS[d]; if(!r)return{open:false};
  return{open:h>=r[0]&&h<r[1],range:r};
}
function updateOpenNow(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],st=isOpenNow();
  const nav=document.getElementById('openNow'),box=document.getElementById('nowBox');
  const now=new Date(),h=now.getHours()+now.getMinutes()/60;
  if(st.open){
    nav.className='open-now on'; nav.querySelector('.ot').textContent=L.now_open;
    box.className='nowbox on';
    document.getElementById('nowT').textContent=L.now_open;
    document.getElementById('nowS').textContent=L.now_until+' '+pad(st.range[1])+':00';
  }else{
    nav.className='open-now off';
    box.className='nowbox off';
    const r=HOURS[now.getDay()];
    if(!r){nav.querySelector('.ot').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowT').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowS').textContent=L.now_closedday;}
    else if(h<r[0]){nav.querySelector('.ot').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowT').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowS').textContent=L.now_opens+' '+pad(r[0])+':00';}
    else{nav.querySelector('.ot').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowT').textContent=L.now_closed;document.getElementById('nowS').textContent=L.now_closedday;}
  }
}
function renderHours(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],list=document.getElementById('hlist'),ti=todayIdx();
  list.innerHTML='';
  // show Mon..Sun order (1..6,0)
  [1,2,3,4,5,6,0].forEach(d=>{
    const r=HOURS[d],row=document.createElement('div');row.className='hrow'+(d===ti?' today':'');
    const t=r?pad(r[0])+':00 – '+pad(r[1])+':00':L.now_closedday;
    row.innerHTML=`<span class="d">${L.daysF[d]}</span><span class="t${r?'':' closed'}">${t}</span>`;
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
updateOpenNow(); setInterval(updateOpenNow,30000);

/* ============ STYLISTS ============ */
const STYLISTS=[
  {id:'colette',img:'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1580618672591-eb180b1a973f?w=500&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop',name:'Colette',role:{ca:'Directora · Color',es:'Directora · Color',en:'Director · Colour'}},
  {id:'marc',img:'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1500648767791-00dcc994a43e?w=500&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop',name:'Marc',role:{ca:'Barber',es:'Barbero',en:'Barber'}},
  {id:'laia',img:'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?w=500&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop',name:'Laia',role:{ca:'Tall i tractaments',es:'Corte y tratamientos',en:'Cut & treatments'}},
  {id:'noa',img:'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1438761681033-6461ffad8d80?w=500&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop',name:'Noa',role:{ca:'Estètica',es:'Estética',en:'Beauty'}}
];
// deterministic "free today" per stylist per day
function hash(s){let h=0;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++)h=(h*31+s.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;return h;}
function stylistFreeToday(id){const k=id+'-'+todayIdx();return (hash(k)%10)>3;} // ~60% free
// deterministic taken slot
function slotTaken(id,dayOffset,h){const k=id+'-'+dayOffset+'-'+h;return (hash(k)%10)<3;} // ~30% taken

let sel={svc:null,svcName:'',svcDur:0,svcPrice:0,sty:null,styName:'',dateIdx:0,slot:null};

function renderStylists(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],wrap=document.getElementById('styOpts');
  wrap.innerHTML='';
  // "any free" option
  const any=document.createElement('div');any.className='sty'+(sel.sty==='any'?' sel':'');
  any.innerHTML=`<div class="ph img-fallback" style="display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:2rem;color:var(--gold)">✦</div><div class="nm">${L.sty_any}</div><div class="rl">${L.sty_any_d}</div>`;
  any.addEventListener('click',()=>{sel.sty='any';sel.styName=L.sty_any;renderStylists();});
  wrap.appendChild(any);
  STYLISTS.forEach(s=>{
    const free=stylistFreeToday(s.id);
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='sty'+(sel.sty===s.id?' sel':'');
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="avail ${free?'yes':'no'}">${free?L.avail_yes:L.avail_no}</span><div class="ph"><img data-src="${s.img}" alt="${s.name}"></div><div class="nm">${s.name}</div><div class="rl">${s.role[curLang]}</div>`;
    el.querySelector('img').onerror=function(){this.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');};
    el.addEventListener('click',()=>{sel.sty=s.id;sel.styName=s.name;renderStylists();});
    wrap.appendChild(el);
  });
}

/* ============ TEAM (public) ============ */
let teamFilter='all';
function renderTeam(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],grid=document.getElementById('teamGrid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  STYLISTS.forEach(s=>{
    const free=stylistFreeToday(s.id);
    const dim=(teamFilter==='today'&&!free);
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='member'+(dim?' dim':'');
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="ph"><span class="badge ${free?'yes':'no'}">${free?L.avail_yes:L.avail_no}</span><img data-src="${s.img}" alt="${s.name}"></div><h3>${s.name}</h3><div class="role">${s.role[curLang]}</div><span class="book-me">${L.book_me}</span>`;
    el.querySelector('img').onerror=function(){this.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');};
    el.addEventListener('click',()=>{sel.sty=s.id;sel.styName=s.name;goStep(2);renderStylists();lenis.scrollTo('#booking',{offset:-60});});
    grid.appendChild(el);
  });
  if(!reduce)gsap.from(grid.children,{y:30,opacity:0,duration:.6,stagger:.08,ease:'power3.out'});
}
document.querySelectorAll('#availToggle button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  document.querySelectorAll('#availToggle button').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));
  teamFilter=b.dataset.f;renderTeam();
}));

/* ============ DATES + SLOTS ============ */
function fmtDate(d){
  const loc=curLang==='en'?'en-GB':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(loc,{weekday:'short',day:'numeric',month:'short'}).format(d);
}
function renderDates(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],row=document.getElementById('dateRow');
  row.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
    const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()+i);
    const r=HOURS[d.getDay()],closed=!r;
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='date'+(closed?' closed':'')+(sel.dateIdx===i?' sel':'');
    const parts=fmtDate(d).replace('.','').split(' ');
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="wd">${L.days[d.getDay()]}</div><div class="dn">${parts[parts.length-2]||parts[0]}</div><div class="mo">${parts.slice(0,-1).join(' ')||''}</div>`;
    if(!closed)el.addEventListener('click',()=>{sel.dateIdx=i;sel.slot=null;renderDates();renderSlots();});
    row.appendChild(el);
  }
  renderSlots();
}
function renderSlots(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],row=document.getElementById('slotRow');
  row.innerHTML='';
  const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()+sel.dateIdx);
  const r=HOURS[d.getDay()];
  if(!r){row.innerHTML=`<div class="slots-empty">${L.now_closedday}</div>`;return;}
  const styKey=sel.sty&&sel.sty!=='any'?sel.sty:'x';
  for(let h=r[0];h<r[1];h++){
    const taken=slotTaken(styKey+sel.dateIdx,sel.dateIdx,h);
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='slot'+(taken?' taken':'')+(sel.slot===h?' sel':'');
    el.textContent=pad(h)+':00';
    if(!taken)el.addEventListener('click',()=>{sel.slot=h;renderSlots();});
    row.appendChild(el);
  }
}

/* ============ BOOKING STEPS ============ */
const bsteps=[...document.querySelectorAll('.bstep')],bbars=[...document.querySelectorAll('.bbar .bs')];
function goStep(n){
  bsteps.forEach(s=>s.style.display=(+s.dataset.step===n)?'block':'none');
  bbars.forEach(b=>{const sn=+b.dataset.step;b.classList.toggle('active',sn===n);b.classList.toggle('done',sn<n);});
  if(n===4)renderSummary();
  if(!reduce){const c=bsteps.find(s=>+s.dataset.step===n);gsap.fromTo(c,{opacity:0,x:24},{opacity:1,x:0,duration:.5,ease:'power3.out'});}
}
// step 1 select
document.querySelectorAll('#svcOpts .opt').forEach(o=>o.addEventListener('click',()=>{
  document.querySelectorAll('#svcOpts .opt').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));
  o.classList.add('sel');
  sel.svc=o.dataset.svc;sel.svcDur=+o.dataset.dur;sel.svcPrice=+o.dataset.price;
  sel.svcName=o.querySelector('.nm').textContent;
}));
function flash(s){if(reduce)return;gsap.fromTo(s,{x:-6},{x:6,duration:.06,repeat:3,yoyo:true});}
document.getElementById('bNext1').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(sel.svc)goStep(2);else flash('#svcOpts');});
document.getElementById('bBack2').addEventListener('click',()=>goStep(1));
document.getElementById('bNext2').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(sel.sty)goStep(3);else flash('#styOpts');});
document.getElementById('bBack3').addEventListener('click',()=>goStep(2));
document.getElementById('bNext3').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(sel.slot!==null)goStep(4);else flash('#slotRow');});
document.getElementById('bBack4').addEventListener('click',()=>goStep(3));

function dateLabel(){const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()+sel.dateIdx);return fmtDate(d);}
function renderSummary(){
  const L=I18N[curLang],box=document.getElementById('summary');
  const when=sel.slot!==null?(dateLabel()+' · '+pad(sel.slot)+':00'):'—';
  box.innerHTML=`
    <div class="sr"><span>${L.sum_svc}</span><b>${sel.svcName||'—'} (${sel.svcDur||0} min)</b></div>
    <div class="sr"><span>${L.sum_sty}</span><b>${sel.styName||'—'}</b></div>
    <div class="sr"><span>${L.sum_when}</span><b>${when}</b></div>
    <div class="sr"><span>${L.sum_price}</span><b>${sel.svcPrice?sel.svcPrice+' €':'—'}</b></div>`;
}

/* ============ BOOKING CONFIRM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('bookForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  const L=I18N[curLang];
  document.getElementById('okMini').innerHTML=`
    <div><span>${L.sum_svc}</span><b>${sel.svcName}</b></div>
    <div><span>${L.sum_sty}</span><b>${sel.styName}</b></div>
    <div><span>${L.sum_when}</span><b>${dateLabel()} · ${pad(sel.slot)}:00</b></div>`;
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});}
  else{
    gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
    gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});
    gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});
  }
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});});

/* ============ LIGHTBOX ============ */
const mitems=[...document.querySelectorAll('.mitem')],lightbox=document.getElementById('lightbox'),lbImg=document.getElementById('lbImg'),lbCapC=document.getElementById('lbCapC'),lbCapT=document.getElementById('lbCapT');
let lbIdx=0;
function openLb(i){
  lbIdx=i;const it=mitems[i],img=it.querySelector('img');
  lbImg.src=img.src;
  lbCapC.textContent=I18N[curLang][it.dataset.capC]||'';
  lbCapT.textContent=I18N[curLang][it.dataset.capT]||'';
  gsap.set(lightbox,{display:'grid'});
  if(!reduce)gsap.fromTo(lightbox,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});
}
function navLb(d){openLb((lbIdx+d+mitems.length)%mitems.length);}
function closeLb(){if(reduce){gsap.set(lightbox,{display:'none'});return;}gsap.to(lightbox,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(lightbox,{display:'none',opacity:1})});}
mitems.forEach((it,i)=>it.addEventListener('click',()=>openLb(i)));
document.getElementById('lbPrev').addEventListener('click',()=>navLb(-1));
document.getElementById('lbNext').addEventListener('click',()=>navLb(1));
document.getElementById('lbClose').addEventListener('click',closeLb);
lightbox.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===lightbox)closeLb();});
window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(lightbox.style.display==='grid'){if(e.key==='Escape')closeLb();if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')navLb(-1);if(e.key==='ArrowRight')navLb(1);}});

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.stat .num').forEach(num=>{
  const target=+num.dataset.count;const suf=num.querySelector('em,span');const sHtml=suf?suf.outerHTML:'';
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:num,start:'top 85%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){num.innerHTML=target+sHtml;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:2,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){num.innerHTML=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v)+sHtml;}});
  }});
});

/* ============ MAGNETIC BUTTONS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(reduce)return;const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.25,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.35,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});});
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});

document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open');});

/* ============ INIT ============ */
renderStylists(); renderDates(); renderTeam(); renderHours();
setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

<answer>

## Qué incluye esta web (negocio #5 — peluquerías / barberías / estética)

**Modalidad nueva: editorial de salón de alta gama.** Sexta identidad distinta a todas las anteriores: base **nude**, tinta **carbón**, acento **borgoña + dorado suave**, tipografía *Playfair Display* itálica (aire revista de moda) + *Jost* estrecha, hero **asimétrico a dos columnas** con imagen a sangre y *badge* circular. Marca *MAISON COLETTE*.

**Sello premium mantenido**
- GSAP 3 + ScrollTrigger + Lenis; preloader 0→100; reveal por palabras/líneas; parallax; cursor magnético; marquee; botones magnéticos; contadores con sufijo preservado; fade-up genérico.

**Cuatro módulos de front-end inéditos en la serie**
1. **Reserva por 4 pasos con slots horarios reales**: (1) servicio con duración+precio, (2) estilista (con opción "cualquiera libre"), (3) **fecha** (7 días generados con `Intl.DateTimeFormat` por idioma, marcando los cerrados) + **horas** generadas por franja con ~30% "ocupadas" de forma determinista por *seed* (cambian al cambiar estilista/día), (4) datos + **resumen** en vivo → overlay de confirmación con el detalle. Barra 01–04 con estados *active/done*.
2. **Equipo de estilistas con disponibilidad conectada al booking**: cada tarjeta muestra "libre hoy / completo hoy" (determinista por día), un **filtro "libres hoy"** que atenúa a los ocupados, y al **clicar un estilista** se preselecciona en el booking y hace scroll al paso 2.
3. **Indicador "abierto ahora" en tiempo real**: calculado con `Date()` sobre un mapa de horarios por día, mostrado en la nav y en la sección de horarios (con "abierto hasta…", "abrimos a las…", "cerrado hoy"), **recalculado cada 30 s** y con texto traducido. La lista de horarios resalta el día actual.
4. **Lookbook masonry con lightbox**: galería en columnas con caption en hover y un **modal** de imagen ampliada con navegación prev/next, cierre con Esc y clic fuera.

**Más secciones propias del vertical**
- Carta de servicios tipo *lookbook* con reveal de duración+precio en hover.
- Credenciales (producto profesional, diagnóstico gratis, reserva online, sin esperas).
- Stats, testimonio editorial, CTA borgoña, footer.

**Trilingüe real (CA / ES / EN)**
- `I18N` con **todas** las claves: servicios, categorías, roles de estilistas, pasos del booking, estados abierto/cerrado, captions del lookbook, resumen y confirmación, y los **nombres de día** (abreviados y completos). Al cambiar de idioma se **regeneran** fechas, slots, equipo, horarios y el estado "abierto ahora".

**Robustez y accesibilidad**
- Imágenes de **Unsplash** vía `data-src` con `onerror → .img-fallback`; `clamp()` + breakpoints a 980px / 560px; `prefers-reduced-motion` con *sets* en hero, pasos, equipo, lightbox, stats y overlay; lightbox operable por teclado (Esc / ← / →).

## Cómo usarla
1. Copia el bloque en `index.html` y ábrelo (necesita internet para fuentes, GSAP, Lenis e imágenes).
2. Edita textos, **servicios/precios/duraciones** (en el HTML y en `I18N`), **estilistas** (array `STYLISTS`), **horarios** (array `HOURS`), teléfono y dirección.
3. Para producción: sustituye las URLs por fotos reales del salón/equipo/lookbook y conecta el formulario de reserva a un backend/servicio (o a tu sistema de citas real).

## Siguiente paso
Cuando me digas **`. 6`**, construiré la web completa premium para el tipo de cliente **#6 — Inmobiliarias y agentes de bienes raíces** (con su propia modalidad de front-end e identidad visual).

</answer>
