```html
  .hero-cta{display:flex;gap:.8rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .hero-meta{display:flex;gap:2.2rem;margin-top:2.6rem;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d);padding-top:1.4rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .hero-meta div{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .hero-meta b{display:block;font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.7rem;color:var(--frost);letter-spacing:-.01em;margin-bottom:.25rem}
  .hero-meta b em{font-size:.9em}
  /* hero visual: floating diagnostic card + tooth motif */
  .hero-vis{position:relative;min-height:clamp(360px,46vh,520px)}
  .hero-vis .ring{position:absolute;inset:8% 14%;border:1px solid var(--line-d);border-radius:50%}
  .hero-vis .ring.r2{inset:18% 24%;border-color:rgba(182,242,78,.2)}
  .hero-vis .ring.r3{inset:30% 36%;border-color:rgba(182,242,78,.35)}
  .hero-vis .tooth{position:absolute;inset:0;display:grid;place-items:center}
  .hero-vis .tooth svg{width:46%;height:auto;filter:drop-shadow(0 20px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.4))}
  .hero-vis .tooth .enamel{fill:var(--frost)}
  .hero-vis .tooth .scan-line{stroke:var(--lime);stroke-width:2}
  .floatcard{position:absolute;background:var(--petrol2);border:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:1rem 1.2rem;max-width:210px}
  .floatcard.fc1{top:6%;left:-2%}
  .floatcard.fc2{bottom:8%;right:-3%}
  .floatcard .fc-t{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem}
  .floatcard .fc-v{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:1.2rem;margin-top:.4rem;line-height:1.1}
  .floatcard .fc-v small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;color:var(--lime);font-weight:500}
  .floatcard .bars{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:4px;height:34px;margin-top:.7rem}
  .floatcard .bars i{flex:1;background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--lime),rgba(182,242,78,.2));transform:scaleY(0);transform-origin:bottom}

  /* MARQUEE */
  .marquee{border-block:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:1.2rem 0;overflow:hidden;background:var(--petrol2)}
  .marquee .track{display:flex;gap:2.6rem;white-space:nowrap;width:max-content;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .marquee .track span{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:2.6rem}
  .marquee .track em{color:var(--lime);font-style:normal;font-family:var(--serif);font-size:1.3rem;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none}
  .marquee .dot{width:6px;height:6px;background:var(--petrol3);transform:rotate(45deg);display:inline-block}

  /* SECTION HEAD */
  .sec{padding:clamp(5rem,11vh,9rem) 0}
  .sec.light{background:var(--frost);color:var(--ink)}
  .sec-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;gap:2rem;margin-bottom:clamp(2rem,5vh,3rem);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .sec-head h2{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.2rem,6vw,5rem);line-height:.92;letter-spacing:-.025em}
  .sec-head h2 em{font-size:1.02em}
  .sec-head .note{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.88rem;max-width:28ch;text-align:right;line-height:1.6}
  .sec:not(.light) .sec-head .note{color:var(--mut-d)}

  /* ===== INTERACTIVE DENTAL ARCH ===== */
  .arch-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr .9fr;gap:clamp(1.5rem,4vw,3.5rem);align-items:center}
  .arch-stage{background:var(--petrol2);border:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:clamp(1.4rem,3vw,2.4rem);position:relative}
  .arch-stage .hint{position:absolute;top:1.2rem;right:1.4rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem}
  .arch-svg{width:100%;height:auto}
  .arch-svg .gum{fill:var(--petrol3)}
  .arch-svg .tooth{fill:var(--frost2);stroke:var(--petrol);stroke-width:1;cursor:none;transition:fill .25s,transform .25s;transform-origin:center;transform-box:fill-box}
  .arch-svg .tooth:hover{fill:var(--lime)}
  .arch-svg .tooth.sel{fill:var(--lime)}
  .arch-svg .tooth.warn{fill:var(--coral)}
  .arch-svg .lbl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:7px;fill:var(--mut-d);letter-spacing:.04em;pointer-events:none}
  .arch-info{border:1px solid var(--line-d);background:var(--petrol2);padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem);min-height:340px;display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .arch-info .piece{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime)}
  .arch-info h3{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem);line-height:.98;margin-top:.5rem;letter-spacing:-.01em}
  .arch-info .desc{color:var(--mut-d);line-height:1.7;margin-top:.9rem;font-size:.92rem}
  .arch-info .specs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0;margin:1.4rem 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .arch-info .specs .sp{padding:.9rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .arch-info .specs .sp:nth-child(odd){padding-right:1rem;border-right:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .arch-info .specs .sp:nth-child(even){padding-left:1rem}
  .arch-info .specs .k{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.54rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .arch-info .specs .v{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:1.3rem;margin-top:.25rem}
  .arch-info .specs .v small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;color:var(--lime);font-weight:500}
  .arch-info .doc{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.8rem;margin-top:auto;padding-top:1.2rem;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .arch-info .doc .av{width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;border:1.5px solid var(--lime);flex:none}
  .arch-info .doc .nm{font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem}
  .arch-info .doc .rl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .arch-info .btn{margin-top:1.2rem;align-self:flex-start}

  /* ===== DIAGNOSTIC LAYER STACK (scrub) ===== */
  .stack-sec{background:var(--petrol2);border-block:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .stack-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:.8fr 1.2fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);align-items:center}
  .stack-side h2{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);line-height:.95;letter-spacing:-.02em}
  .stack-side .lead{color:var(--mut-d);line-height:1.7;margin:1.2rem 0 1.8rem;max-width:32ch}
  .stack-legend{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.2rem}
  .stack-legend .li{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.9rem;padding:.8rem 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mut-d);transition:color .3s}
  .stack-legend .li .n{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.1rem;color:var(--petrol3);width:1.6rem;transition:color .3s}
  .stack-legend .li.on{color:var(--frost)}
  .stack-legend .li.on .n{color:var(--lime)}
  .stack-stage{position:relative;aspect-ratio:4/3;border:1px solid var(--line-d);overflow:hidden;background:var(--petrol)}
  .stack-layer{position:absolute;inset:0;will-change:transform,opacity}
  .stack-layer img{filter:saturate(.9) contrast(1.05)}
  .stack-layer .cap{position:absolute;left:1.2rem;bottom:1.2rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;background:rgba(14,42,38,.7);padding:.4rem .7rem;color:var(--lime)}
  .stack-scrub{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:3px;background:var(--line-d);z-index:5}
  .stack-scrub i{display:block;height:100%;width:0;background:var(--lime)}

  /* ===== ORTHO COMPARATOR ===== */
  .ortho-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:1px;background:var(--line);border:1px solid var(--line)}
  .ortho{background:var(--white);padding:clamp(1.4rem,2.4vw,2rem);display:flex;flex-direction:column;cursor:none;transition:background .35s}
  .ortho:hover{background:var(--frost2)}
  .ortho .ic{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:2rem;color:var(--petrol3);line-height:1}
  .ortho h3{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:1.2rem;margin-top:.8rem;line-height:1.05}
  .ortho .pr{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.5rem;margin:.8rem 0 1.2rem;letter-spacing:-.01em}
  .ortho .pr small{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;color:var(--mut-l);font-weight:500}
  .ortho .metric{margin-bottom:.9rem}
  .ortho .metric .ml{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-l);margin-bottom:.35rem}
  .ortho .metric .bar{height:5px;background:var(--frost2);position:relative}
  .ortho .metric .bar i{position:absolute;left:0;top:0;height:100%;width:0;background:var(--petrol3);transition:width 1s var(--ease)}
  .ortho:hover .metric .bar i{background:var(--lime2)}
  .ortho .pick{margin-top:auto;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600;color:var(--petrol3);border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:1rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem}
  .ortho .pick .ar{transition:transform .35s var(--ease)}
  .ortho:hover .pick .ar{transform:translateX(4px)}

  /* ===== TREATMENT CALCULATOR ===== */
  .calc-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1.2rem;align-items:stretch}
  .calc-card{border:1.5px solid var(--ink);background:var(--white);padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem)}
  .calc-card h3{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.5rem}
  .calc-card .sub{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.86rem;margin:.2rem 0 1.6rem}
  .opt{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:1rem;padding:1rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);cursor:none}
  .opt:last-of-type{border-bottom:0}
  .opt .chk{width:22px;height:22px;border:1.5px solid var(--line2);flex:none;display:grid;place-items:center;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.8rem;color:transparent;transition:.25s}
  .opt.on .chk{background:var(--petrol);border-color:var(--petrol);color:var(--lime)}
  .opt .ot{flex:1}
  .opt .ot b{font-weight:600;font-size:.96rem;display:block}
  .opt .ot span{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mut-l)}
  .opt .op{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;color:var(--petrol3)}
  .opt .stepper{display:none;align-items:center;gap:.7rem}
  .opt.on.hasnum .stepper{display:flex}
  .opt.on.hasnum .op{display:none}
  .stepper button{width:30px;height:30px;border:1.5px solid var(--line2);background:var(--frost);cursor:none;font-family:var(--mono);font-weight:600}
  .stepper .nv{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;min-width:1.4rem;text-align:center}
  .calc-out{background:var(--petrol);color:var(--frost);display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center}
  .calc-out .lbl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.62rem;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime)}
  .calc-out .total{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2.6rem,6vw,4.4rem);line-height:.9;margin:.5rem 0 .2rem;letter-spacing:-.02em}
  .calc-out .fin{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.78rem;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .calc-out .fin b{color:var(--lime2)}
  .calc-break{margin-top:1.8rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.6rem;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d);padding-top:1.4rem}
  .calc-break .br{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:.84rem}
  .calc-break .br span{color:var(--mut-d)}
  .calc-break .br b{font-family:var(--mono)}
  .calc-out .legal{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;color:var(--mut-d);margin-top:1.6rem;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:.04em}
  .calc-out .btn{margin-top:1.6rem;align-self:flex-start}

  /* ===== DOCTOR AGENDA ===== */
  .agenda-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:1.2rem}
  .doc-card{border:1px solid var(--line-d);background:var(--petrol2);display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .doc-card .ph{aspect-ratio:5/4;overflow:hidden;position:relative}
  .doc-card .ph img{filter:grayscale(.4);transition:filter .6s var(--ease),transform 1s var(--ease)}
  .doc-card:hover .ph img{filter:grayscale(0);transform:scale(1.05)}
  .doc-card .ph .spec{position:absolute;top:.9rem;left:.9rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;background:var(--lime);color:var(--ink);padding:.3rem .55rem;font-weight:600}
  .doc-card .body{padding:1.2rem 1.3rem 1.4rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;flex:1}
  .doc-card h3{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:1.3rem}
  .doc-card .lang-sp{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mut-d);margin-top:.3rem;text-transform:uppercase}
  .doc-card .day-lbl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.56rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime);margin:1.1rem 0 .6rem}
  .doc-card .slots{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:.4rem}
  .doc-card .slot{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.72rem;border:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:.45rem .6rem;cursor:none;transition:.25s}
  .doc-card .slot:hover{border-color:var(--lime);color:var(--lime)}
  .doc-card .slot.sel{background:var(--lime);color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--lime)}
  .doc-card .slot.gone{opacity:.3;text-decoration:line-through;cursor:not-allowed}
  .doc-card .book{margin-top:1.2rem;opacity:0;max-height:0;transition:.4s var(--ease);overflow:hidden}
  .doc-card .book.show{opacity:1;max-height:60px;margin-top:1.2rem}

  /* STATS */
  .strip{border-block:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:1.6rem 0;background:var(--petrol2)}
  .strip-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:1rem}
  .strip .s{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:.8rem;padding:0 1rem;border-right:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .strip .s:last-child{border-right:0}
  .strip .s .n{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(1.8rem,3.5vw,2.8rem);line-height:.9}
  .strip .s .n em{color:var(--lime);font-style:normal}
  .strip .s .l{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d);line-height:1.4}

  /* TECH list (asymmetric, not equal cards) */
  .tech-list{display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .tech-item{display:grid;grid-template-columns:.5fr 1fr .8fr;gap:clamp(1rem,3vw,3rem);align-items:center;padding:clamp(1.4rem,3vw,2.2rem) 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);cursor:none;transition:padding .4s var(--ease)}
  .tech-item:last-child{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .tech-item:hover{padding-left:1rem}
  .tech-item .tn{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(1.4rem,2.6vw,2rem);color:var(--petrol3);transition:color .3s}
  .tech-item:hover .tn{color:var(--lime2)}
  .tech-item h4{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:700;font-size:clamp(1.2rem,2.2vw,1.7rem);line-height:1.05}
  .tech-item p{color:var(--mut-l);font-size:.88rem;line-height:1.6;margin-top:.4rem}
  .tech-item .tg{text-align:right;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-l)}

  /* TESTI */
  .testi-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:1.2rem}
  .tcard{border:1px solid var(--line-d);background:var(--petrol2);padding:clamp(1.6rem,3vw,2.4rem);position:relative}
  .tcard .treat{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem}
  .tcard .q{font-family:var(--serif);font-style:italic;font-size:clamp(1.3rem,2.4vw,1.9rem);line-height:1.25;margin-top:1rem}
  .tcard .who{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.9rem;margin-top:1.6rem}
  .tcard .who .av{width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;flex:none;border:1.5px solid var(--lime)}
  .tcard .who .nm{font-weight:600;font-size:.9rem}
  .tcard .who .rl{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--mut-d)}
  .tcard .stars{position:absolute;top:1.6rem;right:1.6rem;font-family:var(--mono);color:var(--lime);letter-spacing:.15em;font-size:.8rem}

  /* CTA */
  .cta-box{background:var(--lime);color:var(--ink);padding:clamp(2.4rem,6vw,5rem);display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);align-items:center;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .cta-box::before{content:"";position:absolute;right:-70px;top:-70px;width:280px;height:280px;border:34px solid rgba(16,32,28,.06);border-radius:50%}
  .cta-box h2{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:clamp(2rem,5vw,4rem);line-height:.95;letter-spacing:-.02em;position:relative}
  .cta-box h2 em{color:var(--petrol)}
  .cta-box .lead{line-height:1.7;margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:34ch;position:relative;font-weight:500}
  form .frow{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1rem;position:relative}
  .cfield{margin-bottom:1rem}
  .cfield.full{grid-column:1/-1}
  .cfield label{display:block;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.58rem;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:.5rem;color:rgba(16,32,28,.6)}
  .cfield input,.cfield select,.cfield textarea{width:100%;background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:1.5px solid rgba(16,32,28,.3);padding:.6rem 0;font-family:var(--disp);font-size:.92rem;color:var(--ink);outline:none;cursor:none;transition:border-color .3s}
  .cfield textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:70px}
  .cfield input:focus,.cfield select:focus,.cfield textarea:focus{border-color:var(--ink)}
  .cfield select{appearance:none}
  .cta-box .btn{background:var(--ink);color:var(--lime);border-color:var(--ink);position:relative}
  .cta-box .btn .fill{background:var(--frost)}
  .cta-box .btn:hover{color:var(--ink)}

  .res-ok{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9500;background:rgba(14,42,38,.92);backdrop-filter:blur(8px);display:none;place-items:center;padding:1.5rem}
  .res-ok .card{background:var(--frost);color:var(--ink);padding:clamp(2rem,5vw,3.5rem);text-align:center;max-width:440px}
  .res-ok .check{width:70px;height:70px;background:var(--petrol);color:var(--lime);display:grid;place-items:center;margin:0 auto 1.2rem;font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.8rem;border-radius:50%}
  .res-ok h3{font-family:var(--disp);font-weight:800;font-size:1.8rem}
  .res-ok p{color:var(--mut-l);margin:1rem 0 1.6rem;line-height:1.6}

  /* FOOTER */
  footer{background:var(--petrol);color:var(--frost);padding:clamp(4rem,8vh,6rem) 0 2rem;border-top:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .foot-top{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.6fr 1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2.5rem;padding-bottom:3rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d)}
  .foot-brand p{color:var(--mut-d);margin-top:1.2rem;max-width:34ch;line-height:1.6;font-size:.9rem}
  .foot-col h4{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--lime);margin-bottom:1.2rem;font-weight:600}
  .foot-col a{display:block;font-size:.9rem;padding:.35rem 0;color:rgba(232,242,236,.75);transition:.3s}
  .foot-col a:hover{color:var(--frost);padding-left:.4rem}
  .foot-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding-top:2rem;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.6rem;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mut-d);flex-wrap:wrap;gap:1rem}

  .img-fallback{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--petrol3),var(--petrol2))!important}
  .img-fallback img{display:none}

  @media(max-width:1000px){
    .menu{display:none}.burger{display:flex}
    .hero-grid,.arch-grid,.stack-grid,.calc-grid,.cta-box{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .hero-vis{min-height:340px;order:-1}
    .ortho-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .agenda-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .testi-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .tech-item{grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;gap:1rem}
    .tech-item .tg{display:none}
    .strip-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .strip .s:nth-child(2){border-right:0}
    .strip .s:nth-child(1),.strip .s:nth-child(2){border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d);padding-bottom:1rem}
    .strip .s:nth-child(3),.strip .s:nth-child(4){padding-top:1rem}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .next-free{display:none}
  }
  @media(max-width:600px){
    .ortho-grid,.agenda-grid,.strip-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .strip .s{border-right:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-d);padding:.8rem 0}
    .strip .s:last-child{border-bottom:0}
    form .frow{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .foot-top{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .arch-info .specs{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .arch-info .specs .sp:nth-child(odd){padding-right:0;border-right:0}
    .arch-info .specs .sp:nth-child(even){padding-left:0}
    .sec-head .note{text-align:left}
    .hero-meta{gap:1.4rem}
  }
  @media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*{animation:none!important;transition:none!important}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="cursor"></div>
<div class="cursor-ring"></div>

<div id="preloader">
  <div class="top"><span>VERDA · clínica</span><span data-i18n="preloader">Calibrant escàner 3D</span></div>
  <div class="mid">
    <div class="count" id="pcount">0</div>
    <div class="scan"><i></i></div>
  </div>
  <div><div class="bar"><i id="pbar"></i></div></div>
</div>

<header id="header">
  <div class="wrap nav">
    <a href="#" class="logo"><span class="mk"></span>VERDA<span data-i18n="logo_sub">odontologia digital</span></a>
    <ul class="menu">
      <li><a href="#arch" data-i18n="nav_arch">Tractaments</a></li>
      <li><a href="#diag" data-i18n="nav_diag">Diagnòstic</a></li>
      <li><a href="#ortho" data-i18n="nav_ortho">Ortodòncia</a></li>
      <li><a href="#agenda" data-i18n="nav_agenda">Agenda</a></li>
      <li><a href="#contact" data-i18n="nav_contact">Cita</a></li>
    </ul>
    <div class="nav-right">
      <span class="next-free"><span class="pulse-dot"></span><span data-i18n="nf">Propera lliure</span> <b id="nfTime">—</b></span>
      <div class="lang">
        <button data-lang="ca" class="active">CA</button>
        <button data-lang="es">ES</button>
        <button data-lang="en">EN</button>
      </div>
      <button class="burger" aria-label="menu"><i></i><i></i><i></i></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</header>

<!-- ===== HERO ===== -->
<section class="hero">
  <div class="wrap hero-grid">
    <div>
      <span class="eyebrow" data-i18n="h_eye">Odontologia digital · Barcelona</span>
      <h1 id="heroTitle" style="margin-top:1.3rem">
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="h_w1">La teva</i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="h_w2">somriure,</i></span><br>
        <span class="word"><i><em data-i18n="h_w3">escanejat</em></i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="h_w4">al</i></span>
        <span class="word"><i data-i18n="h_w5">mil·límetre.</i></span>
      </h1>
      <div class="hero-sub">
        <p data-i18n="h_sub">Diagnòstic 3D sense radiació innecessària, ortodòncia invisible i medicina estètica. Sense presses, sense sorpreses: saps què et farem i quant costa abans de seure a la butaca.</p>
        <div class="hero-cta">
          <a href="#agenda" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="h_cta1">Diagnòstic 3D gratuït</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
          <a href="#arch" class="btn"><span data-i18n="h_cta2">Explorar tractaments</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="hero-meta">
        <div><b>12</b><span data-i18n="hm1">Anys de clínica</span></div>
        <div><b>9<em>.8</em></b><span data-i18n="hm2">Nota mitjana</span></div>
        <div><b>4<em>D</em></b><span data-i18n="hm3">Escàner intraoral</span></div>
        <div><b>0<em>€</em></b><span data-i18n="hm4">Primera visita</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="hero-vis">
      <div class="ring r1"></div><div class="ring r2"></div><div class="ring r3"></div>
      <div class="tooth">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 120 150" id="toothSvg">
          <path class="enamel" d="M60 8 C30 8 18 28 20 56 C22 84 30 96 34 120 C37 138 44 146 50 142 C56 138 56 116 60 116 C64 116 64 138 70 142 C76 146 83 138 86 120 C90 96 98 84 100 56 C102 28 90 8 60 8 Z"/>
          <line class="scan-line" id="scanLine" x1="14" y1="40" x2="106" y2="40"/>
        </svg>
      </div>
      <div class="floatcard fc1">
        <div class="fc-t"><span class="pulse-dot"></span><span data-i18n="fc1_t">Escàner en curs</span></div>
        <div class="fc-v">32 <small data-i18n="fc1_u">peces</small></div>
        <div class="bars" id="fcBars"><i style="height:40%"></i><i style="height:70%"></i><i style="height:55%"></i><i style="height:90%"></i><i style="height:65%"></i></div>
      </div>
      <div class="floatcard fc2">
        <div class="fc-t" data-i18n="fc2_t">Pla de tractament</div>
        <div class="fc-v" data-i18n="fc2_v">Sense dolor · 3 sessions</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- MARQUEE -->
<div class="marquee">
  <div class="track" id="mtrack">
    <span><em data-i18n="m1">Implants</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m2">Ortodòncia invisible</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m3">Endodòncia</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m4">Estètica dental</span><i class="dot"></i><em data-i18n="m5">Periodòncia</em><i class="dot"></i><span data-i18n="m6">Odontopediatria</span><i class="dot"></i></span>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ===== INTERACTIVE ARCH ===== -->
<section class="sec" id="arch">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="arch_title">Toca la <em>peça</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="arch_note">Clica qualsevol dent de l'arcada i t'expliquem el tractament habitual, el preu orientatiu i el doctor que ho porta.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="arch-grid">
      <div class="arch-stage">
        <span class="hint"><span class="pulse-dot"></span><span data-i18n="arch_hint">Arcada interactiva</span></span>
        <svg class="arch-svg" id="archSvg" viewBox="0 0 360 240"></svg>
      </div>
      <div class="arch-info" id="archInfo"></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- STATS STRIP -->
<div class="strip">
  <div class="wrap strip-grid">
    <div class="s"><div class="n" data-count="3200">0<em>+</em></div><div class="l" data-i18n="st1">Somriures tractats</div></div>
    <div class="s"><div class="n" data-count="6">0</div><div class="l" data-i18n="st2">Especialistes</div></div>
    <div class="s"><div class="n" data-count="98">0<em>%</em></div><div class="l" data-i18n="st3">Èxit en implants</div></div>
    <div class="s"><div class="n" data-count="0">0<em>€</em></div><div class="l" data-i18n="st4">Primera visita</div></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ===== DIAGNOSTIC LAYER STACK ===== -->
<section class="sec stack-sec" id="diag">
  <div class="wrap stack-grid">
    <div class="stack-side">
      <span class="eyebrow inv" data-i18n="diag_eye">Diagnòstic 4D</span>
      <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="diag_title">Del raig X al <em>pla exacte</em></h2>
      <p class="lead" data-i18n="diag_lead">Fes scroll i travessa les capes del teu diagnòstic: radiografia, escàner intraoral, planificació 3D i resultat previst.</p>
      <div class="stack-legend" id="stackLegend"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="stack-stage" id="stackStage">
      <div class="stack-layer" data-i="0"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1606811841689-23dfddce3e95?w=1000&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><span class="cap" data-i18n="ly0">01 · Radiografia</span></div>
      <div class="stack-layer" data-i="1"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1588776814546-1ffcf47267a5?w=1000&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><span class="cap" data-i18n="ly1">02 · Escàner intraoral</span></div>
      <div class="stack-layer" data-i="2"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1609840114035-3c981b782dfe?w=1000&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><span class="cap" data-i18n="ly2">03 · Planificació 3D</span></div>
      <div class="stack-layer" data-i="3"><img data-src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1606265752439-1f18756aa5fc?w=1000&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt=""><span class="cap" data-i18n="ly3">04 · Resultat previst</span></div>
      <div class="stack-scrub"><i id="stackBar"></i></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ===== ORTHO COMPARATOR ===== -->
<section class="sec light" id="ortho">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="ortho_title">Quin <em>aparell</em> et va?</h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="ortho_note">Compara estètica, durada, comoditat i preu. Passa el cursor per veure les barres reals.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="ortho-grid" id="orthoGrid"></div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ===== TREATMENT CALCULATOR ===== -->
<section class="sec" id="calc">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <h2 data-i18n="calc_title">Compon el teu <em>pla</em></h2>
      <p class="note" data-i18n="calc_note">Marca el que necessites i tens un total orientatiu amb opció de finançament, a l'instant.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="calc-grid">
      <div class="calc-card">
        <h3 data-i18n="calc_h">El teu tractament</h3>
        <p class="sub" data-i18n="calc_sub">Suma serveis; els numerats ajusten quantitat.</p>
        <div id="calcOpts"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="calc-card calc-out">
        <span class="lbl" data-i18n="calc_total_lbl">Total orientatiu</span>
        <div class="total" id="calcTotal">0 €</div>
        <div class="fin"><span data-i18n="calc_fin">o des de</span> <b id="calcFin">0 €</b> <span data-i18n="calc_fin2">/ mes</span></div>
        <div class="calc-break" id="calcBreak"></div>
        <p class="legal" data-i18n="calc_legal">Orientatiu · el preu tancat surt del diagnòstic 3D gratuït. Finançament fins a 24 mesos sense interessos.</p>
        <a href="#agenda" class="btn solid"><span data-i18n="calc_btn">Reservar diagnòstic</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- ===== DOCTOR AGENDA ===== -->
<section class="sec" id="agenda" style="padding-top:0">

<!-- ===== CTA ===== -->
<section class="sec" id="contact" style="padding-top:0">
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="cta-box">
      <div>
        <span class="eyebrow dark" data-i18n="ct_eye">Primera visita gratuïta</span>
        <h2 style="margin-top:1rem" data-i18n="ct_title">Comença pel <em>diagnòstic</em></h2>
        <p class="lead" data-i18n="ct_lead">Escàner 3D, revisió i pla de tractament amb preu tancat. Sense compromís i, si cal, finançament al moment.</p>
      </div>
      <form id="ctForm" novalidate>
        <div class="frow">
          <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_name">Nom</label><input type="text" required></div>
          <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_phone">Telèfon</label><input type="tel" required></div>
        </div>
        <div class="frow">
          <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_email">Correu</label><input type="email" required></div>
          <div class="cfield"><label data-i18n="f_reason">Motiu</label>
            <select required>
              <option value="" data-i18n="r_ph">Tria</option>
              <option data-i18n="r_check">Revisió / neteja</option>
              <option data-i18n="r_ortho">Ortodòncia</option>
              <option data-i18n="r_impl">Implants</option>
              <option data-i18n="r_aest">Estètica</option>
              <option data-i18n="r_urg">Urgència / dolor</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="cfield full"><label data-i18n="f_msg">Comentari</label><textarea></textarea></div>
        <button type="submit" class="btn"><span data-i18n="f_submit">Demanar cita</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<div class="res-ok" id="resOk">
  <div class="card">
    <div class="check">✓</div>
    <h3 data-i18n="ok_title">Cita sol·licitada</h3>

    <button class="btn dark" id="okClose"><span data-i18n="ok_close">Tancar</span><span class="fill"></span></button>
  </div>
</div>

// deterministic slots per doc
function hash(s){let h=0;for(let i=0;i<s.length;i++)h=(h*31+s.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;return h;}
function slotsFor(docId){
  const base=['09:00','09:40','10:20','11:30','12:10','16:00','16:40','17:20','18:00'];
  return base.map((t,i)=>({t,gone:(hash(docId+'-'+i)%10)<3}));
}

  en:{
    preloader:"Calibrating 3D scanner",logo_sub:"digital dentistry",

    nf:"Next free",

    h_sub:"3D diagnosis with no unnecessary radiation, invisible orthodontics and aesthetic medicine. No rush, no surprises: you know what we'll do and what it costs before you sit in the chair.",
    h_cta1:"Free 3D diagnosis",h_cta2:"Explore treatments",
    hm1:"Years as a clinic",hm2:"Average rating",hm3:"Intraoral scanner",hm4:"First visit",
    fc1_t:"Scan in progress",fc1_u:"teeth",fc2_t:"Treatment plan",fc2_v:"Pain-free · 3 sessions",
    m1:"Implants",m2:"Invisible ortho",m3:"Endodontics",m4:"Dental aesthetics",m5:"Periodontics",m6:"Paediatric",

    ly0:"01 · X-ray",ly1:"02 · Intraoral scan",ly2:"03 · 3D planning",ly3:"04 · Predicted result",
    ortho_title:'Which <em>brace</em> suits you?',ortho_note:"Compare aesthetics, duration, comfort and price. Hover to see the real bars.",
    o_metal:"Metal braces",o_ceram:"Ceramic braces",o_invis:"Clear aligners",o_ling:"Lingual ortho",
    o_from:"from",o_month:"/mo",m_est:"Aesthetics",m_comf:"Comfort",m_vis:"Discretion",o_dur:"Average length",o_months:"months",o_pick:"Choose it",

    calc_h:"Your treatment",calc_sub:"Add services; the numbered ones adjust quantity.",
    co_clean:"Check-up + clean",co_clean_d:"every 6 months",co_white:"Professional whitening",co_white_d:"1 session",
    co_impl:"Titanium implant",co_impl_d:"per unit",co_ven:"Porcelain veneer",co_ven_d:"per tooth",co_ortho:"Invisible ortho",co_ortho_d:"full treatment",
    calc_total_lbl:"Indicative total",calc_fin:"or from",calc_fin2:"/ mo",calc_btn:"Book diagnosis",
    calc_legal:"Indicative · the fixed price comes from the free 3D diagnosis. Finance up to 24 months interest-free.",
    agenda_title:'Pick <em>doctor & time</em>',agenda_note:"Today's slots in real time. Click a time and book straight away.",
    ag_today:"Today ·",ag_book:"Confirm",ag_full:"No slots today",
    tech_title:'Technology in the <em>chair</em>',
    tc1:"3D intraoral scanner",tc1_d:"No more paste moulds: an optical scanner builds a 3D model of your mouth in minutes.",tg1:"No radiation",
    tc2:"Low-dose digital X-ray",tc2_d:"Up to 90% less radiation than traditional X-rays, with an instant on-screen image.",tg2:"−90% dose",
    tc3:"Digital smile design",tc3_d:"See the predicted result before we start, simulated over your own photo.",tg3:"Preview",
    tc4:"Computer-guided implantology",tc4_d:"We place the implant with a 3D-printed surgical guide: more precision, less chair time.",tg4:"3D guided",
    testi_title:'Real <em>smiles</em>',
    tq1:"“I'd been terrified of the dentist since I was a kid. Here I saw the 3D plan first and understood everything. The Invisalign is invisible.”",trk1:"Ortho patient",tk_invis:"Invisible ortho",
    tq2:"“They placed two implants in one visit with a 3D guide. Zero pain and, three months on, I eat everything.”",trk2:"Implant patient",tk_impl:"Implantology",
    ct_eye:"Free first visit",ct_title:'Start with the <em>diagnosis</em>',ct_lead:"3D scan, check-up and treatment plan with a fixed price. No obligation and, if needed, finance on the spot.",
    f_name:"Name",f_phone:"Phone",f_email:"Email",f_reason:"Reason",r_ph:"Choose",r_check:"Check-up / clean",r_ortho:"Orthodontics",r_impl:"Implants",r_aest:"Aesthetics",r_urg:"Urgency / pain",f_msg:"Note",f_submit:"Request visit",

    foot_desc:"Dental and aesthetic clinic in Barcelona. Digital dentistry, invisible orthodontics and 3D diagnosis with no unnecessary radiation.",
    foot_h1:"Treatments",foot_h2:"Clinic",foot_tech:"Technology",foot_press:"Press",foot_h3:"Clinic",foot_rights:"Official reg. no. · Made with care in Barcelona"
  }
};
let curLang='ca';
function T(k){return I18N[curLang][k]!==undefined?I18N[curLang][k]:k;}
function setLang(lang){
  curLang=lang;document.documentElement.lang=lang;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el=>{const k=el.getAttribute('data-i18n');if(I18N[lang][k]!==undefined)el.innerHTML=I18N[lang][k];});
  document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.lang===lang));
  renderArchInfo(curTooth);renderOrtho();renderCalc();renderAgenda();renderStackLegend();renderTech();renderTesti();
}
document.querySelectorAll('.lang button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setLang(b.dataset.lang)));

/* ============ FORMAT ============ */
function money(n){const loc=curLang==='en'?'en-IE':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES';return new Intl.NumberFormat(loc,{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(n);}

/* ============ LAZY IMG ============ */
function lazy(img){const s=img.getAttribute('data-src');if(!s)return;img.onerror=()=>img.parentElement.classList.add('img-fallback');img.src=s;}
document.querySelectorAll('img[data-src]').forEach(lazy);

/* ============ CURSOR ============ */
const cur=document.querySelector('.cursor'),ring=document.querySelector('.cursor-ring');
let mx=0,my=0,rx=0,ry=0;
window.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{mx=e.clientX;my=e.clientY;cur.style.left=mx+'px';cur.style.top=my+'px';});
(function loop(){rx+=(mx-rx)*.2;ry+=(my-ry)*.2;ring.style.left=rx+'px';ring.style.top=ry+'px';requestAnimationFrame(loop);})();
function bindGrow(){document.querySelectorAll('a,button,.ortho,.opt,.doc-card,.tech-item,.tcard,.tooth').forEach(el=>{el.onmouseenter=()=>ring.classList.add('grow');el.onmouseleave=()=>ring.classList.remove('grow');});}

/* ============ LENIS ============ */
const lenis=new Lenis({duration:1.15,easing:t=>Math.min(1,1.001-Math.pow(2,-10*t))});
lenis.on('scroll',ScrollTrigger.update);
gsap.ticker.add(time=>lenis.raf(time*1000));
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);
document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click',e=>{const id=a.getAttribute('href');if(id.length>1){e.preventDefault();lenis.scrollTo(id,{offset:-60});}}));
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);
const reduce=window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)').matches;

/* ambient rings drift */
gsap.to('body::before',{backgroundPosition:'0 60px',ease:'none',scrollTrigger:{trigger:'body',start:'top top',end:'bottom bottom',scrub:2}});

/* ============ PRELOADER ============ */
const pcount=document.getElementById('pcount'),pbar=document.getElementById('pbar');
gsap.timeline()
.to({v:0},{v:100,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.inOut',onUpdate:function(){const v=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v);pcount.textContent=v;pbar.style.width=v+'%';}})
.to('#preloader',{yPercent:-100,duration:1,ease:'expo.inOut'},'+=.25')
.add(()=>{if(!reduce){gsap.to('#heroTitle .word i',{y:0,duration:1,ease:'expo.out',stagger:.08});gsap.from('.hero-sub, .hero-meta > *, .hero-vis > *',{y:30,opacity:0,duration:.9,stagger:.08,ease:'power3.out',delay:.2});gsap.to('#fcBars i',{scaleY:1,duration:.7,stagger:.08,ease:'power3.out',delay:.8});}});

/* ============ HEADER ============ */
ScrollTrigger.create({start:80,onUpdate:s=>document.getElementById('header').classList.toggle('scrolled',s.progress>0)});

/* ============ HERO tooth scan line + next-free countdown ============ */
if(!reduce){gsap.to('#scanLine',{attr:{y1:120,y2:120},duration:1.6,ease:'sine.inOut',repeat:-1,yoyo:true});}
let nfSec=47*60+12;
function tickNf(){const m=Math.floor(nfSec/60),s=nfSec%60;document.getElementById('nfTime').textContent=String(m).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(s).padStart(2,'0');nfSec=nfSec>0?nfSec-1:59*60+59;}
tickNf();setInterval(tickNf,1000);

/* ============ MARQUEE ============ */
const mtrack=document.getElementById('mtrack');mtrack.innerHTML+=mtrack.innerHTML;
gsap.to(mtrack,{xPercent:-50,duration:28,ease:'none',repeat:-1});

/* ============ INTERACTIVE ARCH ============ */
let curTooth=null;
function buildArch(){
  const svg=document.getElementById('archSvg');svg.innerHTML='';
  // gum arcs
  const gumU=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','path');
  gumU.setAttribute('class','gum');gumU.setAttribute('d','M40 70 Q180 20 320 70 Q320 92 300 92 Q180 56 60 92 Q40 92 40 70 Z');svg.appendChild(gumU);
  const gumL=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','path');
  gumL.setAttribute('class','gum');gumL.setAttribute('d','M40 170 Q180 220 320 170 Q320 148 300 148 Q180 184 60 148 Q40 148 40 170 Z');svg.appendChild(gumL);
  TEETH.forEach(t=>{
    const y=t.row==='u'?78:162;
    const w=t.type==='mol'?26:t.type==='pre'?22:t.type==='can'?20:18;
    const h=t.type==='mol'?34:t.type==='inc'?30:30;
    const r=document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','rect');
    r.setAttribute('class','tooth'+(TREAT[t.type].warn?' warn':''));
    r.setAttribute('x',t.x-w/2);r.setAttribute('y',t.row==='u'?y-h/2:y-h/2);
    r.setAttribute('width',w);r.setAttribute('height',h);r.setAttribute('rx',t.type==='inc'?3:6);
    r.dataset.id=t.id;r.setAttribute('tabindex','0');r.setAttribute('role','button');
    r.setAttribute('aria-label',T(PIECE_NAME[t.type]));
    r.addEventListener('click',()=>selectTooth(t.id));
    r.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();selectTooth(t.id);}});
    svg.appendChild(r);
  });
}
function selectTooth(id){
  curTooth=id;
  document.querySelectorAll('.arch-svg .tooth').forEach(r=>r.classList.toggle('sel',r.dataset.id===id));
  renderArchInfo(id);
}
function renderArchInfo(id){
  const box=document.getElementById('archInfo');
  if(!id){box.innerHTML=`<span class="piece">${T('arch_hint')}</span><h3>${T('arch_pick')}</h3><p class="desc">${T('arch_pick_d')}</p>`;return;}
  const t=TEETH.find(x=>x.id===id);const tr=TREAT[t.type];const doc=DOCS.find(d=>d.id===tr.doc);
  box.innerHTML=`
    <span class="piece">${T(PIECE_NAME[t.type])} · ${id.toUpperCase()}</span>
    <h3>${T(tr.tkey)}</h3>
    <p class="desc">${T(tr.tkey+'_d')}</p>
    <div class="specs">
      <div class="sp"><div class="k">${T('ar_price')}</div><div class="v">${money(tr.price)}</div></div>
      <div class="sp"><div class="k">${T('ar_dur')}</div><div class="v">${tr.dur} <small>${T('ar_min')}</small></div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="doc"><div class="av"><img data-src="${doc.img}" alt="${doc.name}"></div><div><div class="nm">${doc.name}</div><div class="rl">${T('ar_doc')} · ${doc.spec[curLang]}</div></div></div>
    <a href="#agenda" class="btn solid"><span>${T('ar_book')}</span><span class="ar">→</span><span class="fill"></span></a>`;
  lazy(box.querySelector('img'));
  if(!reduce)gsap.from(box.children,{y:14,opacity:0,duration:.45,stagger:.05,ease:'power3.out'});
  bindGrow();
}

/* ============ DIAGNOSTIC STACK (scrub) ============ */
function renderStackLegend(){
  const lg=document.getElementById('stackLegend');lg.innerHTML='';
  STACK.forEach((s,i)=>{const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='li'+(i===0?' on':'');el.dataset.i=i;el.innerHTML=`<span class="n">0${i+1}</span><span>${T(s.k)}</span>`;lg.appendChild(el);});
}
function setupStack(){
  const layers=[...document.querySelectorAll('.stack-layer')];
  layers.forEach(l=>lazy(l.querySelector('img')));
  if(reduce){layers.forEach((l,i)=>{l.style.opacity=i===0?1:0;l.style.transform='translateY(0)';});document.getElementById('stackBar').style.width='0%';return;}
  // initial stack
  gsap.set(layers,(i)=>({zIndex:layers.length-i,opacity:i===0?1:0,y:i*8}));
  ScrollTrigger.create({
    trigger:'.stack-stage',start:'top 75%',end:'bottom 30%',scrub:.6,
    onUpdate:s=>{
      const p=s.progress;document.getElementById('stackBar').style.width=(p*100)+'%';
      const idx=Math.min(layers.length-1,Math.floor(p*layers.length+0.0001));
      layers.forEach((l,i)=>{
        const local=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(p*layers.length)-i));
        gsap.set(l,{opacity:i<=idx?1:0,y:(idx-i)*-14,zIndex:layers.length-Math.abs(idx-i)});
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.stack-legend .li').forEach((li,i)=>li.classList.toggle('on',i<=idx));
    }
  });
}

/* ============ ORTHO COMPARATOR ============ */
function renderOrtho(){
  const g=document.getElementById('orthoGrid');g.innerHTML='';
  ORTHO.forEach(o=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='ortho';
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div class="ic">${o.ic}</div>
      <h3>${T('o_'+o.id)}</h3>
      <div class="pr">${money(o.price)} <small>${T('o_from')} ${money(Math.round(o.price/24))}${T('o_month')}</small></div>
      <div class="metric"><div class="ml"><span>${T('m_est')}</span><span>${o.est}</span></div><div class="bar"><i data-w="${o.est}"></i></div></div>
      <div class="metric"><div class="ml"><span>${T('m_comf')}</span><span>${o.comf}</span></div><div class="bar"><i data-w="${o.comf}"></i></div></div>
      <div class="metric"><div class="ml"><span>${T('m_vis')}</span><span>${o.vis}</span></div><div class="bar"><i data-w="${o.vis}"></i></div></div>
      <div class="metric"><div class="ml"><span>${T('o_dur')}</span><span>${o.dur} ${T('o_months')}</span></div><div class="bar"><i data-w="${Math.round(100-o.dur*3)}"></i></div></div>
      <div class="pick"><span>${T('o_pick')}</span><span class="ar">→</span></div>`;
    el.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>{if(reduce){el.querySelectorAll('.bar i').forEach(b=>b.style.width=b.dataset.w+'%');return;}el.querySelectorAll('.bar i').forEach(b=>gsap.to(b,{width:b.dataset.w+'%',duration:.9,ease:'power2.out'}));});
    g.appendChild(el);
  });
  // also animate on scroll into view
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:g,start:'top 78%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{g.querySelectorAll('.ortho').forEach((el,k)=>{el.querySelectorAll('.bar i').forEach(b=>{if(reduce)b.style.width=b.dataset.w+'%';else gsap.to(b,{width:b.dataset.w+'%',duration:.9,delay:k*.08,ease:'power2.out'});});});}});
  bindGrow();
}

/* ============ CALCULATOR ============ */
function renderCalc(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('calcOpts');wrap.innerHTML='';
  CALC_OPTS.forEach(o=>{
    const on=o.num?calcState[o.id]>0:calcState[o.id];
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='opt'+(on?' on':'')+(o.num?' hasnum':'');
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="chk">✓</span><div class="ot"><b>${T(o.k)}</b><span>${T(o.k+'_d')}</span></div>
      ${o.num?`<div class="stepper"><button data-d="-1">−</button><span class="nv">${calcState[o.id]}</span><button data-d="1">+</button></div>`:`<span class="op">${money(o.price)}</span>`}`;
    if(o.num){
      el.querySelector('.chk').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(calcState[o.id]===0)calcState[o.id]=1;else calcState[o.id]=0;renderCalc();calc();});
      el.querySelectorAll('.stepper button').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{calcState[o.id]=Math.max(0,Math.min(o.max,calcState[o.id]+ +b.dataset.d));renderCalc();calc();}));
    }else{
      el.addEventListener('click',()=>{calcState[o.id]=!calcState[o.id];renderCalc();calc();});
    }
    wrap.appendChild(el);
  });
  bindGrow();
}
function calc(){
  let total=0;const lines=[];
  CALC_OPTS.forEach(o=>{
    const qty=o.num?calcState[o.id]:(calcState[o.id]?1:0);
    if(qty>0){const sub=o.price*qty;total+=sub;lines.push({k:o.k,qty,sub});}
  });
  const fin=Math.round(total/24);
  if(reduce){document.getElementById('calcTotal').textContent=money(total);document.getElementById('calcFin').textContent=money(fin);}
  else{gsap.to({v:0},{v:total,duration:.8,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto',onUpdate:function(){document.getElementById('calcTotal').textContent=money(Math.round(this.targets()[0].v));}});
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:fin,duration:.8,ease:'power2.out',overwrite:'auto',onUpdate:function(){document.getElementById('calcFin').textContent=money(Math.round(this.targets()[0].v));}});}
  const br=document.getElementById('calcBreak');br.innerHTML=lines.length?lines.map(l=>`<div class="br"><span>${T(l.k)}${l.qty>1?' ×'+l.qty:''}</span><b>${money(l.sub)}</b></div>`).join(''):`<div class="br"><span>—</span><b>0 €</b></div>`;
}

/* ============ DOCTOR AGENDA ============ */
function renderAgenda(){
  const g=document.getElementById('agendaGrid');g.innerHTML='';
  const today=new Date();const dayName=new Intl.DateTimeFormat(curLang==='en'?'en-GB':curLang==='es'?'es-ES':'ca-ES',{weekday:'long'}).format(today);
  DOCS.forEach(d=>{
    const slots=slotsFor(d.id);const anyFree=slots.some(s=>!s.gone);
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='doc-card';
    el.innerHTML=`
      <div class="ph"><span class="spec">${d.spec[curLang]}</span><img data-src="${d.img}" alt="${d.name}"></div>
      <div class="body">
        <h3>${d.name}</h3>
        <div class="lang-sp">${d.langs[curLang]}</div>
        <div class="day-lbl">${T('ag_today')} ${dayName}</div>
        <div class="slots">${anyFree?slots.map(s=>`<span class="slot${s.gone?' gone':''}" data-t="${s.t}">${s.t}</span>`).join(''):`<span style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mut-d)">${T('ag_full')}</span>`}</div>
        <div class="book"><button class="btn solid" style="width:100%;justify-content:center"><span>${T('ag_book')} · <em class="bk-t" style="font-style:normal">--:--</em></span><span class="fill"></span></button></div>
      </div>`;
    lazy(el.querySelector('img'));
    el.querySelectorAll('.slot:not(.gone)').forEach(s=>s.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      el.querySelectorAll('.slot').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('sel'));s.classList.add('sel');
      const bk=el.querySelector('.book');bk.classList.add('show');bk.querySelector('.bk-t').textContent=s.dataset.t;
    }));
    el.querySelector('.book .btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{lenis.scrollTo('#contact',{offset:-60});});
    g.appendChild(el);
  });
  if(!reduce)gsap.from(g.children,{y:34,opacity:0,duration:.6,stagger:.1,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:g,start:'top 80%'}});
  bindGrow();
}

/* ============ TECH ============ */
function renderTech(){
  const g=document.getElementById('techList');g.innerHTML='';
  TECH.forEach((t,i)=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='tech-item';
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="tn">0${i+1}</span><div><h4>${T(t.k)}</h4><p>${T(t.k+'_d')}</p></div><span class="tg">${T(t.tg)}</span>`;
    g.appendChild(el);
  });
  if(!reduce)gsap.utils.toArray('.tech-item').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{x:-24,opacity:0,duration:.7,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 85%'}}));
  bindGrow();
}

/* ============ TESTI ============ */
function renderTesti(){
  const g=document.getElementById('testiGrid');g.innerHTML='';
  TESTI.forEach(t=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='tcard';
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="stars">★★★★★</span><div class="treat"><span class="pulse-dot"></span>${T(t.treat)}</div><p class="q">${T(t.tk)}</p><div class="who"><div class="av"><img data-src="${t.img}" alt=""></div><div><div class="nm">${t.nm}</div><div class="rl">${T(t.rk)}</div></div></div>`;
    lazy(el.querySelector('img'));g.appendChild(el);
  });
  if(!reduce)gsap.from(g.children,{y:34,opacity:0,duration:.7,stagger:.12,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:g,start:'top 82%'}});
  bindGrow();
}

/* ============ STATS ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.strip .n').forEach(n=>{
  const target=+n.dataset.count;const suf=n.querySelector('em');const sHtml=suf?suf.outerHTML:'';
  ScrollTrigger.create({trigger:n,start:'top 88%',once:true,onEnter:()=>{
    if(reduce){n.innerHTML=target+sHtml;return;}
    gsap.to({v:0},{v:target,duration:1.8,ease:'power2.out',onUpdate:function(){n.innerHTML=Math.round(this.targets()[0].v)+sHtml;}});
  }});
});

/* ============ GENERIC REVEAL ============ */
gsap.utils.toArray('.sec-head,.arch-grid > *,.calc-grid > *,.cta-box').forEach(el=>gsap.from(el,{y:42,opacity:0,duration:.85,ease:'power3.out',scrollTrigger:{trigger:el,start:'top 88%'}}));

/* ============ FORM ============ */
const resOk=document.getElementById('resOk');
document.getElementById('ctForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();if(!e.target.checkValidity()){e.target.reportValidity();return;}
  gsap.set(resOk,{display:'grid'});
  if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{opacity:1,scale:1});}
  else{gsap.fromTo(resOk,{opacity:0},{opacity:1,duration:.3});gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.card'),{y:30,scale:.94,opacity:0},{y:0,scale:1,opacity:1,duration:.5,ease:'back.out(1.6)'});gsap.fromTo(resOk.querySelector('.check'),{scale:0,rotation:-40},{scale:1,rotation:0,duration:.6,ease:'back.out(2)',delay:.15});}
  e.target.reset();
});
document.getElementById('okClose').onclick=()=>{if(reduce){gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none'});return;}gsap.to(resOk,{opacity:0,duration:.3,onComplete:()=>gsap.set(resOk,{display:'none',opacity:1})});};

/* ============ MAGNETIC ============ */
document.querySelectorAll('.btn').forEach(btn=>{
  btn.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{if(reduce)return;const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect();gsap.to(btn,{x:(e.clientX-r.left-r.width/2)*.22,y:(e.clientY-r.top-r.height/2)*.3,duration:.4,ease:'power2.out'});});
  btn.addEventListener('mouseleave',()=>{if(!reduce)gsap.to(btn,{x:0,y:0,duration:.6,ease:'elastic.out(1,.4)'});});
});
document.querySelector('.burger').addEventListener('click',function(){this.classList.toggle('open');});

/* ============ INIT ============ */
buildArch();renderArchInfo(null);renderStackLegend();setupStack();renderOrtho();renderCalc();calc();renderAgenda();renderTech();renderTesti();bindGrow();
setLang('ca');
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

Abro con el gesto más propio de una clínica dental moderna y lo convierto en el motor visual de la página: a la izquierda el titular con una palabra en itálica de *Instrument Serif* sobre verde petróleo, y a la derecha un **diente en SVG con una línea de escáner que lo barre en bucle**, rodeado de anillos concéntricos tipo ecógrafo y dos tarjetas flotantes —una con un mini-gráfico de "escáner en curso" cuyas barras crecen al cargar, otra con el plan de tratamiento—. El fondo son ondas de escáner radiales sobre una cuadrícula milimetrada clínica con grano, que se desplazan con el scroll: la página se siente como un aparato de diagnóstico encendido antes de tocar nada. En la nav, un contador de **"próxima cita libre"** cuenta regresivamente en vivo.

A partir de ahí, cuatro módulos nuevos y específicos del vertical que no había hecho en la serie. El primero es el que da nombre a la sección: una **arcada dentaria interactiva en SVG** con incisivos, caninos, premolares y molares clicables (y operables por teclado, con `aria-label` por pieza); al tocar un diente, el panel lateral revela el tratamiento de referencia para esa zona, su precio y duración orientativos, y el doctor que lo lleva —los molares y premolares aparecen en coral como "piezas con aviso" para dar lectura clínica real. El segundo es un **stack de capas de diagnóstico con scrub**: al hacer scroll atraviesas radiografía → escáner intraoral → planificación 3D → resultado previsto, con una leyenda lateral que se va encendiendo y una barra de progreso del "corte". El tercero, un **comparador de ortodoncia** (metálico / cerámico / invisible / lingual) donde las barras de estética, comodidad, discreción y duración se animan al pasar el cursor y al entrar en viewport. El cuarto, una **calculadora de tratamiento** que suma servicios con steppers numéricos para implantes y carillas y te devuelve total + cuota de financiación animados. Cierro el bloque funcional con una **agenda por doctor** que muestra los huecos de hoy en tiempo real (deterministas por profesional) y reserva al clicar una hora.

Identidad: verde petróleo profundo alternando con secciones en blanco frío (no beige), acento **lima-menta** y un **coral** reservado solo a los avisos clínicos; *Schibsted Grotesk* en pesos pesados para titulares, *Instrument Serif* itálica para los acentos y *Spline Sans Mono* para todos los datos (tiempos, precios, etiquetas), radios rectos y sin glassmorphism. Trilingüe CA/ES/EN completo: nombres de piezas dentales, tratamientos, especialidades, pasos del diagnóstico, métricas del comparador, opciones de la calculadora, huecos de agenda y tecnología se regeneran al cambiar de idioma, con moneda y separadores locales. Imágenes de Unsplash con *fallback* y `prefers-reduced-motion` respetado (la línea de escáner, el countdown y el scrub se quedan estáticos).

Guárdalo como `index.html` y ábrelo. Para adaptarlo edita los arrays `TEETH`/`TREAT` (qué tratamiento, precio y doctor por tipo de pieza), `DOCS` (fotos, especialidad e idiomas), `ORTHO` (métricas del comparador), `CALC_OPTS` (servicios y precios) y `STACK`/`TECH`; en producción conecta el formulario y la agenda a tu sistema de citas real.

Con **`. 9`** sigo con el siguiente tipo de cliente (**#9 — E-commerce / tiendas online**), o dime “otra modalidad 8” si prefieres una segunda variante de clínica/salud.
